inutes.
 // The current game qualifying remains one continuous playable session, using the
 // full 60-minute scheduled qualifying window. Standalone Free Practice stays unlimited.
 if(s==='fp1'||s==='fp2'||s==='fp3'||s==='qualifying')return 60*60;
 return Infinity;
}
function sessionRemainingSeconds(){return Number.isFinite(sessionLimit)?Math.max(0,sessionLimit-raceT):Infinity;}
function sessionClockLabel(){const r=sessionRemainingSeconds();return Number.isFinite(r)?fmt(r):'SIN LÍMITE';}
function stripCountryEmoji(s){return String(s||'').replace(/^[^A-Za-zÁÉÍÓÚÜÑÀÈÌÒÙÂÊÎÔÛÄËÏÖÜÇ]+\s*/,'')}

let teamLogoRenderSeq=0;
function teamLogoMarkup(key,cls='teamLogoSvg'){
 const t=teamInfo(key),b=teamBranding(key),c=t.color,
       customAccent=(key===selectedTeamKey()&&$('carAccent')&&$('carAccent').value)?$('carAccent').value:null,
       a=customAccent||t.accent||'#fff',g1=shadeColor(c,24),g2=shadeColor(c,-28),
       gid='core-'+String(key||'TEAM').replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]/g,'')+'-'+(++teamLogoRenderSeq);
 let inner='';
 if(b.logo==='star')inner='<path d="M32 6l5.2 14.2 15-1.5-11.6 9.7 4.7 15.5L32 35.5l-13.3 8.4 4.7-15.5-11.6-9.7 15 1.5Z" fill="url(#core)"/><circle cx="32" cy="32" r="5" fill="'+a+'" opacity=".88"/>';
 else if(b.logo==='horns')inner='<path d="M8 46L20 15l12 9 12-9 12 31-16-6-8 11-8-11Z" fill="url(#core)"/><path d="M21 22l11 9 11-9" fill="none" stroke="'+a+'" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>';
 else if(b.logo==='shield')inner='<path d="M32 7l18 7v18c0 13-7.5 21-18 26-10.5-5-18-13-18-26V14Z" fill="url(#core)" stroke="'+a+'" stroke-opacity=".45" stroke-width="1.5"/><path d="M29 16h6v31h-6z" fill="'+a+'" opacity=".9"/>';
 else if(b.logo==='ring')inner='<circle cx="32" cy="32" r="19" fill="none" stroke="url(#core)" stroke-width="9"/><circle cx="32" cy="32" r="7.5" fill="none" stroke="'+a+'" stroke-width="2" opacity=".7"/>';
 else if(b.logo==='leaf')inner='<path d="M32 6c19 9 21 30 1 50C12 46 11 24 32 6Z" fill="url(#core)"/><path d="M32 16c-1 15-1 26 0 35" stroke="'+a+'" stroke-width="2.6" stroke-linecap="round"/>';
 else if(b.logo==='wave')inner='<path d="M5 39C15 14 25 49 35 24c6-14 14-10 24-4" fill="none" stroke="url(#core)" stroke-width="8" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M9 49c9-8 18-1 27-8" fill="none" stroke="'+a+'" stroke-width="2.3" stroke-linecap="round" opacity=".78"/>';
 else if(b.logo==='crest')inner='<path d="M11 10h42v24L32 57 11 34Z" fill="url(#core)" stroke="'+a+'" stroke-opacity=".4" stroke-width="1.4"/><path d="M32 16l9 13-9 11-9-11Z" fill="'+a+'"/>';
 else if(b.logo==='aeroA')inner='<path d="M32 5L56 55H43l-5-12H26l-5 12H8Z" fill="url(#core)"/><path d="M27.5 34h9L32 21Z" fill="#081019"/><path d="M22 44h20" stroke="'+a+'" stroke-width="2.2" opacity=".75"/>';
 else if(b.logo==='doubleBar')inner='<path d="M7 14h50v14H7zm8 25h38v10H15z" fill="url(#core)"/><path d="M12 20h40M19 44h30" stroke="'+a+'" stroke-width="2" opacity=".58"/>';
 else if(b.logo==='hash')inner='<path d="M11 7h9v50h-9zm16 0h9v50h-9zm16 0h9v50h-9z" fill="url(#core)"/><path d="M11 25h41M11 41h41" stroke="'+a+'" stroke-width="2" opacity=".55"/>';
 else if(b.logo==='swoosh')inner='<path d="M4 43C19 8 29 46 60 15c-9 27-31 39-56 28Z" fill="url(#core)"/><path d="M13 43c13-10 24-9 39-22" fill="none" stroke="'+a+'" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" opacity=".62"/>';
 else inner='<path d="M32 6L58 56H6Z" fill="url(#core)" stroke="'+a+'" stroke-opacity=".35"/>';
 inner=inner.replace(/url\(#core\)/g,'url(#'+gid+')');
 return `<svg class="${cls}" viewBox="0 0 64 64" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true"><defs><linearGradient id="${gid}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="${g1}"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="${g2}"/></linearGradient></defs>${inner}</svg>`;
}
function renderTeamChooser(){
 const key=selectedTeamKey(),t=teamInfo(key),logo=$('selectedTeamLogo'),sel=$('teamSelect');
 if(logo)logo.innerHTML=teamLogoMarkup(key);
 const menuTeam=sel&&sel.selectedOptions&&sel.selectedOptions[0]?sel.selectedOptions[0].textContent:key;for(const id of ["menuTeamBadge","menuTeamSummary"]){const n=$(id);if(n)n.textContent=menuTeam;}
 const locked=currentMode==='season'&&!!save.seasonTeam;
 if(sel){
   if(locked&&save.seasonTeam&&sel.value!==save.seasonTeam)sel.value=save.seasonTeam;
   sel.disabled=locked;
   sel.title=locked?'La escudería queda fijada para esta temporada':'';
 }
}
function estimateLapBase(spec){const v=[78,92,89,89,74,72,78,65,87,103,77,72,80,82,103,94,96,94,78,73,94,83,84];return v[(spec.r||1)-1]||84;}
function weekendTeamKey(){return weekendContext&&weekendContext.mode==='season'&&save.seasonTeam?save.seasonTeam:selectedTeamKey()}
function getWeekendRoster(teamKey){const seat=weekendContext&&weekendContext.mode==='season'?save.seasonSeatUid:null;return buildRaceRoster(teamKey,seat)}
function simulateTiming(session,playerTime=null){
 const spec=weekendContext.spec,teamKey=weekendTeamKey(),roster=getWeekendRoster(teamKey),base=estimateLapBase(spec),all=[{uid:'PLAYER',name:'TÚ',team:teamKey},...roster];
 return all.map((d,i)=>{const perf=teamPerformance(d.team),trait=stableTrait(d.uid,'Q'.charCodeAt(0)),cons=stableTrait(d.uid,7),teamGain=((perf.top+perf.aero+perf.brake+perf.traction)/4-1)*18;let tm=base-teamGain-(trait-.5)*1.45+(1-cons)*.55+Math.random()*1.15;if(d.uid==='PLAYER'){tm=Number.isFinite(playerTime)?playerTime:tm+.18;}if(session.startsWith('fp'))tm+=.5+Math.random()*.9;return{uid:d.uid,name:d.name,team:d.team,time:tm}}).sort((a,b)=>a.time-b.time);
}
function simulateRaceOrder(gridOrder=null){
 const teamKey=weekendTeamKey(),roster=getWeekendRoster(teamKey),all=[{uid:'PLAYER',name:'TÚ',team:teamKey},...roster],grid=gridOrder||simulateTiming('qualifying').map(x=>x.uid);
 return all.map(d=>{const perf=teamPerformance(d.team),g=grid.indexOf(d.uid),skill=stableTrait(d.uid,19),cons=stableTrait(d.uid,31),score=(perf.top+perf.aero+perf.traction+perf.brake)*17+skill*7+cons*3-Math.max(0,g)*.18+Math.random()*6;return{...d,score}}).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);
}
function awardSeasonOrder(order){const pts=[25,18,15,12,10,8,6,4,2,1];order.forEach((d,i)=>{save.points[d.uid]=(save.points[d.uid]||0)+(pts[i]||0)});if(order[0]&&order[0].uid==='PLAYER')save.wins++;save.lastSeasonResult=order.map(d=>d.uid);save.round=Math.min(23,save.round+1);save.seasonWeekend=null;persist();}
function timingMarkup(results,title){if(!results||!results.length)return'';const lead=results[0].time;return `<div class="timingBoard"><div class="timingTitle">${title}</div>${results.slice(0,22).map((r,i)=>`<div class="timingRow ${r.uid==='PLAYER'?'you':''} ${i===0?'best':''}"><span class="pos">${i+1}</span><span>${r.name}</span><span class="teamCell">${teamLogoMarkup(r.team,'teamMiniLogo')}${teamInfo(r.team).name}</span><span class="time">${fmt(r.time)}</span><span class="gap">${i?('+'+(r.time-lead).toFixed(3)):'MEJOR'}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`}

function fmtDeltaSigned(v){if(!Number.isFinite(v))return '—';const s=v>0?'+':'';return s+v.toFixed(3)}
function finishTitleCountry(spec){return ('GRAN PREMIO DE '+stripCountryEmoji(spec.country||spec.name||'')).toUpperCase()}
function finishSessionCaption(session){return session==='freepractice'?'ENTRENAMIENTOS LIBRES · RESUMEN':session==='fp1'?'PRACTICE 1 · CLASSIFICATION':session==='fp2'?'PRACTICE 2 · CLASSIFICATION':session==='fp3'?'PRACTICE 3 · CLASSIFICATION':session==='qualifying'?'QUALIFYING · CLASSIFICATION':session==='race'?'RACE · RESULT':'RESULTADO'}
function finishChip(label,value){return `<div class="finishChip"><span>${label}</span><b>${value}</b></div>`}
function teamLogoFinishMarkup(team){return `<span class="finishTeamLogoWrap">${teamLogoMarkup(team,'finishTeamLogo')}</span>`}
function buildSessionResultsTable(results,mode='time'){if(!results||!results.length)return `<div class="finishMuted">Sin datos.</div>`;const lead=results[0].time;return `<div class="sectorTableScroll"><table class="finishTable"><thead><tr><th style="width:44px">Pos</th><th>Driver</th><th style="width:90px">Team</th><th style="text-align:right">${mode==='race'?'Gap to winner':'Gap to fastest'}</th><th>S1*</th><th>S2*</th><th>S3*</th><th style="width:74px;text-align:right">Laps</th></tr></thead><tbody>${results.map((r,i)=>{const isBest=sessionFastestLap&&sessionFastestLap.id===r._raceId;return `<tr class="${r.uid==='PLAYER'?'you':''} ${i===0?'best':''}"><td class="pos">${i+1}</td><td class="driver">${String(r.name||'').toUpperCase()}${isBest?'<span class="finishBestFlag">◴</span>':''}<small>${r.extra||''}</small></td><td class="team center">${teamLogoFinishMarkup(r.team)}</td><td class="metric">${i?fmtDeltaSigned(r.time-lead):fmt(r.time)}</td>${apexResultSectorCells(r)}<td class="metric2">${Number.isFinite(r.laps)?r.laps:'—'}</td></tr>`}).join('')}</tbody></table></div><div class="finishMuted">* Sectores de la mejor vuelta medida de cada piloto. —: sin lectura.</div>`}
function buildRaceResultsTable(results){if(!results||!results.length)return `<div class="finishMuted">Sin datos.</div>`;return `<div class="sectorTableScroll"><table class="finishTable"><thead><tr><th style="width:44px">Pos</th><th>Driver</th><th style="width:90px">Team</th><th style="text-align:right">Gap to winner</th><th>S1*</th><th>S2*</th><th>S3*</th><th style="width:74px;text-align:right">Laps</th></tr></thead><tbody>${results.map((r,i)=>{const isBest=sessionFastestLap&&sessionFastestLap.id===r._raceId;return `<tr class="${r.uid==='PLAYER'?'you':''} ${i===0?'best':''}"><td class="pos">${i+1}</td><td class="driver">${String(r.name||'').toUpperCase()}${isBest?'<span class="finishBestFlag">◴</span>':''}<small>${r.extra||''}</small></td><td class="team center">${teamLogoFinishMarkup(r.team)}</td><td class="metric">${r.gap}</td>${apexResultSectorCells(r)}<td class="metric2">${r.laps}</td></tr>`}).join('')}</tbody></table></div><div class="finishMuted">* Sectores de la mejor vuelta medida de cada piloto. —: sin lectura.</div>`}
function buildLapLogBoard(title,lapRows){
 const rows=lapRows||[],bestLap=rows.reduce((m,r)=>Number.isFinite(r.time)?Math.min(m,r.time):m,Infinity),mins=[0,1,2].map(i=>rows.reduce((m,r)=>Number.isFinite((r.sectors||[])[i])?Math.min(m,r.sectors[i]):m,Infinity));
 return `<div class="finishPageTitle">${title}</div><div class="sectorTableScroll"><table class="finishTable sectorLapTable"><thead><tr><th>Vuelta</th><th>S1</th><th>S2</th><th>S3</th><th>Tiempo</th><th>Dif.</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.length?rows.map(r=>`<tr class="you"><td>V${r.lap}${r.auto?' · AUTO':''}${r.firstLap?' · SALIDA':''}</td>${[0,1,2].map(i=>{const t=(r.sectors||[])[i];return `<td class="${Number.isFinite(t)&&t<=mins[i]+.0005?'sectorPersonalBest':''}">${apexSectorText(t)}</td>`}).join('')}<td>${Number.isFinite(r.time)?fmt(r.time):Number.isFinite(r.elapsed)?fmt(r.elapsed):'—'}</td><td>${Number.isFinite(r.time)&&Number.isFinite(bestLap)?fmtDeltaSigned(r.time-bestLap):'—'}</td></tr>`).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="6">Todavía no hay vueltas completas registradas.</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div>`;
}
function seasonConstructorStandings(pointsMap=save.points){const byTeam={};championshipRoster().forEach(r=>{const t=r.team||selectedTeamKey();byTeam[t]=(byTeam[t]||0)+(pointsMap[r.uid]||0)});return Object.keys(byTeam).map(team=>({team,name:teamInfo(team).name,points:byTeam[team]})).sort((a,b)=>b.points-a.points)}
function seasonDriverStandings(){return championshipRoster().map(r=>({uid:r.uid,name:r.name,team:r.team,p:save.points[r.uid]||0})).sort((a,b)=>b.p-a.p)}
function buildStandingsTable(kind='drivers'){if(kind==='drivers'){const arr=seasonDriverStandings(),lead=arr[0]?arr[0].p:0;return `<table class="finishTable"><thead><tr><th style="width:44px">Pos</th><th>Championship de pilotos</th><th style="width:90px">Team</th><th style="text-align:right">Pts</th><th style="width:74px;text-align:right">Dif.</th></tr></thead><tbody>${arr.map((r,i)=>`<tr class="${r.uid==='PLAYER'?'you':''} ${i===0?'best':''}"><td class="pos">${i+1}</td><td class="driver">${String(r.name).toUpperCase()}</td><td class="team center">${teamLogoFinishMarkup(r.team)}</td><td class="metric">${r.p}</td><td class="metric2">${i?lead-r.p:'—'}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`}
 const arr=seasonConstructorStandings(),lead=arr[0]?arr[0].points:0;return `<table class="finishTable"><thead><tr><th style="width:44px">Pos</th><th>Championship de constructores</th><th style="width:90px">Logo</th><th style="text-align:right">Pts</th><th style="width:74px;text-align:right">Dif.</th></tr></thead><tbody>${arr.map((r,i)=>`<tr class="${(save.seasonTeam||selectedTeamKey())===r.team?'you':''} ${i===0?'best':''}"><td class="pos">${i+1}</td><td class="driver">${String(r.name).toUpperCase()}</td><td class="team center">${teamLogoFinishMarkup(r.team)}</td><td class="metric">${r.points}</td><td class="metric2">${i?lead-r.points:'—'}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`}
function showFinishFlow(pages,onDone){finishFlow={pages:pages||[],index:0,onDone:onDone||null};$('menu').classList.add('hidden');$('weekendPanel').classList.add('hidden');$('hud').classList.add('hidden');$('touch').classList.add('hidden');$('pausePanel').classList.add('hidden');$('sessionBadge').classList.add('hidden');$('finish').classList.remove('hidden');mode='finish';renderFinishFlow()}
function renderFinishFlow(){if(!finishFlow||!finishFlow.pages||!finishFlow.pages.length)return;const p=finishFlow.pages[finishFlow.index]||finishFlow.pages[0];$('finishGpTitle').textContent=p.gpTitle||'GRAND PRIX';$('finishSessionTitle').textContent=p.sessionTitle||'RESULTADO';$('finishSummaryBar').innerHTML=(p.summaryChips||[]).join('');$('finishBoardWrap').innerHTML=p.body||'';$('finishPager').innerHTML=finishFlow.pages.map((_,i)=>`<span class="finishDot ${i===finishFlow.index?'on':''}"></span>`).join('');const prev=$('finishPrev'),next=$('finishNext');if(prev)prev.style.display=finishFlow.pages.length>1&&finishFlow.index>0?'inline-flex':'none';if(next)next.textContent=finishFlow.index>=finishFlow.pages.length-1?'CONTINUAR':'SIGUIENTE';}
function advanceFinishFlow(){if(!finishFlow)return; if(finishFlow.index<finishFlow.pages.length-1){finishFlow.index++;renderFinishFlow();return;} const done=finishFlow.onDone; finishFlow=null; if(done)done(); else goMenu();}
function retreatFinishFlow(){if(!finishFlow)return; if(finishFlow.index>0){finishFlow.index--;renderFinishFlow();}}
function prepareSimulatedSessionResults(session,res){return (res||[]).map(r=>{let laps=r.laps; if(!Number.isFinite(laps)){ if(r.uid==='PLAYER')laps=Math.max(1,sessionLapLog.length); else { const base=session==='qualifying'?4:14; const spread=session==='qualifying'?5:12; laps=base+Math.floor(stableTrait(r.uid,91)*spread);} } return {...r,laps};})}
function buildPracticeSoloPages(spec){const rows=sessionLapLog.slice();const bestLap=rows.reduce((m,r)=>Number.isFinite(r.time)&&r.time<m?r.time:m,Infinity);const summary=[finishChip('Circuito',(spec.name||'').toUpperCase()),finishChip('Vueltas',String(rows.length)),finishChip('Mejor',Number.isFinite(bestLap)?fmt(bestLap):'—'),finishChip('Sesión',fmt(Math.max(0,raceT||0)))];const side=`<div class="finishSideCard"><div class="finishPageTitle">Resumen</div><div class="finishSummaryLine"><span>Piloto</span><b>TÚ</b></div><div class="finishSummaryLine"><span>Tiempo de sesión</span><b>${fmt(Math.max(0,raceT||0))}</b></div><div class="finishSummaryLine"><span>Vueltas completas</span><b>${rows.length}</b></div><div class="finishSummaryLine"><span>Mejor vuelta</span><b>${Number.isFinite(bestLap)?fmt(bestLap):'—'}</b></div></div>`;const page0={gpTitle:finishTitleCountry(spec),sessionTitle:finishSessionCaption('freepractice'),summaryChips:summary,body:`<div class="finishTwoCol"><div>${buildLapLogBoard('Tiempos de todas las vueltas',rows)}</div>${side}</div>`};return [page0]}
function buildTimedSessionPages(spec,session,res){const results=prepareSimulatedSessionResults(session,res);const you=results.findIndex(r=>r.uid==='PLAYER');const ytxt=you>=0?('P'+(you+1)):'—';const leader=results[0];const chips=[finishChip('Circuito',(spec.name||'').toUpperCase()),finishChip('Sesión',sessionLabel(session)),finishChip('Posición',ytxt),finishChip('Mejor',leader?fmt(leader.time):'—')];const page0={gpTitle:finishTitleCountry(spec),sessionTitle:finishSessionCaption(session),summaryChips:chips,body:buildSessionResultsTable(results,'time')};const pages=[page0];if(sessionLapLog.length){pages.push({gpTitle:finishTitleCountry(spec),sessionTitle:sessionLabel(session)+' · TUS VUELTAS',summaryChips:[finishChip('Vueltas',String(sessionLapLog.length)),finishChip('Tu mejor',best<1e8?fmt(best):'—')],body:buildLapLogBoard('Tus vueltas',sessionLapLog)})}return pages}
function buildRaceFinishPages(spec,results,position){const fastestName=sessionFastestLap&&Number.isFinite(sessionFastestLap.time)?(sessionFastestLap.id===0?'TÚ':(raceName(sessionFastestLap.id)||'Rival')):'—';const fastestTxt=Number.isFinite(sessionFastestLap.time)?fmt(sessionFastestLap.time):'—';const chips=[finishChip('Posición',position?('P'+position):'—'),finishChip('Tiempo',fmt(Math.max(0,raceT||0))),finishChip('Tu mejor',best<1e8?fmt(best):'—'),finishChip('V. rápida',fastestName+' · '+fastestTxt)];if(seasonAwardContext&&seasonAwardContext.playerGpPoints!=null)chips.push(finishChip('Puntos',String(seasonAwardContext.playerGpPoints)));const pages=[{gpTitle:finishTitleCountry(spec),sessionTitle:finishSessionCaption('race'),summaryChips:chips,body:buildRaceResultsTable(results)}];if(sessionLapLog.length){pages.push({gpTitle:finishTitleCountry(spec),sessionTitle:'RACE · TUS VUELTAS',summaryChips:[finishChip('Vueltas',String(sessionLapLog.length)),finishChip('Tu mejor',best<1e8?fmt(best):'—')],body:buildLapLogBoard('Tus vueltas',sessionLapLog)})}if(currentMode==='season'){pages.push({gpTitle:'TEMPORADA 2026',sessionTitle:'CHAMPIONSHIP DE PILOTOS',summaryChips:[finishChip('Ronda',String(Math.max(1,save.round))),finishChip('Victorias',String(save.wins||0)),finishChip('Tus puntos',String(save.points.PLAYER||0))],body:buildStandingsTable('drivers')});pages.push({gpTitle:'TEMPORADA 2026',sessionTitle:'CHAMPIONSHIP DE CONSTRUCTORES',summaryChips:[finishChip('Equipo',(teamInfo(save.seasonTeam||selectedTeamKey()).name||'').toUpperCase()),finishChip('Puntos equipo',String((seasonConstructorStandings().find(x=>x.team===(save.seasonTeam||selectedTeamKey()))||{}).points||0)),finishChip('Puntos GP',String(seasonAwardContext&&seasonAwardContext.teamGpPoints!=null?seasonAwardContext.teamGpPoints:0))],body:buildStandingsTable('constructors')})}
 return pages}

function saveWeekendProgress(){if(!weekendContext||weekendContext.mode!=='season')return;save.seasonWeekend={round:save.round,sessionIndex:weekendContext.sessionIndex,states:weekendContext.states,gridOrder:weekendContext.gridOrder,sessionResults:weekendContext.sessionResults};persist()}
function beginWeekend(kind,spec){
 currentMode=kind;if(kind==='season'){if(!save.seasonTeam){save.seasonTeam=selectedTeamKey();const seat=driverRoster.find(d=>d.team===save.seasonTeam);save.seasonSeatUid=seat?seat.uid:null;persist()}if($('teamSelect')){$('teamSelect').value=save.seasonTeam;syncTeamPreset(false)}}
 const restored=kind==='season'&&save.seasonWeekend&&save.seasonWeekend.round===save.round?save.seasonWeekend:null;
 weekendContext={mode:kind,spec,sessionIndex:restored?restored.sessionIndex:0,states:restored?restored.states:{},gridOrder:restored?restored.gridOrder:null,sessionResults:restored?restored.sessionResults:{},raceLaps:kind==='grandprix'?+$('gpLaps').value:3,diff:kind==='grandprix'?+$('gpDiff').value:+$('diff').value,weather:kind==='grandprix'?$('gpWeather').value:'VARIABLE'};
 $('menu').classList.add('hidden');$('finish').classList.add('hidden');$('weekendPanel').classList.remove('hidden');renderWeekendHub();renderTeamChooser();saveWeekendProgress();
}
function renderWeekendHub(){
 if(!weekendContext)return;const w=weekendContext,spec=w.spec,idx=w.sessionIndex;
 $('weekendTitle').innerHTML=`${flagMarkup(spec)} ${stripCountryEmoji(spec.country)} · ${spec.name}`;$('weekendSubtitle').textContent=w.mode==='season'?'TEMPORADA 2026 · R'+String(spec.r).padStart(2,'0'):'GRAN PREMIO · FIN DE SEMANA';
 $('weekendStages').innerHTML=WEEKEND_SESSIONS.map((s,i)=>{const st=w.states[s]||'',cl=i===idx?'current':st==='done'?'done':st==='skipped'?'skipped':'';return `<div class="sessionStage ${cl}"><b>${sessionLabel(s)}</b>${st==='done'?'COMPLETADO':st==='skipped'?'SALTADO':i===idx?'SIGUIENTE':'PENDIENTE'}</div>`}).join('');
 let last=null;for(let i=Math.min(idx-1,4);i>=0;i--){const s=WEEKEND_SESSIONS[i];if(w.sessionResults[s]){last={s,r:w.sessionResults[s]};break}}
 $('weekendTiming').innerHTML=last?timingMarkup(last.r,sessionLabel(last.s)):'';
 const actions=$('weekendActions');actions.innerHTML='';
 if(idx>=WEEKEND_SESSIONS.length){$('weekendNote').textContent='Fin de semana completado.';return}
 const s=WEEKEND_SESSIONS[idx];
 const play=document.createElement('button');play.className='btn primary';play.textContent='DISPUTAR '+sessionLabel(s);play.onclick=playWeekendSession;actions.appendChild(play);
 if(!(s==='race'&&w.mode==='grandprix')){const skip=document.createElement('button');skip.className='btn';skip.textContent='SALTAR '+sessionLabel(s);skip.onclick=skipWeekendSession;actions.appendChild(skip)}
 if(w.mode==='season'){const all=document.createElement('button');all.className='btn';all.textContent='SALTAR GP COMPLETO';all.onclick=skipWholeSeasonGP;actions.appendChild(all)}
 $('weekendNote').textContent=s==='race'?(w.mode==='season'?'En Temporada la carrera también se puede simular/saltar.':'En Gran Premio la carrera es obligatoria.'):'Las sesiones previas son opcionales. Si saltas la Clasificación, la parrilla se simula.';
}
function playWeekendSession(){if(!weekendContext)return;const s=WEEKEND_SESSIONS[weekendContext.sessionIndex],laps=s==='race'?weekendContext.raceLaps:999;currentSession=s;sessionSettings={diff:weekendContext.diff,weather:weekendContext.weather,noRivals:false};$('weekendPanel').classList.add('hidden');startRace(weekendContext.spec,laps,weekendContext.mode,$('weekendMenu'))}
function skipWeekendSession(){if(!weekendContext)return;const s=WEEKEND_SESSIONS[weekendContext.sessionIndex];if(s==='race'&&weekendContext.mode==='grandprix')return;if(s==='race'){const order=simulateRaceOrder(weekendContext.gridOrder);weekendContext.sessionResults.race=order.map((d,i)=>({uid:d.uid,name:d.name,team:d.team,time:estimateLapBase(weekendContext.spec)*(1.8+i*.015)}));weekendContext.states.race='skipped';awardSeasonOrder(order);weekendContext=null;goMenu();renderSchedule();return}const res=simulateTiming(s);weekendContext.sessionResults[s]=res;weekendContext.states[s]='skipped';if(s==='qualifying')weekendContext.gridOrder=res.map(x=>x.uid);weekendContext.sessionIndex++;saveWeekendProgress();renderWeekendHub()}
function skipWholeSeasonGP(){if(!weekendContext||weekendContext.mode!=='season')return;if(!confirm('¿Simular y saltar completamente este Gran Premio?'))return;const q=simulateTiming('qualifying'),order=simulateRaceOrder(q.map(x=>x.uid));awardSeasonOrder(order);weekendContext=null;goMenu();renderSchedule()}
function skipSeasonGPFromMenu(){if(save.round>=F1_SEASON_COUNT)return;currentMode='season';beginWeekend('season',calendar[save.round]);skipWholeSeasonGP()}
function practiceRecordTime(rec){return Number.isFinite(rec)?rec:(rec&&Number.isFinite(rec.time)?rec.time:Infinity)}
function practiceRecordVehicle(rec){return rec&&typeof rec==='object'?(rec.vehicle||[rec.team,rec.carModel].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')):''}
function practiceRecordDate(rec){
 if(!rec||typeof rec!=='object'||!rec.recordedAt)return'';
 const d=new Date(rec.recordedAt);if(!Number.isFinite(d.getTime()))return'';
 try{return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('es-ES',{day:'2-digit',month:'2-digit',year:'numeric',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}).format(d)}catch(e){return d.toLocaleString()}
}
function recordSessionLap(lt){
 if(!Number.isFinite(lt)||lt<20||currentSession!=='freepractice'||!player)return;
 const key=trackData.spec.dir,old=save.practiceRecords[key],oldTime=practiceRecordTime(old);
 if(lt<oldTime){
   save.practiceRecords[key]={time:lt,vehicle:[player.teamName,player.carModel].filter(Boolean).join(' · '),team:player.teamName||'',carModel:player.carModel||'',recordedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
   persist();$('raceAlert').textContent='NUEVO RÉCORD PERSONAL · '+fmt(lt);$('raceAlert').style.color='#c78cff';
 }
}
function finishNonRaceSession(){
 if(currentSession==='freepractice'){
   const spec=trackData&&trackData.spec?trackData.spec:calendar[+$('practiceTrack').value];
   const pages=buildPracticeSoloPages(spec||{country:'GP',name:'Circuito'});
   showFinishFlow(pages,()=>goMenu());
   return;
 }
 if(!weekendContext){goMenu();return}
 const s=currentSession;
 let res=simulateTiming(s,best<1e8?best:null);
 res=res.map(r=>{const car=r.uid==='PLAYER'?player:rivals.find(c=>c.uid===r.uid);return car&&Number.isFinite(car.broadcastBestLap)?{...r,time:car.broadcastBestLap,sectors:car.bestLapSectorTimes.slice()}:r;}).sort((a,b)=>a.time-b.time);
 res=prepareSimulatedSessionResults(s,res);
 weekendContext.sessionResults[s]=res;weekendContext.states[s]='done';
 if(s==='qualifying')weekendContext.gridOrder=res.map(x=>x.uid);
 weekendContext.sessionIndex++;saveWeekendProgress();
 const spec=weekendContext.spec;
 const pages=buildTimedSessionPages(spec,s,res);
 player=null;trackData=null;
 showFinishFlow(pages,()=>{$('finish').classList.add('hidden');$('weekendPanel').classList.remove('hidden');mode='menu';renderWeekendHub();});
}
function renderPracticeRecord(){if(!$('practiceTrack'))return;const spec=calendar[+$('practiceTrack').value],rec=save.practiceRecords[spec.dir],t=practiceRecordTime(rec),veh=practiceRecordVehicle(rec),dt=practiceRecordDate(rec);$('practiceRecord').textContent=Number.isFinite(t)?('MEJOR HISTÓRICO '+fmt(t)+(veh?' · '+veh:'')+(dt?' · '+dt:'')):'MEJOR HISTÓRICO —';const pf=$('practiceTrackFlag');if(pf)pf.innerHTML=flagMarkup(spec)+' '+stripCountryEmoji(spec.country)+' · '+spec.name;updateCircuitCard('practiceTrack','practiceCircuitInfo',{showPracticeBest:true});}

function circuitThemeLabel(theme){return ({park:'Parque urbano',city:'Urbano',forest:'Bosque',dry:'Técnico',mountain:'Montaña',cloud:'Templado',dunes:'Dunas',tropical:'Tropical',night:'Nocturno',sunset:'Crepuscular'})[theme]||'Circuito';}
function svgSafeText(s){return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');}
function apexPreviewNamedSectionDefs(dir){
 const j=readStoredCircuitJson(dir)||{},ns=Array.isArray(j.namedSections)?j.namedSections:[];
 if(ns.length)return ns.filter(x=>x&&x.name&&Number.isFinite(+x.lapFraction)).map(x=>[String(x.name),+x.lapFraction,Number.isFinite(+x.previewDx)?+x.previewDx:12,Number.isFinite(+x.previewDy)?+x.previewDy:0]);
 return [];
}
function trackPreviewLabelSvg(dir,sample,sc,ox,oy){
 const defs=apexPreviewNamedSectionDefs(dir); if(!defs.length)return '';
 return defs.map(it=>{
   const name=it[0],frac=it[1],dx=it[2]||0,dy=it[3]||0;
   const i=Math.max(0,Math.min(sample.length-1,Math.round(frac*(sample.length-1))));
   const p=sample[i],x=p[0]*sc+ox,y=p[1]*sc+oy,lx=x+dx,ly=y+dy,anchor=dx<0?'end':'start';
   return `<g opacity=".98"><circle cx="${x.toFixed(1)}" cy="${y.toFixed(1)}" r="2.4" fill="rgba(255,255,255,.95)"/><path d="M${x.toFixed(1)} ${y.toFixed(1)} L${lx.toFixed(1)} ${ly.toFixed(1)}" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,.34)" stroke-width="1.2" stroke-linecap="round"/><text x="${lx.toFixed(1)}" y="${ly.toFixed(1)}" font-size="10.4" font-family="Inter,Segoe UI,Arial,sans-serif" font-weight="800" fill="rgba(241,246,255,.98)" text-anchor="${anchor}" dominant-baseline="central" paint-order="stroke" stroke="rgba(5,8,14,.92)" stroke-width="2.8" stroke-linejoin="round">${svgSafeText(name)}</text></g>`;
 }).join('');
}
function apexPreviewCornerNames(markers,dir,length=0){
 const L=Number.isFinite(+length)&&+length>0?+length:1;
 const out=apexNamedCornerMarkers(markers,dir).filter(m=>Number.isFinite(+m.s)&&+m.turn>0).map(m=>({...m})).sort((a,b)=>a.turn-b.turn);
 // Los nombres históricos del menú (La Source, Eau Rouge, Tarzan, Lesmo, etc.)
 // no sustituyen la geometría Tn: se asignan a la curva real más cercana por posición de vuelta.
 const defs=apexPreviewNamedSectionDefs(dir),used=new Set();
 for(const d of defs){
   const name=String(d&&d[0]||'').trim(),frac=Number(d&&d[1]);
   if(!name||!Number.isFinite(frac))continue;
   // Los rótulos puramente Tn/Tn-Tm no aportan un nombre histórico adicional.
   if(/^T\d+(?:\s*[-–]\s*T?\d+)?$/i.test(name))continue;
   let best=-1,bestDist=Infinity;
   for(let i=0;i<out.length;i++){
     if(used.has(i))continue;
     const mf=((+out[i].s%L)+L)%L/L,raw=Math.abs(mf-frac),dist=Math.min(raw,1-raw);
     if(dist<bestDist){bestDist=dist;best=i;}
   }
   if(best<0)continue;
   // Sólo usamos la tabla histórica cuando JSON/SVG no trae ya un nombre específico.
   const cur=String(out[best].name||'').trim(),generic=!cur||cur===`T${out[best].turn}`||/^T\d+$/i.test(cur);
   if(generic)out[best].name=name;
   else if(cur.toLowerCase()!==name.toLowerCase())out[best].previewAlias=name;
   used.add(best);
 }
 return out;
}
function apexMapPresentation(spec){
 const id=String(spec&&spec.dir||spec||''),j=readStoredCircuitJson(id)||{},v=j.visual||{},p=v.mapPresentation||v.mapOrientation||{};
 return {rotationDeg:finite(+p.rotationDeg,0),mirrorX:!!p.mirrorX,mirrorY:!!p.mirrorY,source:p.source||'APEX_DEFAULT',status:p.status||'MODELED_NOT_SURVEYED'};
}
function apexMapPresentationXY(x,y,p){
 p=p||{rotationDeg:0,mirrorX:false,mirrorY:false};let xx=finite(+x,0),yy=finite(+y,0);if(p.mirrorX)xx=-xx;if(p.mirrorY)yy=-yy;const r=finite(+p.rotationDeg,0)*Math.PI/180,c=Math.cos(r),s=Math.sin(r);return[xx*c-yy*s,xx*s+yy*c];
}
function apexMapWorldXY(x,z,p){return apexMapPresentationXY(x,-z,p)}
function apexMapPresentationCache(){
 if(!trackData||!Array.isArray(trackData.pts))return null;const p=apexMapPresentation(trackData.spec),sig=[p.rotationDeg,p.mirrorX?1:0,p.mirrorY?1:0].join('|'),old=trackData._mapPresentationCache;if(old&&old.sig===sig&&old.n===trackData.pts.length)return old;
 const pts=trackData.pts.map(q=>apexMapWorldXY(q[0],q[1],p)),xs=pts.map(q=>q[0]),ys=pts.map(q=>q[1]),cache={sig,n:pts.length,p,pts,minX:Math.min(...xs),maxX:Math.max(...xs),minY:Math.min(...ys),maxY:Math.max(...ys)};trackData._mapPresentationCache=cache;return cache;
}
function apexPreviewPathFromRuns(runs,sc,ox,oy,presentation){
 if(!Array.isArray(runs)||!runs.length)return '';
 return runs.map(run=>{
   const pts=(Array.isArray(run.points)?run.points:[]).map(p=>apexMapWorldXY(+p.x,+p.z,presentation)).filter(p=>Number.isFinite(p[0])&&Number.isFinite(p[1]));
   if(pts.length<2)return '';
   return pts.map((p,i)=>(i?'L':'M')+(p[0]*sc+ox).toFixed(1)+' '+(p[1]*sc+oy).toFixed(1)).join(' ');
 }).filter(Boolean).join(' ');
}
function apexPreviewPathPoint(sample,length,s,sc,ox,oy){
 const n=sample.length;if(n<2)return null;const frac=((+s%length)+length)%length/length,index=frac*n,at=Math.floor(index),t=index-at;
 const p0=sample[(at+n)%n],p1=sample[(at+1+n)%n],x=(p0[0]+(p1[0]-p0[0])*t)*sc+ox,y=(p0[1]+(p1[1]-p0[1])*t)*sc+oy;
 const tx=(p1[0]-p0[0]),ty=(p1[1]-p0[1]),mag=Math.hypot(tx,ty)||1;return{x,y,tx:tx/mag,ty:ty/mag,nx:-ty/mag,ny:tx/mag};
}
function apexPreviewCompactMarkers(markers,length,sample,sc,ox,oy){
 if(!Array.isArray(markers)||!markers.length)return '';
 const n=sample.length;if(n<3)return '';
 let cx=0,cy=0;for(const p of sample){cx+=p[0];cy+=p[1]}cx=cx/n;cy=cy/n;
 const used=[];
 function pushApart(x,y,minD){
   let px=x,py=y;
   for(let pass=0;pass<12;pass++){
     let moved=false;
     for(const u of used){const dx=px-u.x,dy=py-u.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||.001;if(d<minD){const push=(minD-d)/2,nx=dx/d,ny=dy/d;px+=nx*push;py+=ny*push;moved=true;}}
     if(!moved)break;
   }
   return {x:px,y:py};
 }
 return markers.map(m=>{
   const turn=+m.turn||0;if(turn<=0)return '';
   const p=apexPreviewPathPoint(sample,length,+m.s||0,sc,ox,oy);if(!p)return '';
   const dx=p.x-(cx*sc+ox),dy=p.y-(cy*sc+oy),mag=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,rx=dx/mag,ry=dy/mag;
   const base=pushApart(p.x+rx*8.5,p.y+ry*8.5,11.5); used.push(base);
   const primaryName=String(m.name||'').trim(),alias=String(m.previewAlias||'').trim(),genericName=primaryName===''||primaryName===`T${turn}`;
   const badge=`<g><circle cx="${base.x.toFixed(1)}" cy="${base.y.toFixed(1)}" r="6.1" fill="#07101d" stroke="#d8f6ff" stroke-width="1.35"/><text x="${base.x.toFixed(1)}" y="${(base.y+.25).toFixed(1)}" fill="#eff9ff" font-size="7.3" font-weight="900" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="middle" font-family="system-ui,Segoe UI,Arial">${turn}</text></g>`;
   if(genericName)return `<g><path d="M${p.x.toFixed(1)} ${p.y.toFixed(1)} L${base.x.toFixed(1)} ${base.y.toFixed(1)}" stroke="rgba(214,238,255,.48)" stroke-width=".85"/>${badge}</g>`;
   const lx=base.x+rx*8.5,ly=base.y+ry*7.5,anchor=rx<-.25?'end':'start';
   const fullLabel=alias&&alias.toLowerCase()!==primaryName.toLowerCase()?`${primaryName} / ${alias}`:primaryName,label=fullLabel.length>20?fullLabel.slice(0,19)+'…':fullLabel;
   return `<g><path d="M${p.x.toFixed(1)} ${p.y.toFixed(1)} L${base.x.toFixed(1)} ${base.y.toFixed(1)}" stroke="rgba(214,238,255,.48)" stroke-width=".85"/>${badge}<text x="${lx.toFixed(1)}" y="${ly.toFixed(1)}" font-size="8.1" font-weight="800" font-family="system-ui,Segoe UI,Arial" fill="rgba(241,246,255,.96)" text-anchor="${anchor}" dominant-baseline="middle" paint-order="stroke" stroke="rgba(5,8,14,.90)" stroke-width="2.2">${svgSafeText(label)}</text></g>`;
 }).join('');
}
function apexPreviewStartFinishSvg(sample,length,startFinish,sc,ox,oy){
 if(!startFinish)return '';
 const finish=Number.isFinite(+startFinish.finish)?apexPreviewPathPoint(sample,length,+startFinish.finish,sc,ox,oy):null;
 const start=Number.isFinite(+startFinish.start)?apexPreviewPathPoint(sample,length,+startFinish.start,sc,ox,oy):null;
 let out='';
 if(start){
   const l=6.3,x1=start.x-start.nx*l,y1=start.y-start.ny*l,x2=start.x+start.nx*l,y2=start.y+start.ny*l;
   out+=`<g data-preview-role="start-line"><path d="M${x1.toFixed(1)} ${y1.toFixed(1)} L${x2.toFixed(1)} ${y2.toFixed(1)}" stroke="rgba(8,12,18,.88)" stroke-width="4.8" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M${x1.toFixed(1)} ${y1.toFixed(1)} L${x2.toFixed(1)} ${y2.toFixed(1)}" stroke="#f2f6fa" stroke-width="2.8" stroke-linecap="round"/></g>`;
 }
 if(finish){
   const l=6.8,x1=finish.x-finish.nx*l,y1=finish.y-finish.ny*l,x2=finish.x+finish.nx*l,y2=finish.y+finish.ny*l;
   // La geometría de meta es ESTA línea transversal. La bandera lateral no define la posición.
   out+=`<g data-preview-role="finish-line"><path d="M${x1.toFixed(1)} ${y1.toFixed(1)} L${x2.toFixed(1)} ${y2.toFixed(1)}" stroke="#0b0e12" stroke-width="5.2" stroke-linecap="butt"/><path d="M${x1.toFixed(1)} ${y1.toFixed(1)} L${x2.toFixed(1)} ${y2.toFixed(1)}" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="3.1" stroke-dasharray="2.2 2.2" stroke-linecap="butt"/>`;
   const mastX=finish.x+finish.nx*11.0,mastY=finish.y+finish.ny*11.0,topY=mastY-12.5,flagW=8.6,flagH=6.6;
   out+=`<path d="M${finish.x.toFixed(1)} ${finish.y.toFixed(1)} L${mastX.toFixed(1)} ${mastY.toFixed(1)}" stroke="rgba(241,246,255,.38)" stroke-width=".8"/><path d="M${mastX.toFixed(1)} ${mastY.toFixed(1)} L${mastX.toFixed(1)} ${topY.toFixed(1)}" stroke="#e9eef3" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round"/><rect x="${mastX.toFixed(1)}" y="${(topY+.2).toFixed(1)}" width="${(flagW/2).toFixed(1)}" height="${(flagH/2).toFixed(1)}" fill="#fff"/><rect x="${(mastX+flagW/2).toFixed(1)}" y="${(topY+.2).toFixed(1)}" width="${(flagW/2).toFixed(1)}" height="${(flagH/2).toFixed(1)}" fill="#11161d"/><rect x="${mastX.toFixed(1)}" y="${(topY+flagH/2+.2).toFixed(1)}" width="${(flagW/2).toFixed(1)}" height="${(flagH/2).toFixed(1)}" fill="#11161d"/><rect x="${(mastX+flagW/2).toFixed(1)}" y="${(topY+flagH/2+.2).toFixed(1)}" width="${(flagW/2).toFixed(1)}" height="${(flagH/2).toFixed(1)}" fill="#fff"/></g>`;
 }
 return out;
}
function trackPreviewSvgFromPoints(pts,color='#59f3ff',dir='',markers=null,km=0,pitRuns=null,startFinish=null){
 if(!pts||pts.length<2)return '<div class="muted small">Sin previsualización</div>';
 const presentation=apexMapPresentation(dir),sample=pts.map(p=>apexMapWorldXY(p[0],p[1],presentation)),xs=sample.map(p=>p[0]),ys=sample.map(p=>p[1]);
 const minX=Math.min(...xs),maxX=Math.max(...xs),minY=Math.min(...ys),maxY=Math.max(...ys),w=maxX-minX||1,h=maxY-minY||1;
 const pad=14,vw=340,vh=196,sc=Math.min((vw-pad*2)/w,(vh-pad*2)/h),ox=(vw-w*sc)/2-minX*sc,oy=(vh-h*sc)/2-minY*sc;
 const d=sample.map((p,i)=>(i?'L':'M')+(p[0]*sc+ox).toFixed(1)+' '+(p[1]*sc+oy).toFixed(1)).join(' ')+' Z';
 const sp=sample[0],sx=(sp[0]*sc+ox).toFixed(1),sy=(sp[1]*sc+oy).toFixed(1);
 const L=Number.isFinite(+km)&&+km>0?(+km*1000):5414;
 const hasMarkers=Array.isArray(markers)&&markers.some(m=>Number.isFinite(+m.s));
 const labels=!hasMarkers?trackPreviewLabelSvg(dir,sample,sc,ox,oy):'';
 const previewMarkers=hasMarkers?apexPreviewCompactMarkers(apexPreviewCornerNames(markers,dir,L),L,sample,sc,ox,oy):'';
 const pitPath=apexPreviewPathFromRuns(pitRuns,sc,ox,oy,presentation);
 const pitSvg=pitPath?`<path d="${pitPath}" fill="none" stroke="rgba(0,0,0,.42)" stroke-width="7.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/><path d="${pitPath}" fill="none" stroke="#eef3f7" stroke-width="4.7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-dasharray="8 5" opacity=".98"/><path d="${pitPath}" fill="none" stroke="#7b8792" stroke-width="1.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-dasharray="8 5" opacity=".95"/>`:'';
 const sfSvg=apexPreviewStartFinishSvg(sample,L,startFinish,sc,ox,oy);
 return `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${vw} ${vh}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true"><defs><linearGradient id="tg" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="${color}"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#9d86ff"/></linearGradient><filter id="gl"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="2.7" result="b"/><feMerge><feMergeNode in="b"/><feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic"/></feMerge></filter></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="${vw}" height="${vh}" rx="18" fill="rgba(0,0,0,.0)"/>${pitSvg}<path d="${d}" fill="none" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,.10)" stroke-width="12.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/><path d="${d}" fill="none" stroke="url(#tg)" stroke-width="7.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" filter="url(#gl)"/>${sfSvg}${labels}${previewMarkers}<circle cx="${sx}" cy="${sy}" r="6.1" fill="#ffffff"/><circle cx="${sx}" cy="${sy}" r="3.0" fill="${color}"/></svg>`;
}
let apexMenuBooting=true,apexMenuCardTimer=0;
async function updateCircuitCard(selectId,targetId,opts={}){
 const sel=$(selectId),box=$(targetId); if(!sel||!box) return;
 if(apexMenuBooting){
   const spec=calendar[+sel.value]||calendar[0];
   box.innerHTML=`<div class="circuitHero"><div class="circuitHeroText"><div class="small muted">${flagMarkup(spec)} ${stripCountryEmoji(spec.country)}</div><b>${spec.name}</b><div class="muted small">Vista del circuito disponible en unos instantes…</div></div></div>`;
   return;
 }
 const spec=calendar[+sel.value]||calendar[0];
 const bestTxt=opts.showPracticeBest? (Number.isFinite(practiceRecordTime(save.practiceRecords[spec.dir]))?fmt(practiceRecordTime(save.practiceRecords[spec.dir])):'—') : null;
 box.innerHTML=`<div class="circuitHero"><div class="circuitHeroText"><div class="small muted">${flagMarkup(spec)} ${stripCountryEmoji(spec.country)}</div><b>${spec.name}</b><div class="muted small">Cargando trazado y ficha del circuito…</div></div></div><div class="trackPreviewBox"><div class="muted">Preparando vista del trazado…</div></div>`;
 try{
   const card=await withPreparedTrackSpec(spec,async(td)=>{
     apexEnsurePitFallback();
     const km=Number.isFinite(td.km)?td.km.toFixed(3)+' km':'—',laps=td.laps||raceMetaFor(spec)?.laps||'—',turns=td.turns||raceMetaFor(spec)?.turns||'—';
     const geo=trackGeometryProfileSpec(spec.dir),geoElev=geo?`${Number(geo.elevRange).toFixed(1)} m`:'—',geoWidth=geo?(Math.abs(geo.widthMin-geo.widthMax)<.01?`${geo.widthMin.toFixed(1)} m`:`${geo.widthMin.toFixed(1)}–${geo.widthMax.toFixed(1)} m`):'—',geoBank=geo?`${Number(geo.bankMax).toFixed(1)}°`:'—',geoNote=geo&&geo.note?geo.note:'';
     const pit=raceMetaFor(spec)?.pit||pitLaneSpec(),pitSide=pit?(pit.side>0?'Izquierda':'Derecha'):'—',pitSpeed=pit&&Number.isFinite(+pit.speedKph)?`${Math.round(+pit.speedKph)} km/h`:'—';
     const cornerList=Array.isArray(td.cornerMarkers)&&td.cornerMarkers.length?td.cornerMarkers:(Array.isArray(td.authoredBanking)?td.authoredBanking.filter(c=>Number.isFinite(+c.s)):[]);
     const world=td.customWorld&&td.customWorld.pitNetwork&&Array.isArray(td.customWorld.pitNetwork.runs)?td.customWorld:readEffectiveWorldProfile(spec.dir),pitRuns=world&&world.pitNetwork&&Array.isArray(world.pitNetwork.runs)?world.pitNetwork.runs:[];
     const sf=apexCircuitStartFinishS(spec);
     const preview=trackPreviewSvgFromPoints(td.pts,teamInfo(selectedTeamKey()).accent||'#59f3ff',spec.dir,cornerList,td.km,pitRuns,sf);
     return {preview,km,laps,turns,geoElev,geoWidth,geoBank,geoNote,pitSide,pitSpeed,direction:directionLabel(td.direction),type:circuitThemeLabel(spec.theme),layout:td.layout||'—',source:td.geometrySource||'—'};
   });
   box.innerHTML=`<div class="circuitHero"><div class="circuitHeroText"><div class="small muted">${flagMarkup(spec)} ${stripCountryEmoji(spec.country)} · ${spec.nonCalendar?'FUERA DEL CALENDARIO':('R'+String(spec.r).padStart(2,'0'))}</div><b>${spec.name}</b><div class="muted small">${card.type}</div></div></div><div class="trackPreviewBox">${card.preview}</div><div class="circuitMeta"><div class="stat"><span>Longitud</span><b>${card.km}</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Vueltas GP</span><b>${card.laps}</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Curvas</span><b>${card.turns}</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Sentido</span><b>${card.direction}</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Pit lane</span><b>${card.pitSide}</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Límite pit</span><b>${card.pitSpeed}</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Tipo</span><b>${card.type}</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Trazado</span><b>${card.layout}</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Origen</span><b>${card.source}</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Desnivel modelado</span><b>${card.geoElev}</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Ancho modelado</span><b>${card.geoWidth}</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Peralte máx.</span><b>${card.geoBank}</b></div>${bestTxt!==null?`<div class="stat"><span>Mejor personal</span><b>${bestTxt}</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Sesión</span><b>Entrenamiento libre</b></div>`:`<div class="stat"><span>Parrilla</span><b>22 coches</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Vista</span><b>Cockpit</b></div>`}</div>${card.geoNote?`<div class="small muted" style="margin-top:8px;opacity:.92">${card.geoNote}</div>`:''}`;
 }catch(err){
   box.innerHTML=`<div class="circuitHero"><div class="circuitHeroText"><div class="small muted">${flagMarkup(spec)} ${stripCountryEmoji(spec.country)}</div><b>${spec.name}</b><div class="muted small">No se ha podido cargar la ficha del circuito.</div></div></div><div class="trackPreviewBox"><div class="muted">Comprueba la conexión y vuelve a elegir el circuito.</div></div><div class="circuitMeta"><div class="stat"><span>Tipo</span><b>${circuitThemeLabel(spec.theme)}</b></div><div class="stat"><span>Calendario</span><b>${spec.nonCalendar?'Fuera de F1 2026':('R'+String(spec.r).padStart(2,'0'))}</b></div></div>`;
 }
}
function updateAllCircuitCards(){
 clearTimeout(apexMenuCardTimer);
 const run=()=>{
   const active=document.querySelector('#menuModeTabs .tab.active');
   const tab=active&&active.dataset?active.dataset.t:'quick';
   // En móvil sólo construimos la ficha visible. En escritorio se mantienen las tres.
   const light=(innerWidth<=900)||/Android|iPhone|iPad|Mobile/i.test(navigator.userAgent||'');
   if(light){
     if(tab==='grandprix')updateCircuitCard('gpTrack','gpCircuitInfo');
     else if(tab==='practice')updateCircuitCard('practiceTrack','practiceCircuitInfo',{showPracticeBest:true});
     else updateCircuitCard('track','quickCircuitInfo');
   }else{
     updateCircuitCard('track','quickCircuitInfo');
     updateCircuitCard('gpTrack','gpCircuitInfo');
     updateCircuitCard('practiceTrack','practiceCircuitInfo',{showPracticeBest:true});
   }
 };
 apexMenuCardTimer=setTimeout(run,apexMenuBooting?900:60);
}

function svgNum(v,d=2){return Number.isFinite(v)?Number(v).toFixed(d).replace(/\.0+$/,'').replace(/(\.\d*?)0+$/,'$1'):'0'}
function escapeXml(s){return String(s??'').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;')}
function currentSvgToolSpec(){const sel=$("svgTrackSelect");return calendar[sel?+sel.value:13]||calendar[13]}
function setSvgToolStatus(msg,kind=''){const el=$("svgToolsStatus");if(!el)return;el.className='svgToolsStatus'+(kind?(' '+kind):'');el.textContent=msg}
async function withPreparedTrackSpec(spec,fn){
 const prevTrackData=trackData,prevPlayer=player;
 const td=await loadTrack(spec);
 trackData={...td,spec};prepareTrackModel();
 try{return await fn(trackData)}finally{trackData=prevTrackData;player=prevPlayer}
}
function editableTrackPathFromPoints(pts){if(!pts||!pts.length)return'';return pts.map((p,i)=>`${i?'L':'M'} ${svgNum(p[0])} ${svgNum(-p[1])}`).join(' ')+' Z'}
function editableTrackOffsetPath(side=1,sampleStep=1){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.pts||!trackData.pts.length)return'';const n=trackData.pts.length,L=trackData.km*1000,step=L/n,out=[];
 for(let i=0;i<n;i+=Math.max(1,sampleStep)){
   const s=i*step,q=poseAt(s),env=envProfile(s),half=Math.max(3,env.half||6.2),x=q.x+q.nx*half*side,z=q.z+q.nz*half*side;out.push([x,z]);
 }
 return out.length?editableTrackPathFromPoints(out):'';
}
function editablePitLanePath(){
 if(!trackData)return'';
 // v5.230.12: si existe una red PIT (SVG exacto o fallback procedural), exportamos
 // SU geometría mundial directamente. Nunca volvemos a reconstruirla contra track_centerline.
 const pn=trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork;
 if(pn&&Array.isArray(pn.runs)&&pn.runs.length){
   return pn.runs.map(run=>{const pts=Array.isArray(run.points)?run.points:[];return pts.map((p,i)=>`${i?'L':'M'} ${svgNum(p.x)} ${svgNum(-p.z)}`).join(' ')}).filter(Boolean).join(' ');
 }
 const L=trackData.km*1000,segs=[];let cur=[];
 for(let s=0;s<L;s+=6){const pit=pitLaneAtS(s);if(pit){const q=poseAt(s),x=q.x+q.nx*pit.centerLat,z=q.z+q.nz*pit.centerLat;cur.push([x,z]);}else if(cur.length){if(cur.length>3)segs.push(cur);cur=[]}}
 if(cur.length>3)segs.push(cur);
 return segs.map(seg=>seg.map((p,i)=>`${i?'L':'M'} ${svgNum(p[0])} ${svgNum(-p[1])}`).join(' ')).join(' ');
}
function editableWallPath(side=1){
 if(!trackData)return'';const L=trackData.km*1000,segs=[];let cur=[];let prev=null;
 for(let s=0;s<L;s+=5){const wg=wallGeometryAtS(s,side);if(wg&&wg.enabled){const p=[wg.bottom.x,wg.bottom.z],jump=prev?Math.hypot(p[0]-prev[0],p[1]-prev[1]):0;if(prev&&jump>52&&cur.length>3){segs.push(cur);cur=[]}cur.push(p);prev=p}else if(cur.length){if(cur.length>3)segs.push(cur);cur=[];prev=null}}
 if(cur.length>3)segs.push(cur);
 return segs.map(seg=>seg.map((p,i)=>`${i?'L':'M'} ${svgNum(p[0])} ${svgNum(-p[1])}`).join(' ')).join(' ');
}
function landmarkApproxBounds(it){
 const a=it&&it.anchor?it.anchor:null;if(!a)return null;let rx=16,rz=16;
 if(it.type==='grandstand'){rx=Math.max(8,(it.length||40)*.55);rz=Math.max(7,(it.depth||12)*1.15)}
 else if(it.type==='pit'){rx=Math.max(10,(it.length||70)*.55);rz=Math.max(10,(it.depth||18)*1.22)}
 else if(it.type==='block'||it.type==='building'){rx=Math.max(8,(it.width||20)*.9);rz=Math.max(8,(it.depth||20)*.9)}
 else if(it.type==='tower'){rx=Math.max(8,(it.width||8)*1.6);rz=rx}
 else if(it.type==='ferris'){rx=rz=Math.max(14,(it.radius||20)*1.35)}
 else if(it.type==='tree'){rx=rz=Math.max(8,(it.width||5)*1.9)}
 else if(it.type==='tunnelPortal'){rx=Math.max(10,(it.span||16)*.8);rz=Math.max(7,(it.deckWidth||22)*.4)}
 else if(it.type==='road'||it.type==='parking'||it.type==='forestZone'||it.type==='bridge'||it.type==='tunnel'||it.type==='terrainZone'||it.type==='surfaceZone'||it.type==='water'||it.type==='marking'||it.type==='barrier'){rx=Math.max(12,(it.width||it.length||24)*.8);rz=Math.max(10,(it.depth||it.width||18)*.8)}
 if(Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3){let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minZ=Infinity,maxZ=-Infinity;for(const p of it.worldFootprint){minX=Math.min(minX,p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p[0]);minZ=Math.min(minZ,p[1]);maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,p[1])}return {minX,maxX,minZ,maxZ}}
 return {minX:a.x-rx,maxX:a.x+rx,minZ:a.z-rz,maxZ:a.z+rz};
}
function editableWorldBounds(){
 const pts=trackData&&trackData.pts?trackData.pts:[];let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minZ=Infinity,maxZ=-Infinity;
 for(const p of pts){minX=Math.min(minX,p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p[0]);minZ=Math.min(minZ,p[1]);maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,p[1])}
 for(const it of trackLandmarks()){const bb=landmarkApproxBounds(it);if(!bb)continue;minX=Math.min(minX,bb.minX);maxX=Math.max(maxX,bb.maxX);minZ=Math.min(minZ,bb.minZ);maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,bb.maxZ)}
 if(!Number.isFinite(minX)){minX=-200;maxX=200;minZ=-200;maxZ=200}
 const margin=140;return {minX:minX-margin,maxX:maxX+margin,minY:-(maxZ+margin),maxY:-(minZ-margin),width:(maxX-minX)+margin*2,height:(maxZ-minZ)+margin*2}
}
function anchorAngleDeg(anchor){const q=anchor&&(anchor.q||anchor.groundQ);if(!q)return 0;return Math.atan2(-q.tz,q.tx)*180/Math.PI}
function landmarkRoleName(it){
 if(!it)return 'generic';
 if(it.sourceRole)return String(it.sourceRole);
 if(it.type==='gantry'&&it.startGantry)return 'start-gantry';
 if(it.type==='gantry'&&it.finishGantry)return 'finish-gantry';
 if(it.type==='surfaceZone')return 'runoff-'+String(it.surfaceKind||'ASPHALT').toLowerCase();
 if(it.type==='water')return it.waterKind==='SEA'?'sea':'lake';
 if(it.type==='marking')return it.markingKind==='START_LINE'?'start-line':(it.markingKind==='FINISH_LINE'?'finish-line':(it.markingKind==='URBAN_STRIPES'?'urban-stripes':'crosswalk'));
 if(it.type==='distanceBoard')return 'distance-board';
 if(it.type==='barrier'){const m={TECPRO:'barrier-tecpro',SAFER:'barrier-safer',TYRE:'barrier-tirewall',CONCRETE:'barrier-concrete',GUARDRAIL:'barrier-guardrail'};return m[it.barrierKind]||'barrier-concrete'}
 if(it.type==='road')return it.roadKind==='CITY'?'city-road':(it.roadKind==='SERVICE'?'service-road':'road');
 if(it.type==='bridge'&&it.pedestrianBridge)return 'pedestrian-bridge';
 const map={grandstand:'grandstand',pit:'pit-building',block:'building',building:'building',tower:'tower',ferris:'ferris',tree:'tree',gantry:'gantry',billboard:'billboard',tunnelPortal:'tunnel-portal',flagPole:'flag-pole',road:'road',parking:'parking',forestZone:'forest-zone',bridge:'bridge',tunnel:'tunnel',terrainZone:'terrain-zone'};
 return map[it.type]||String(it.type||'generic');
}
function landmarkSvgNode(it,index){
 const a=it.anchor||{},x=+svgNum(a.x),y=+svgNum(-a.z),nm=escapeXml(it.name||`${it.type||'item'} ${index+1}`),role=landmarkRoleName(it),variant=it.treeVariant||it.variant||'',extraSurface=it.surfaceKind?` data-apex-surface="${escapeXml(it.surfaceKind)}"`:'',extraClear=Number.isFinite(it.clearance)?` data-apex-clearance="${escapeXml(it.clearance)}"`:'',extraCollision=it.type==='barrier'?` data-apex-collision="${it.collision===false?'false':'true'}"`:'',apexStyle=it.apexStyle||it.style||apexSvgDefaultStyle(it.type||'block',role,null),family=it.apexFamily||apexSvgFamilyFor(it.type||'block',role),base=`id="lm_${index}" data-apex-type="${escapeXml(it.type||'block')}" data-apex-family="${escapeXml(family)}" data-apex-role="${escapeXml(role)}" data-apex-style="${escapeXml(apexStyle)}" data-apex-name="${nm}" data-apex-side="${escapeXml(it.side??0)}" data-apex-height="${escapeXml(it.height??0)}" data-apex-width="${escapeXml(it.width??0)}" data-apex-depth="${escapeXml(it.depth??0)}" data-apex-length="${escapeXml(it.length??0)}" data-apex-radius="${escapeXml(it.radius??0)}" data-apex-color="${escapeXml(it.color||it.foliage||'')}" data-apex-variant="${escapeXml(variant)}" data-apex-maxds="${escapeXml(it.maxDs??a.maxRenderDistance??0)}"${extraSurface}${extraClear}${extraCollision}${it.type==='tunnel'&&Number.isFinite(it.roofRiseM)?` data-apex-roof-rise-m="${svgNum(it.roofRiseM)}"`:''}${it.type==='pit'?` data-apex-pit-style="${escapeXml(it.pitStyle||apexStyle||'pit_modern_f1')}" data-apex-garage-bays="${escapeXml(it.garageBays??'')}" data-apex-glass="${it.hasGlass===false?'false':'true'}" data-apex-balcony="${it.hasBalcony===false?'false':'true'}" data-apex-pitwall="${it.hasPitwall===false?'false':'true'}" data-apex-garage-style="${escapeXml(it.garageStyle||'f1')}"`:''}${it.type==='grandstand'?` data-apex-grandstand-style="${escapeXml(it.grandstandStyle||apexStyle||'grandstand_open')}" data-apex-covered="${it.hasRoof===false?'false':'true'}" data-apex-seat-color="${escapeXml(it.seatColor||'')}" data-apex-seat-rows="${escapeXml(it.seatRows??'')}" data-apex-roof-style="${escapeXml(it.roofStyle||'cantilever')}"`:''}${(it.type==='block'||it.type==='building')?` data-apex-building-style="${escapeXml(it.buildingStyle||apexStyle||'building_concrete')}" data-apex-roof-style="${escapeXml(it.roofStyle||'flat')}" data-apex-glass="${it.hasGlass===false?'false':'true'}" data-apex-panels="${it.hasPanels===false?'false':'true'}" data-apex-lights="${it.hasLights===true?'true':'false'}"`:''}`;
 const ang=anchorAngleDeg(a),rot=`rotate(${svgNum(ang,2)} ${svgNum(x)} ${svgNum(y)})`;
 if(it.type==='grandstand'||it.type==='pit'){
   const cls=it.type==='pit'?'pit':'grandstand';
   if(Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3){const pts=it.worldFootprint.map(p=>`${svgNum(p[0])},${svgNum(-p[1])}`).join(' ');return `<polygon ${base} class="${cls}" points="${pts}" />`}
   const len=Math.max(12,it.length||70),dep=Math.max(7,it.depth||16);return `<rect ${base} class="${cls}" x="${svgNum(x-len*.5)}" y="${svgNum(y-dep*.5)}" width="${svgNum(len)}" height="${svgNum(dep)}" rx="2.8" transform="${rot}" />`;
 }
 if(it.type==='block'||it.type==='building'){if(Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3){const pts=it.worldFootprint.map(p=>`${svgNum(p[0])},${svgNum(-p[1])}`).join(' ');return `<polygon ${base} class="building" points="${pts}" />`}const w=Math.max(8,it.width||20),d=Math.max(8,it.depth||16);return `<rect ${base} class="building" x="${svgNum(x-w*.5)}" y="${svgNum(y-d*.5)}" width="${svgNum(w)}" height="${svgNum(d)}" rx="2.2" transform="${rot}" />`}
 if(it.type==='tower'){const w=Math.max(6,it.width||10),h=Math.max(18,Math.min(36,(it.height||60)*.18));return `<rect ${base} class="tower" x="${svgNum(x-w*.5)}" y="${svgNum(y-h)}" width="${svgNum(w)}" height="${svgNum(h)}" rx="1.8" />`}
 if(it.type==='ferris'){const r=Math.max(10,it.radius||18);return `<circle ${base} class="ferris" cx="${svgNum(x)}" cy="${svgNum(y)}" r="${svgNum(r)}" />`}
 if(it.type==='tree'){const r=Math.max(4,(it.width||5)*.85),v=it.treeVariant||it.variant||'deciduous';if(v==='fir')return `<polygon ${base} class="tree tree-fir" points="${svgNum(x)},${svgNum(y-r*1.35)} ${svgNum(x-r)},${svgNum(y+r)} ${svgNum(x+r)},${svgNum(y+r)}" />`;if(v==='palm')return `<path ${base} class="tree tree-palm" d="M ${svgNum(x)} ${svgNum(y+r)} L ${svgNum(x)} ${svgNum(y-r*.35)} M ${svgNum(x)} ${svgNum(y-r*.35)} L ${svgNum(x-r)} ${svgNum(y-r)} M ${svgNum(x)} ${svgNum(y-r*.35)} L ${svgNum(x+r)} ${svgNum(y-r)} M ${svgNum(x)} ${svgNum(y-r*.35)} L ${svgNum(x-r*.75)} ${svgNum(y-r*.1)} M ${svgNum(x)} ${svgNum(y-r*.35)} L ${svgNum(x+r*.75)} ${svgNum(y-r*.1)}" />`;return `<circle ${base} class="tree tree-deciduous" cx="${svgNum(x)}" cy="${svgNum(y)}" r="${svgNum(r)}" />`}
 if(it.type==='gantry'||it.type==='billboard'||it.type==='advertising'){const w=Math.max(10,it.span||it.width||16),h=Math.max(3,it.height||4.5),cls=it.type==='gantry'?'gantry':'billboard';return `<rect ${base} class="${cls}" x="${svgNum(x-w*.5)}" y="${svgNum(y-h*.5)}" width="${svgNum(w)}" height="${svgNum(h)}" rx="1.2" transform="${rot}" />`}
 if(it.type==='distanceBoard'){const w=Math.max(2.2,it.width||2.9),d=Math.max(.8,it.depth||1.1),mark=Number(it.mark)||100,boardRot=`rotate(${svgNum(ang+90,2)} ${svgNum(x)} ${svgNum(y)})`;return `<g><rect ${base} data-apex-mark="${escapeXml(mark)}" data-apex-lat-extra="${escapeXml(it.latExtra??1.1)}" class="distance-board" x="${svgNum(x-w*.5)}" y="${svgNum(y-d*.5)}" width="${svgNum(w)}" height="${svgNum(d)}" rx=".35" transform="${boardRot}"/><text class="distance-board-text" x="${svgNum(x)}" y="${svgNum(y)}">${escapeXml(mark)}</text></g>`}
 if(it.type==='tunnelPortal'){const w=Math.max(12,it.span||18),h=Math.max(5,it.height||6.5);return `<rect ${base} class="portal" x="${svgNum(x-w*.5)}" y="${svgNum(y-h*.5)}" width="${svgNum(w)}" height="${svgNum(h)}" rx="1.6" />`}
 if(it.type==='flagPole'){const h=Math.max(10,Math.min(28,(it.height||25)*.65));return `<line ${base} class="flagpole" x1="${svgNum(x)}" y1="${svgNum(y)}" x2="${svgNum(x)}" y2="${svgNum(y-h)}" />`}
 if(it.type==='surfaceZone'||it.type==='terrainZone'||it.type==='water'||it.type==='marking'||it.type==='barrier'){
   const cls=it.type==='barrier'?`barrier ${String(it.barrierKind||'concrete').toLowerCase()}`:(it.type==='water'?'water':(it.type==='marking'?'marking':(it.type==='terrainZone'?'terrain-zone':'surface')));
   if(Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&it.worldPath.length>=2){const d=it.worldPath.map((p,i)=>`${i?'L':'M'} ${svgNum(p[0])} ${svgNum(-p[1])}`).join(' ');return `<path ${base} class="${cls}" d="${d}" />`}
   if(Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3){const pts=it.worldFootprint.map(p=>`${svgNum(p[0])},${svgNum(-p[1])}`).join(' ');return `<polygon ${base} class="${cls}" points="${pts}" />`}
 }
 if(it.type==='road'||it.type==='parking'||it.type==='forestZone'||it.type==='bridge'||it.type==='tunnel'){const cls=it.type==='road'?'road':(it.type==='parking'?'parking':(it.type==='forestZone'?'forest':'infrastructure'));if(Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&it.worldPath.length>=2){const pts=it.worldPath.map(p=>`${svgNum(p[0])},${svgNum(-p[1])}`).join(' ');if(it.type==='road')return `<polyline ${base} class="${cls}" points="${pts}" />`;const d=it.worldPath.map((p,i)=>`${i?'L':'M'} ${svgNum(p[0])} ${svgNum(-p[1])}`).join(' ');return `<path ${base} class="${cls}" d="${d}" />`}if(Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3){const pts=it.worldFootprint.map(p=>`${svgNum(p[0])},${svgNum(-p[1])}`).join(' ');return `<polygon ${base} class="${cls}" points="${pts}" />`}const w=Math.max(8,it.width||20),d=Math.max(8,it.depth||14);return `<rect ${base} class="${cls}" x="${svgNum(x-w*.5)}" y="${svgNum(y-d*.5)}" width="${svgNum(w)}" height="${svgNum(d)}" rx="2.2" transform="${rot}" />`}
 const w=Math.max(8,it.width||12),h=Math.max(8,it.depth||10);return `<rect ${base} class="generic" x="${svgNum(x-w*.5)}" y="${svgNum(y-h*.5)}" width="${svgNum(w)}" height="${svgNum(h)}" rx="1.8" />`
}

function apexEnsureSvgLibraryStyle(doc,root){
 const ns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';let defs=root.querySelector('defs');if(!defs){defs=doc.createElementNS(ns,'defs');root.insertBefore(defs,root.firstChild)}
 let st=defs.querySelector('#APEX_LIBRARY_STYLE_V3');if(!st){st=doc.createElementNS(ns,'style');st.setAttribute('id','APEX_LIBRARY_STYLE_V3');defs.appendChild(st)}st.textContent=SVG_LIBRARY_SCOPED_CSS;
}
function buildSvgElementLibrary(bounds){
 const x=bounds.maxX+90,y=bounds.minY,W=1220,H=1010,pad=20,gap=16,colw=(W-pad*2-gap*2)/3,c1=x+pad,c2=c1+colw+gap,c3=c2+colw+gap;
 const layouts={
  TIMING:[c1,y+78,colw,330],TRACK:[c1,y+424,colw,270],SIGNAGE:[c1,y+710,colw,250],
  SAFETY:[c2,y+78,colw,250],RACE_INFRA:[c2,y+344,colw,300],URBAN:[c2,y+660,colw,300],
  ENV:[c3,y+78,colw,430]
 };
 const cats=[
  ['TIMING','CRONOMETRAJE · ACTIVE AERO',[
   ['start-line','START LINE'],['finish-line','FINISH / CONTROL'],['s1','S1 CONTROL'],['s2','S2 CONTROL'],['speedtrap','SPEED TRAP'],['sm','SM ACT'],['omdet','OM DET'],['omact','OM ACT'],['drsdet','DRS DET · LEGACY'],['drsstart','DRS START · LEGACY'],['drsend','DRS END · LEGACY']]],

   ['corner-number','CURVA · T1'],['track-centerline','TRACK CENTERLINE'],['track-left-edge','LÍMITE IZQUIERDO'],['track-right-edge','LÍMITE DERECHO'],['curb-left','PIANO IZQUIERDO'],['curb-right','PIANO DERECHO'],['drivable-area','DRIVABLE AREA'],['pitlane','PIT LANE']]],
  ['SIGNAGE','SEÑALIZACIÓN DE CARRERA',[
   ['billboard','CARTEL FÍSICO'],['crosswalk','PASO DE CEBRA'],['stripes','RALLAS / CEBREADO'],['distance','CARTEL 200 m',200],['distance','CARTEL 150 m',150],['distance','CARTEL 100 m',100],['distance','CARTEL 50 m',50]]],
  ['SAFETY','BARRERAS · SEGURIDAD',[
   ['wall-left','MURO IZQUIERDO'],['wall-right','MURO DERECHO'],['barrier-tecpro','BARRERA TECPRO'],['barrier-safer','BARRERA SAFER'],['barrier-tire','FILAS NEUMÁTICOS'],['barrier-concrete','MURO HORMIGÓN'],['barrier-guardrail','GUARDARRAÍL']]],
  ['RACE_INFRA','INFRAESTRUCTURA DE CARRERA',[
   ['pit-building','EDIFICIO DE BOXES'],['grandstand','GRADA'],['race-tunnel','TÚNEL DE PISTA'],['race-portal','PORTAL TÚNEL'],['race-bridge','PUENTE DE PISTA'],['start-gantry','PÓRTICO SALIDA'],['finish-gantry','PÓRTICO META'],['gantry','PÓRTICO / PASARELA']]],
  ['URBAN','VIALES · INFRAESTRUCTURA GENERAL',[
   ['building','EDIFICIO'],['tower','TORRE'],['service-road','CARRETERA SERVICIO'],['city-road-line','VIAL URBANO · LÍNEA'],['city-road-polyline','VIAL URBANO · POLILÍNEA'],['parking','PARKING'],['ped-bridge','PUENTE PEATONAL']]],
  ['ENV','ENTORNO · SUPERFICIES',[
   ['flagpole','MÁSTIL'],['ferris','NORIA'],['fir','ABETO'],['decid','ÁRBOL CADUCO'],['palm','PALMERA'],['forest','BOSQUE DE ABETOS'],['terrain','ZONA TERRENO'],['runoff-asphalt','ESCAPATORIA ASFALTO'],['runoff-gravel','ZONA GRAVA'],['runoff-sand','ZONA ARENA'],['runoff-grass','ZONA CÉSPED'],['lake','LAGO'],['sea','MAR'],['contour','POLÍGONO NIVEL +10 m']]
  ]
 ];
 const shape=(kind,ix,iy,mark)=>{const cx=ix+55,cy=iy+18;
  if(kind==='building')return `<rect data-apex-type="building" data-apex-role="building" data-apex-height="16" class="building" x="${ix+20}" y="${iy+5}" width="70" height="26" rx="3"/>`;
  if(kind==='grandstand')return `<rect data-apex-type="grandstand" data-apex-family="structure" data-apex-role="grandstand" data-apex-style="grandstand_covered" data-apex-height="14" data-apex-length="80" data-apex-depth="18" class="grandstand race-infra" x="${ix+16}" y="${iy+8}" width="80" height="22" rx="3"/>`;

  if(kind==='parking')return `<rect data-apex-type="parking" data-apex-role="parking" class="parking" x="${ix+10}" y="${iy+4}" width="90" height="28" rx="2"/>`;
  if(kind==='ped-bridge')return `<path data-apex-type="bridge" data-apex-family="structure" data-apex-role="pedestrian-bridge" data-apex-style="bridge_pedestrian" data-apex-width="5" data-apex-height="7" class="infrastructure" d="M ${ix+8} ${cy} L ${ix+102} ${cy}"/>`;
  if(kind==='race-bridge')return `<path data-apex-type="bridge" data-apex-family="structure" data-apex-role="bridge" data-apex-style="bridge_concrete" data-apex-width="18" data-apex-height="7" class="infrastructure race-infra" d="M ${ix+8} ${cy} L ${ix+102} ${cy}"/>`;
  if(kind==='race-tunnel')return `<path data-apex-type="tunnel" data-apex-family="structure" data-apex-role="tunnel" data-apex-style="tunnel_concrete" data-apex-tunnel-style="tunnel_concrete" data-apex-portal-style="portal_rectangular" data-apex-interior-style="concrete_panels" data-apex-lights="true" data-apex-panels="true" data-apex-width="18" data-apex-height="6" class="infrastructure race-infra" d="M ${ix+8} ${cy} L ${ix+102} ${cy}"/>`;
  if(kind==='race-portal')return `<rect data-apex-type="tunnelPortal" data-apex-family="structure" data-apex-role="tunnel-portal" data-apex-style="portal_rectangular" data-apex-width="18" data-apex-height="6" class="portal race-infra" x="${ix+30}" y="${iy+3}" width="50" height="28" rx="4"/>`;
  if(kind==='start-gantry'||kind==='finish-gantry'||kind==='gantry')return `<rect data-apex-type="gantry" data-apex-role="${kind}" data-apex-width="20" data-apex-height="6" class="gantry ${kind} race-infra" x="${ix+10}" y="${iy+7}" width="90" height="20" rx="2"/>`;
  if(kind==='crosswalk')return `<rect data-apex-type="marking" data-apex-role="crosswalk" class="marking" x="${ix+10}" y="${iy+8}" width="90" height="20"/>`;
  if(kind==='stripes')return `<polygon data-apex-type="marking" data-apex-role="urban-stripes" class="marking urban-stripes" points="${ix+8},${iy+7} ${ix+102},${iy+7} ${ix+92},${iy+29} ${ix+18},${iy+29}"/>`;
  if(kind==='terrain')return `<polygon data-apex-type="terrainZone" data-apex-role="terrain-zone" class="terrain-zone" points="${ix+8},${iy+5} ${ix+102},${iy+5} ${ix+92},${iy+31} ${ix+18},${iy+31}"/>`;
  if(kind.startsWith('runoff-')){const surf=kind.slice(7).toUpperCase(),cl=surf.toLowerCase();return `<polygon data-apex-type="surfaceZone" data-apex-role="runoff-${cl}" data-apex-surface="${surf}" class="surface ${cl}" points="${ix+8},${iy+5} ${ix+102},${iy+5} ${ix+92},${iy+31} ${ix+18},${iy+31}"/>`}
  if(kind==='lake')return `<ellipse data-apex-type="water" data-apex-role="lake" class="water" cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" rx="44" ry="16"/>`;
  if(kind==='sea')return `<rect data-apex-type="water" data-apex-role="sea" class="water" x="${ix+10}" y="${iy+3}" width="90" height="30" rx="7"/>`;
  if(kind.startsWith('barrier-')){const sub=kind.slice(8),cl=sub==='tire'?'tirewall':sub,role=sub==='tire'?'barrier-tirewall':'barrier-'+sub;return `<path data-apex-type="barrier" data-apex-role="${role}" data-apex-collision="true" class="barrier ${cl}" d="M ${ix+8} ${cy} L ${ix+102} ${cy}"/>`}
  if(kind==='wall-left'||kind==='wall-right')return `<path data-apex-type="wall" data-apex-role="${kind}" data-apex-family="wall" data-apex-style="wall_f1" class="wall-line" d="M ${ix+8} ${cy} L ${ix+102} ${cy}"/>`;
  if(kind==='pitlane')return `<path data-apex-type="pitLane" data-apex-role="pitlane-centerline" data-apex-width="5.9" class="pit-line" d="M ${ix+8} ${cy} L ${ix+102} ${cy}"/>`;
  if(kind==='flagpole')return `<line data-apex-type="flagPole" data-apex-role="flag-pole" data-apex-height="25" class="flagpole" x1="${cx}" y1="${iy+32}" x2="${cx}" y2="${iy}"/>`;
  if(kind==='billboard')return `<rect data-apex-type="billboard" data-apex-role="billboard" data-apex-width="16" data-apex-height="4" class="billboard" x="${ix+15}" y="${iy+7}" width="80" height="22" rx="2"/>`;
  if(kind==='distance')return `<g><rect data-apex-type="distanceBoard" data-apex-role="distance-board" data-apex-mark="${mark||100}" data-apex-width="2.9" data-apex-height="4" data-apex-lat-extra="1.1" class="distance-board" x="${ix+28}" y="${iy+4}" width="54" height="28" rx="2"/><text class="distance-board-text" x="${cx}" y="${cy+1}">${mark||100}</text></g>`;
  if(kind==='start-line')return `<line data-apex-type="timingMarker" data-apex-family="timing" data-apex-role="start-line" data-apex-name="Línea de salida" class="start-line-template" x1="${ix+10}" y1="${cy}" x2="${ix+100}" y2="${cy}"/>`;
  if(kind==='finish-line')return `<line data-apex-type="timingMarker" data-apex-family="timing" data-apex-role="finish-line" data-apex-name="Control / meta" class="finish-line-template" x1="${ix+10}" y1="${cy}" x2="${ix+100}" y2="${cy}"/>`;
  if(kind==='s1'||kind==='s2')return `<line data-apex-type="timingMarker" data-apex-family="timing" data-apex-role="sector-line" data-apex-sector="${kind==='s1'?1:2}" class="timing-line" x1="${ix+10}" y1="${cy-10}" x2="${ix+100}" y2="${cy+10}"/>`;
  if(kind==='speedtrap')return `<line data-apex-type="timingMarker" data-apex-family="timing" data-apex-role="speed-trap" class="speedtrap-line" x1="${ix+10}" y1="${cy-10}" x2="${ix+100}" y2="${cy+10}"/>`;
  if(kind==='sm')return `<line data-apex-type="timingMarker" data-apex-family="timing" data-apex-role="straight-mode-activation" class="straightmode-line" x1="${ix+10}" y1="${cy-10}" x2="${ix+100}" y2="${cy+10}"/>`;
  if(kind==='omdet'||kind==='omact')return `<line data-apex-type="timingMarker" data-apex-family="timing" data-apex-role="${kind==='omdet'?'override-detection':'override-activation'}" class="override-line" x1="${ix+10}" y1="${cy-10}" x2="${ix+100}" y2="${cy+10}"/>`;
  if(kind==='drsdet'||kind==='drsstart'||kind==='drsend')return `<line data-apex-type="timingMarker" data-apex-role="${kind==='drsdet'?'drs-detection':kind==='drsstart'?'drs-start':'drs-end'}" class="drs-line" x1="${ix+10}" y1="${cy-10}" x2="${ix+100}" y2="${cy+10}"/>`;
  if(kind==='contour')return `<polygon data-apex-type="terrainContour" data-apex-role="terrain-contour" data-apex-elevation-m="10" data-apex-closed="true" class="terrain-contour" points="${ix+12},${iy+27} ${ix+25},${iy+7} ${ix+55},${iy+2} ${ix+92},${iy+9} ${ix+100},${iy+26} ${ix+78},${iy+34} ${ix+35},${iy+35}"/>`;
  if(kind==='corner-number')return `<g><circle data-apex-type="cornerMarker" data-apex-family="timing" data-apex-role="corner-marker" data-apex-turn="1" data-apex-name="T1" class="corner-template-marker" cx="${ix+35}" cy="${cy}" r="7"/><text data-apex-corner-number="1" class="corner-template-number" x="${ix+49}" y="${cy}">T1</text></g>`;
  if(kind==='track-centerline')return `<path data-apex-type="track" data-apex-family="track" data-apex-role="track-centerline" data-apex-style="track_centerline" data-apex-template-core="true" class="core-track-template" d="M ${ix+8} ${cy} L ${ix+102} ${cy}"/>`;
  if(kind==='track-left-edge'||kind==='track-right-edge')return `<path data-apex-type="trackEdge" data-apex-family="track" data-apex-role="${kind}" data-apex-style="edge_line" data-apex-template-core="true" class="core-edge-template" d="M ${ix+8} ${cy} L ${ix+102} ${cy}"/>`;
  if(kind==='curb-left'||kind==='curb-right')return `<g><path class="curb-base-template" d="M ${ix+8} ${cy} L ${ix+102} ${cy}"/><path data-apex-type="marking" data-apex-family="track" data-apex-role="${kind}" data-apex-style="curb_red_white" data-apex-width="1.25" class="curb-red-template" d="M ${ix+8} ${cy} L ${ix+102} ${cy}"/></g>`;
  if(kind==='drivable-area')return `<polygon data-apex-type="drivableArea" data-apex-family="track" data-apex-role="drivable-area" data-apex-template-core="true" class="drivable-template" points="${ix+8},${iy+7} ${ix+102},${iy+7} ${ix+96},${iy+30} ${ix+14},${iy+30}"/>`;
  return '';
 };
 let total=0,groups=[];
 for(const [cid,title,items] of cats){const [sx,sy,sw,sh]=layouts[cid],cellw=(sw-24)/2,cellh=52,baseY=sy+36,body=[];body.push(`<g data-apex-library-category="${cid}"><rect class="library-section" x="${sx}" y="${sy}" width="${sw}" height="${sh}" rx="10"/><text class="library-section-title" x="${sx+14}" y="${sy+22}">${title}</text>`);items.forEach((it,i)=>{const col=i%2,row=Math.floor(i/2),ix=sx+10+col*cellw,iy=baseY+row*cellh;body.push(`<g data-apex-library-item="1" data-apex-library-label="${it[1]}">${shape(it[0],ix,iy,it[2])}<text class="lib-label" x="${ix+4}" y="${iy+46}">${it[1]}</text></g>`);total++});body.push('</g>');groups.push(body.join(''))}
 return{x,y,w:W,h:H,svg:`<g id="APEX_ELEMENT_LIBRARY" data-apex-library="1" data-apex-library-version="5"><rect id="APEX_ELEMENT_LIBRARY_ZONE" data-apex-library-zone="1" x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${W}" height="${H}" rx="16" class="library-zone"/><text class="library-title" x="${x+24}" y="${y+30}">ELEMENTOS NO INCLUIDOS · BIBLIOTECA · ${total} PLANTILLAS</text><text class="library-help" x="${x+24}" y="${y+51}">Plantillas ordenadas. Las piezas comunes se activan al sacarlas; para GEOMETRÍA DE PISTA edita/reemplaza el elemento BASE existente.</text>${groups.join('')}</g>`};
}

function apexSvgLibraryZoneBounds(root){const z=root.querySelector('[data-apex-library-zone="1"],#APEX_ELEMENT_LIBRARY_ZONE');if(!z)return null;const pts=apexSvgSampleMounted(root,z,{count:4,includeEnd:true});if(!pts.length)return null;let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minY=Infinity,maxY=-Infinity;for(const p of pts){minX=Math.min(minX,p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p[0]);minY=Math.min(minY,p[1]);maxY=Math.max(maxY,p[1])}return{minX,maxX,minY,maxY}}
function apexSvgElementInsideLibrary(ctx,el,lib){
 if(!el)return false;
 // La posición física manda: si arrastras/copies una plantilla fuera de la caja en Boxy,
 // pasa a ser un elemento activo aunque Boxy conserve el mismo grupo padre.
 if(lib){const p=apexSvgMidPoint(ctx.root,el);return !!(p&&p.x>=lib.minX&&p.x<=lib.maxX&&p.y>=lib.minY&&p.y<=lib.maxY)}
 try{return !!(el.closest&&el.closest('[data-apex-library="1"]'))}catch(e){return false}
}
function apexSvgBasicPoint(el){
 const tag=String(el&&el.tagName||'').toLowerCase(),n=a=>parseFloat(el.getAttribute(a));
 if(tag==='rect')return{x:(n('x')||0)+(n('width')||0)/2,y:(n('y')||0)+(n('height')||0)/2};
 if(tag==='circle'||tag==='ellipse')return{x:n('cx')||0,y:n('cy')||0};
 if(tag==='line')return{x:((n('x1')||0)+(n('x2')||0))/2,y:((n('y1')||0)+(n('y2')||0))/2};
 if(tag==='polygon'||tag==='polyline'){const p=parseSvgPointPairsRaw(el.getAttribute('points'));if(p.length){let x=0,y=0;for(const q of p){x+=q[0];y+=q[1]}return{x:x/p.length,y:y/p.length}}}
 if(tag==='text')return{x:n('x')||0,y:n('y')||0};
 if(tag==='path'){const m=String(el.getAttribute('d')||'').match(/[Mm]\s*(-?\d*\.?\d+(?:e[-+]?\d+)?)\s*[, ]\s*(-?\d*\.?\d+(?:e[-+]?\d+)?)/i);if(m)return{x:+m[1],y:+m[2]}}
 return null;
}
function apexUpgradeImportedSvgLibrary(rawSvg){
 // Conserva literalmente la geometría activa importada, pero sustituye únicamente
 // la zona de plantillas por la biblioteca vigente. Así un SVG antiguo obtiene
 // automáticamente todos los elementos nuevos sin regenerar pista, pit ni muros.
 try{
  const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString(String(rawSvg||''),'image/svg+xml'),root=doc.documentElement;if(!root||root.nodeName==='parsererror')return rawSvg;
  apexEnsureSvgLibraryStyle(doc,root);
  let zone=root.querySelector('[data-apex-library-zone="1"],#APEX_ELEMENT_LIBRARY_ZONE'),libGroup=root.querySelector('[data-apex-library="1"],#APEX_ELEMENT_LIBRARY');
  let vb=(root.getAttribute('viewBox')||'').trim().split(/[ ,]+/).map(Number),vx=0,vy=0,vw=2048,vh=2048;if(vb.length>=4&&vb.every(Number.isFinite)){[vx,vy,vw,vh]=vb}
  let lx=Number.NaN,ly=Number.NaN,lw=0,lh=0;
  if(zone){lx=parseFloat(zone.getAttribute('x'));ly=parseFloat(zone.getAttribute('y'));lw=parseFloat(zone.getAttribute('width'))||0;lh=parseFloat(zone.getAttribute('height'))||0}
  if(!Number.isFinite(lx))lx=vx+vw+90;if(!Number.isFinite(ly))ly=vy;
  if(libGroup){libGroup.remove();zone=null}
  else if(zone){
    const x0=lx,y0=ly,x1=lx+lw,y1=ly+lh;
    for(const el of [...root.querySelectorAll('*')]){
      if(el===zone)continue;
      const cls=String(el.getAttribute&&el.getAttribute('class')||'');
      if(/(?:^|\s)(library-title|library-help|lib-label)(?:\s|$)/.test(cls)){el.remove();continue}
      if(!(el.hasAttribute&& (el.hasAttribute('data-apex-type')||el.hasAttribute('data-apex-role'))))continue;
      const p=apexSvgBasicPoint(el);if(p&&p.x>=x0&&p.x<=x1&&p.y>=y0&&p.y<=y1)el.remove();
    }
    zone.remove();
  }
  const library=buildSvgElementLibrary({maxX:lx-90,minY:ly});
  const fragDoc=new DOMParser().parseFromString(`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">${library.svg}</svg>`,'image/svg+xml');
  for(const ch of [...fragDoc.documentElement.childNodes])if(ch.nodeType===1)root.appendChild(doc.importNode(ch,true));
  root.setAttribute('data-apex-library-outside-viewbox','true');
  apexSvgApplyUniversalSchema(root);root.setAttribute('data-apex-schema','APEX_EDITABLE_TRACK_V6');root.setAttribute('data-apex-library-version','5');
  return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n${new XMLSerializer().serializeToString(root)}`;
 }catch(e){return rawSvg}
}

    <path id="track_right_edge" data-apex-role="track-right-edge" class="track-edge" d="${rightEdge}"/>
    ${pitPath?`<path id="pitlane_centerline" data-apex-role="pitlane-centerline" data-apex-width="5.9" class="pit-line" d="${pitPath}"/>`:''}
    ${wallLeft?`<path id="wall_left" data-apex-role="wall-left" data-apex-family="wall" data-apex-style="wall_f1" data-apex-wall-style="wall_f1" data-apex-fence="true" data-apex-fence-height="2.10" data-apex-fence-lean-m="0.45" data-apex-post-spacing-m="2.6" class="wall-line" d="${wallLeft}"/>`:''}
    ${wallRight?`<path id="wall_right" data-apex-role="wall-right" data-apex-family="wall" data-apex-style="wall_f1" data-apex-wall-style="wall_f1" data-apex-fence="true" data-apex-fence-height="2.10" data-apex-fence-lean-m="0.45" data-apex-post-spacing-m="2.6" class="wall-line" d="${wallRight}"/>`:''}
    <circle id="start_marker" data-apex-type="marker" data-apex-role="start-marker" data-apex-elevation-m="0" data-apex-elevation-role="local-datum" class="start-marker" cx="${svgNum(start[0])}" cy="${svgNum(-start[1])}" r="6"/>
  </g>
  <g id="circuit_landmarks"><text class="group-title" x="${svgNum(bounds.minX+12)}" y="${svgNum(bounds.minY+20)}">ELEMENTOS ACTIVOS DEL CIRCUITO</text>
      ${nodes}</g>
  ${library.svg}
</svg>`
}

function apexExportHasRole(root,test){
 const libSel='[data-apex-library="1"],#APEX_ELEMENT_LIBRARY';
 return [...root.querySelectorAll('[data-apex-role]')].some(el=>{
   if(el.closest&&el.closest(libSel))return false;
   const r=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-role')||'').toLowerCase();
   return typeof test==='function'?!!test(r):r===String(test||'').toLowerCase();
 });
}
function apexRuntimeCrossMarkSvg(role,s,label,extraAttrs=''){
 if(!trackData)return'';const L=trackData.km*1000,ss=mod(Number.isFinite(s)?s:0,L),q=poseAt(ss),env=envProfile(ss),half=Math.max(3,env.half||6.2),th=.34;
 const P=(along,lat)=>[q.x+q.tx*along+q.nx*lat,-(q.z+q.tz*along+q.nz*lat)];
 const pts=[P(-th*.5,half),P(-th*.5,-half),P(th*.5,-half),P(th*.5,half)].map(p=>`${svgNum(p[0])},${svgNum(p[1])}`).join(' '),r=String(role||'').toLowerCase(),operational=r==='straight-mode-activation'||r==='override-detection'||r==='override-activation';
 const cls=r.startsWith('drs-')?'drs-line':(r==='speed-trap'?'speedtrap-line':(r==='straight-mode-activation'?'straightmode-line':((r==='override-detection'||r==='override-activation')?'override-line':((r==='sector-line')?'timing-line':((r==='start-line'||r==='finish-line')?'startfinish-line':'marking')))));
 return `<polygon data-apex-generated="runtime" data-apex-type="${r==='sector-line'||r.startsWith('drs-')||r==='speed-trap'||operational||r==='start-line'||r==='finish-line'?'timingMarker':'marking'}" data-apex-family="timing" data-apex-role="${escapeXml(r)}" data-apex-name="${escapeXml(label)}" data-apex-s="${svgNum(ss,3)}" ${extraAttrs||''} class="${cls}" points="${pts}"/>`;
}
function apexTimingControlResolvedS(c){
 if(!trackData||!c)return null;const L=trackData.km*1000;if(Number.isFinite(+c.s))return mod(+c.s,L);
 if(c.anchor&&Number.isFinite(+c.anchor.turn)){const turn=+c.anchor.turn,marks=Array.isArray(trackData.cornerMarkers)?trackData.cornerMarkers:[],m=marks.find(x=>+x.turn===turn);if(m&&Number.isFinite(+m.s))return mod(+m.s+(Number(c.anchor.offsetM)||0),L)}
 if(Number.isFinite(+c.lapFraction))return mod(+c.lapFraction*L,L);if(Number.isFinite(+c.lapFractionFallback))return mod(+c.lapFractionFallback*L,L);return null;
}
function apexCircuitStartFinishS(spec){
 const L=trackData?trackData.km*1000:0,td=trackData&&trackData.timingData||{};let start=Number.isFinite(+td.startS)?+td.startS:NaN,finish=Number.isFinite(+td.finishS)?+td.finishS:NaN;
 const ctrls=trackData&&Array.isArray(trackData.timingControls)&&trackData.timingControls.length?trackData.timingControls:apexDefaultTimingControls(spec);
 for(const c of ctrls){const s=apexTimingControlResolvedS(c);if(!c||!Number.isFinite(s))continue;if(c.role==='start-line')start=s;else if(c.role==='finish-line')finish=s}
