 const legacyPathOrigin=0,usedLegacy=!Number.isFinite(start)&&!Number.isFinite(finish);if(!Number.isFinite(finish))finish=Number.isFinite(start)?start:legacyPathOrigin;if(!Number.isFinite(start))start=finish;if(trackData)trackData.timingLegacyFallback=usedLegacy;
 return{start:mod(start,L||1),finish:mod(finish,L||1),legacyFallback:usedLegacy};
}
function apexGridControlRule(start,finish,L){
 if(!(L>0))return{relation:'UNKNOWN',signedDeltaM:0,controlCrossings:1};
 const eps=Math.max(.25,L*1e-7),signed=mod((finish-start)+L*.5,L)-L*.5;
 // FIA ISC 8.4.5: Control antes de Start => primer cruce completa vuelta 1.
 // Control después de Start => segundo cruce. Si ambas líneas coinciden, el primer cruce
 // inmediatamente posterior a la parrilla sólo arma la línea común y la vuelta termina al segundo.
 if(Math.abs(signed)<=eps)return{relation:'COINCIDENT',signedDeltaM:0,controlCrossings:2};
 return signed<0?{relation:'BEFORE_START',signedDeltaM:signed,controlCrossings:1}:{relation:'AFTER_START',signedDeltaM:signed,controlCrossings:2};
}
function apexRaceTimingGeometry(spec,laps=1){
 const L=trackData?trackData.km*1000:0;if(!(L>0))return{L:0,start:0,finish:0,offset:0,firstFinishDistance:0,raceDistance:0,controlRelation:'UNKNOWN',firstLapControlCrossings:1};
 const sf=apexCircuitStartFinishS(spec||trackData.spec),start=mod(sf.start,L),finish=mod(sf.finish,L),offset=mod(start-finish,L),rule=apexGridControlRule(start,finish,L),toControl=mod(finish-start,L);
 let firstFinishDistance;
 if(rule.relation==='BEFORE_START')firstFinishDistance=toControl;
 else if(rule.relation==='AFTER_START')firstFinishDistance=toControl+L;
 else firstFinishDistance=L;
 const n=Math.max(1,Math.floor(+laps||1));
 return{L,start,finish,offset,firstFinishDistance,raceDistance:firstFinishDistance+(n-1)*L,controlRelation:rule.relation,firstLapControlCrossings:rule.controlCrossings,controlSignedDeltaM:rule.signedDeltaM,firstLapDeltaM:firstFinishDistance-L};
}
function apexActiveAeroData(spec){const j=readStoredCircuitJson(spec&&spec.dir)||{};return j.activeAero&&typeof j.activeAero==='object'?j.activeAero:{}}
function apexStraightModeAuthored(spec){
 const aa=apexActiveAeroData(spec),sm=aa.straightMode&&typeof aa.straightMode==='object'?aa.straightMode:{},zones=Array.isArray(sm.zones)?sm.zones:(Array.isArray(aa.zones)?aa.zones:[]);
 return {enabled:sm.enabled!==false&&aa.straightModeEnabled!==false,source:sm.source||aa.source||'APEX_CIRCUIT_JSON',status:sm.status||aa.status||aa.quality||'MODELED_NOT_SURVEYED',sourceUrl:sm.sourceUrl||aa.sourceUrl||null,zones};
}
function apexOperationalControls(spec){
 const aa=apexActiveAeroData(spec),src=Array.isArray(aa.operationalControls)?aa.operationalControls:[],roles=new Set(['straight-mode-activation','override-detection','override-activation']),svg=trackData&&Array.isArray(trackData.timingControls)?trackData.timingControls.filter(c=>c&&roles.has(String(c.role||'').toLowerCase())&&Number.isFinite(+c.s)):[];
 const all=[...src,...svg],seen=new Set(),out=[];for(const c of all){if(!c||!c.role||!Number.isFinite(+c.s))continue;const role=String(c.role).toLowerCase(),key=role+'@'+Number(+c.s).toFixed(3);if(seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);out.push({...c,role,s:+c.s});}return out;
}
function apexResolveZoneAnchor(a){
 if(!trackData)return null;const L=trackData.km*1000,g=apexRaceTimingGeometry(trackData.spec,1);
 if(a==='finish-line')return g.finish;if(a==='start-line')return g.start;
 if(a&&Number.isFinite(+a.s))return mod(+a.s,L);
 if(a&&Number.isFinite(+a.turn)){const m=(trackData.cornerMarkers||[]).find(x=>+x.turn===+a.turn);if(m&&Number.isFinite(+m.s))return mod(+m.s+(Number(a.offsetM)||0),L)}
 return null;
}
function apexDerivedStraightModeZones(){
 if(!trackData)return[];const L=trackData.km*1000,step=20,minLen=180,maxCurv=.0022,zones=[];let run=null;
 for(let s0=0;s0<L+step;s0+=step){const ss=mod(s0,L),k=Math.abs(curvatureAt(ss)||0),straight=k<=maxCurv;
   if(straight&&!run)run={start:ss,startAbs:s0};
   if((!straight||s0>=L)&&run){const len=s0-run.startAbs;if(len>=minLen)zones.push({id:'SM'+(zones.length+1),startS:mod(run.start,L),endS:mod(run.start+len,L),lengthM:Number(len.toFixed(1)),quality:'MODELED_NOT_SURVEYED'});run=null;}
 }
 return zones.slice(0,8);
}
function apexPrepareStraightModeZones(){
 if(!trackData)return[];const authored=apexStraightModeAuthored(trackData.spec);
 if(authored.enabled===false){trackData.straightMode={...authored,quality:authored.status,zones:[]};return[];}
 if(authored.zones.length){trackData.straightMode={...authored,quality:authored.status,zones:authored.zones.map(z=>{const startS=apexResolveZoneAnchor(z.from!=null?z.from:(Number.isFinite(+z.startS)?{s:+z.startS}:null)),endS=apexResolveZoneAnchor(z.to!=null?z.to:(Number.isFinite(+z.endS)?{s:+z.endS}:null));return {...z,startS,endS,lengthM:Number.isFinite(startS)&&Number.isFinite(endS)?Number(forwardDistanceS(startS,endS,trackData.km*1000).toFixed(1)):null,status:z.status||authored.status};})};return trackData.straightMode.zones;}
 const zones=apexDerivedStraightModeZones().map(z=>({...z,status:'MODELED_NOT_SURVEYED'}));trackData.straightMode={source:authored.source||'APEX_GEOMETRY',sourceUrl:authored.sourceUrl||null,status:'MODELED_NOT_SURVEYED',quality:'MODELED_NOT_SURVEYED',enabled:true,zones};return zones;
}
function apexLapFractionFromFinish(s){
 if(!trackData)return 0;const g=apexRaceTimingGeometry(trackData.spec,1);return g.L>0?mod((+s||0)-g.finish,g.L)/g.L:0;
}
function apexDefaultTimingControls(spec){
 if(!trackData)return[];const j=readStoredCircuitJson(spec&&spec.dir),td=(j&&j.timing)||trackData.timingData||{},src=Array.isArray(td.controls)?td.controls:[];return src.filter(c=>c&&c.role).map(c=>{const s=apexTimingControlResolvedS(c);return Number.isFinite(s)?{...c,s}:null}).filter(Boolean);
}
function apexTimingSectorControls(spec){
 if(!trackData)return[];const L=trackData.km*1000,g=apexRaceTimingGeometry(spec||trackData.spec,1),src=Array.isArray(trackData.timingControls)&&trackData.timingControls.length?trackData.timingControls:apexDefaultTimingControls(spec||trackData.spec);
 return src.filter(c=>c&&String(c.role).toLowerCase()==='sector-line').map(c=>{const s=apexTimingControlResolvedS(c);return Number.isFinite(s)?{...c,s}:null}).filter(Boolean).sort((a,b)=>{const na=Number(a.sector),nb=Number(b.sector);if(Number.isFinite(na)&&Number.isFinite(nb)&&na!==nb)return na-nb;return forwardDistanceS(g.finish,a.s,L)-forwardDistanceS(g.finish,b.s,L)});
}
function apexTimingSpeedTrapControls(spec){if(!trackData)return[];const src=Array.isArray(trackData.timingControls)&&trackData.timingControls.length?trackData.timingControls:apexDefaultTimingControls(spec||trackData.spec);return src.filter(c=>c&&String(c.role).toLowerCase()==='speed-trap'&&Number.isFinite(apexTimingControlResolvedS(c))).map(c=>({...c,s:apexTimingControlResolvedS(c)}));}
function apexTimingPointControls(spec){
 if(!trackData)return[];const L=trackData.km*1000,g=apexRaceTimingGeometry(spec||trackData.spec,1),src=Array.isArray(trackData.timingControls)&&trackData.timingControls.length?trackData.timingControls:apexDefaultTimingControls(spec||trackData.spec),roles=new Set(['start-line','finish-line','sector-line','speed-trap']);
 return src.filter(c=>c&&roles.has(String(c.role||'').toLowerCase())).map(c=>{const ss=apexTimingControlResolvedS(c);return Number.isFinite(ss)?{...c,s:ss,dFromFinish:forwardDistanceS(g.finish,ss,L)}:null}).filter(Boolean).sort((a,b)=>a.dFromFinish-b.dFromFinish);
}
function apexTimingSectorsFromControls(spec){
 if(!trackData)return[];const L=trackData.km*1000,g=apexRaceTimingGeometry(spec||trackData.spec,1),sectorCtrls=apexTimingSectorControls(spec||trackData.spec),f=s=>Number((forwardDistanceS(g.finish,s,L)/L).toFixed(7));
 const s1=sectorCtrls.find(c=>String(c.sector)==='1')||sectorCtrls[0],s2=sectorCtrls.find(c=>String(c.sector)==='2')||sectorCtrls[1];if(!s1||!s2)return[];let f1=f(s1.s),f2=f(s2.s);if(f2<=f1)f2=Math.min(.9999999,f2+1);
 return [{n:1,from:0,to:f1,endControlId:s1.id||'sector-1-control',endS:Number(s1.s.toFixed(3))},{n:2,from:f1,to:f2,endControlId:s2.id||'sector-2-control',endS:Number(s2.s.toFixed(3))},{n:3,from:f2,to:1,endControlId:'finish-line',endS:Number(g.finish.toFixed(3))}];
}
window.apexTimingPointControls=()=>apexTimingPointControls(trackData&&trackData.spec);

function apexRuntimeTimingControlsSvg(spec,root){
 if(!trackData)return'';const out=[],existing=trackData&&Array.isArray(trackData.timingControls)?trackData.timingControls:[],defs=existing.length?existing:apexDefaultTimingControls(spec);
 for(const c of defs){if(!c||!c.role||!Number.isFinite(c.s))continue;const role=String(c.role).toLowerCase();if(role==='start-line'||role==='finish-line')continue;if(apexExportHasRole(root,role))continue;
   let extra='';if(role==='sector-line'&&c.sector!=null)extra=`data-apex-sector="${escapeXml(c.sector)}"`;if(c.zone!=null)extra+=` data-apex-zone="${escapeXml(c.zone)}"`;
   out.push(apexRuntimeCrossMarkSvg(role,c.s,c.name||role,extra));
 }
 return out.join('\n    ');
}
function apexVerticalProfileForExport(){
 if(!trackData)return null;const L=trackData.km*1000,n=Math.max(64,Math.ceil(L/10)),samples=[],datum=elevationAt(0);
 const rn=(v,d=4)=>Number(Number(v||0).toFixed(d));
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const f=i/n,s=f*L;samples.push([rn(f,7),rn(elevationAt(s)-datum,4)])}
 return{schema:'APEX_VERTICAL_PROFILE_V1',reference:'START_FINISH_ZERO',normalizeToZero:true,spacingM:rn(L/n,4),fields:['lapFraction','elevationM'],samples};
}
function apexNormalizeAuthoredBanking(cfg){
 const src=cfg&&cfg.banking&&Array.isArray(cfg.banking.corners)?cfg.banking.corners:(Array.isArray(cfg&&cfg.banking)?cfg.banking:null);
 if(!src)return null;
 const corners=src.map(c=>({turn:parseInt(c.turn)||0,name:String(c.name||`T${parseInt(c.turn)||''}`),bankDeg:Number(c.bankDeg)||0,direction:String(c.direction||''),s:Number.isFinite(+c.s)?+c.s:null,profile:Array.isArray(c.profile)?c.profile.filter(p=>Array.isArray(p)&&p.length>=2&&Number.isFinite(+p[0])&&Number.isFinite(+p[1])).map(p=>[+p[0],+p[1]]).sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]):null})).filter(c=>c.turn>0&&Number.isFinite(c.bankDeg));
 return corners.length?corners:null;
}
function apexCircuitMetadataForExport(spec){
 if(!trackData)return{};const sf=apexCircuitStartFinishS(spec),L=trackData.km*1000,ctrls=(trackData.timingControls&&trackData.timingControls.length?trackData.timingControls:apexDefaultTimingControls(spec)).map(c=>({role:c.role,s:Number(Number(c.s||0).toFixed(3)),f:Number((mod(c.s||0,L)/L).toFixed(7)),sector:c.sector??null,zone:c.zone??null,name:c.name||''}));
 let sectors=apexTimingSectorsFromControls(spec);
 if(!sectors.length&&trackData.timingData&&Array.isArray(trackData.timingData.sectors))sectors=trackData.timingData.sectors.map(x=>({...x}));

 const corners=apexDetectedCornerMarkersForExport().map(c=>{const r=apexCornerRegionForTurn(c.turn);return{turn:+c.turn||0,s:Number(mod(+c.s||0,L).toFixed(3)),f:Number((mod(+c.s||0,L)/L).toFixed(7)),name:String(c.name||`T${c.turn||''}`),direction:String(c.direction||((curvatureAt(c.s)>=0)?'LEFT':'RIGHT')),startS:r?Number(r.startS.toFixed(3)):null,endS:r?Number(r.endS.toFixed(3)):null,lengthM:r?Number(r.lengthM.toFixed(2)):null,reference:'MID'};});return{schema:'APEX_CIRCUIT_METADATA_V1',appVersion:APP_VERSION,circuit:{dir:spec&&spec.dir||'',name:spec&&spec.name||'',km:trackData.km,direction:trackData.direction,layout:trackData.layout||'',turns:trackData.turns||0,laps:trackData.laps||0},timing:{startS:Number(sf.start.toFixed(3)),finishS:Number(sf.finish.toFixed(3)),sectors,controls:ctrls,drsMode:ctrls.some(c=>String(c.role).startsWith('drs-'))?'AUTHORED_SVG':'DYNAMIC_RACECRAFT_NO_AUTHORED_ZONES'},corners,verticalProfile:apexVerticalProfileForExport()};
}
function apexWriteCircuitMetadata(doc,root,spec){
 const old=root.querySelector('#APEX_CIRCUIT_METADATA');if(old)old.remove();const ns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg',m=doc.createElementNS(ns,'metadata');m.setAttribute('id','APEX_CIRCUIT_METADATA');m.setAttribute('data-apex-metadata-schema','APEX_CIRCUIT_METADATA_V1');m.textContent=JSON.stringify(apexCircuitMetadataForExport(spec));
 const defs=root.querySelector('defs');if(defs&&defs.nextSibling)root.insertBefore(m,defs.nextSibling);else root.insertBefore(m,root.firstChild);return m;
}
function apexSignedArea2D(points){let a=0;if(!Array.isArray(points)||points.length<3)return 0;for(let i=0,j=points.length-1;i<points.length;j=i++)a+=points[j][0]*points[i][1]-points[i][0]*points[j][1];return a*.5}
function apexOrientSvgRing(points,sign=1){const p=(points||[]).map(q=>[+q[0],+q[1]]);if(p.length<3)return p;const a=apexSignedArea2D(p);if((sign>0&&a<0)||(sign<0&&a>0))p.reverse();return p}
function apexRingPathD(points){const p=points||[];if(p.length<3)return'';return `M ${svgNum(p[0][0])} ${svgNum(p[0][1])} `+p.slice(1).map(q=>`L ${svgNum(q[0])} ${svgNum(q[1])}`).join(' ')+' Z'}
function apexPitCorridorSvgRings(){
 const pn=trackData&&trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork;if(!pn||!Array.isArray(pn.runs)||!pn.runs.length)return[];const rings=[];
 for(const run of pn.runs){const pts=run&&Array.isArray(run.points)?run.points:[];if(pts.length<2)continue;const half=Math.max(2.2,(+run.width||+pn.width||5.9)*.5+.75),L=[],R=[];
  for(let i=0;i<pts.length;i++){const a=pts[Math.max(0,i-1)],b=pts[Math.min(pts.length-1,i+1)],dx=b.x-a.x,dz=b.z-a.z,ll=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1,nx=-dz/ll,nz=dx/ll,p=pts[i];L.push([p.x+nx*half,-(p.z+nz*half)]);R.push([p.x-nx*half,-(p.z-nz*half)])}
  const ring=L.concat(R.reverse());if(ring.length>=4)rings.push(ring)
 }
 return rings;
}
function apexRuntimeDrivableAreaSvg(){
 if(!trackData)return'';const Lm=trackData.km*1000,step=18,left=[],right=[];
 for(let s=0;s<Lm;s+=step){const q=poseAt(s),e=envProfile(s),half=Math.max(2.25,trackRoadWidthAt(s)*.5)+Math.max(0,+e.curb||0)+Math.max(0,+e.runoff||0);left.push([q.x+q.nx*half,-(q.z+q.nz*half)]);right.push([q.x-q.nx*half,-(q.z-q.nz*half)])}
 if(left.length<8||right.length<8)return'';let outer=Math.abs(apexSignedArea2D(left))>=Math.abs(apexSignedArea2D(right))?left:right,inner=outer===left?right:left;outer=apexOrientSvgRing(outer,1);inner=apexOrientSvgRing(inner,-1);
 const parts=[apexRingPathD(outer),apexRingPathD(inner)];for(let ring of apexPitCorridorSvgRings())parts.push(apexRingPathD(apexOrientSvgRing(ring,1)));
 return `<path id="apex_drivable_area" data-apex-generated="runtime" data-apex-type="drivableArea" data-apex-role="drivable-area" data-apex-name="Área transitable · pista + escapatorias + pit lane" fill-rule="nonzero" class="drivable-area" d="${parts.filter(Boolean).join(' ')}"/>`;
}

// v5.230.19 · Exportación topográfica por POLÍGONOS de nivel, con cierre por esquinas y relleno climático.
// Cada polígono es una "placa de curva de nivel": APEX usa EXCLUSIVAMENTE su contorno.
// El relleno no tiene significado altimétrico y se exporta como fill:none. Al importar,
// APEX interpola entre contornos y genera internamente la malla 3D continua.
function apexTerrainContourExportGrid(){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.pts||trackData.pts.length<3)return null;
 let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minZ=Infinity,maxZ=-Infinity;for(const p of trackData.pts){minX=Math.min(minX,p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p[0]);minZ=Math.min(minZ,p[1]);maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,p[1])}
 const margin=115;minX-=margin;maxX+=margin;minZ-=margin;maxZ+=margin;
 let cell=28,nx=Math.max(3,Math.ceil((maxX-minX)/cell)+1),nz=Math.max(3,Math.ceil((maxZ-minZ)/cell)+1),nodes=nx*nz;if(nodes>7200){cell*=Math.sqrt(nodes/7200);nx=Math.max(3,Math.ceil((maxX-minX)/cell)+1);nz=Math.max(3,Math.ceil((maxZ-minZ)/cell)+1)}
 const vals=new Float32Array(nx*nz);let minY=Infinity,maxY=-Infinity;
 for(let iz=0;iz<nz;iz++)for(let ix=0;ix<nx;ix++){const x=Math.min(maxX,minX+ix*cell),z=Math.min(maxZ,minZ+iz*cell),y=apexProceduralTerrainHeightAtWorld(x,z)-elevationAt(0);vals[iz*nx+ix]=y;minY=Math.min(minY,y);maxY=Math.max(maxY,y)}
 return{minX,maxX,minZ,maxZ,cell,nx,nz,vals,minY,maxY};
}
function apexTerrainContourLevels(grid){
 if(!grid||!Number.isFinite(grid.minY)||!Number.isFinite(grid.maxY))return[];const range=Math.max(.1,grid.maxY-grid.minY);let step=range<12?2:(range>55?10:5);if(range>28&&range<=55)step=5;
 let lo=Math.ceil(grid.minY/step)*step,hi=Math.floor(grid.maxY/step)*step,out=[];for(let v=lo;v<=hi+1e-6;v+=step)out.push(Number(v.toFixed(3)));
 if(out.length<3){step=Math.max(1,range/4);out=[];for(let i=1;i<=3;i++)out.push(Number((grid.minY+range*i/4).toFixed(2)))}
 return out.slice(0,14);
}
function apexContourEdgePoint(level,x0,z0,v0,x1,z1,v1){const d=v1-v0,t=Math.abs(d)<1e-9?.5:clamp((level-v0)/d,0,1);return[x0+(x1-x0)*t,z0+(z1-z0)*t]}
function apexTerrainMarchingSegments(grid,level){
 const segs=[],{nx,nz,cell,minX,minZ,vals}=grid,edgePt=(ix,iz,e)=>{const x=minX+ix*cell,z=minZ+iz*cell,i=iz*nx+ix,v0=vals[i],v1=vals[i+1],v3=vals[i+nx],v2=vals[i+nx+1];if(e===0)return apexContourEdgePoint(level,x,z,v0,x+cell,z,v1);if(e===1)return apexContourEdgePoint(level,x+cell,z,v1,x+cell,z+cell,v2);if(e===2)return apexContourEdgePoint(level,x+cell,z+cell,v2,x,z+cell,v3);return apexContourEdgePoint(level,x,z+cell,v3,x,z,v0)};
 for(let iz=0;iz<nz-1;iz++)for(let ix=0;ix<nx-1;ix++){
   const i=iz*nx+ix,v=[vals[i],vals[i+1],vals[i+nx+1],vals[i+nx]],cross=[];for(let e=0;e<4;e++){const a=v[e],b=v[(e+1)%4];if((a<level&&b>=level)||(a>=level&&b<level))cross.push(e)}
   if(cross.length===2)segs.push([edgePt(ix,iz,cross[0]),edgePt(ix,iz,cross[1])]);
   else if(cross.length===4){const center=(v[0]+v[1]+v[2]+v[3])*.25;if(center>=level){segs.push([edgePt(ix,iz,0),edgePt(ix,iz,3)],[edgePt(ix,iz,1),edgePt(ix,iz,2)])}else{segs.push([edgePt(ix,iz,0),edgePt(ix,iz,1)],[edgePt(ix,iz,2),edgePt(ix,iz,3)])}}
 }
 return segs;
}
function apexStitchContourSegments(segs){
 const key=p=>Math.round(p[0]*10)+','+Math.round(p[1]*10),adj=new Map(),used=new Uint8Array(segs.length);for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i++)for(const p of segs[i]){const k=key(p),a=adj.get(k);if(a)a.push(i);else adj.set(k,[i])}
 const runs=[];function walk(seed,startAtA=true){used[seed]=1;let a=segs[seed][startAtA?0:1],b=segs[seed][startAtA?1:0],run=[a,b],guard=0;while(guard++<segs.length+4){const k=key(run[run.length-1]),cand=adj.get(k)||[],next=cand.find(j=>!used[j]);if(next==null)break;used[next]=1;const sg=segs[next],end=run[run.length-1],ka=key(sg[0]),kb=key(sg[1]);run.push(ka===key(end)?sg[1]:sg[0]);}return run}
 for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i++){if(used[i])continue;const da=(adj.get(key(segs[i][0]))||[]).length,db=(adj.get(key(segs[i][1]))||[]).length;runs.push(walk(i,da===1||db!==1))}
 return runs;
}
function apexContourRunLength(run){let L=0;for(let i=1;i<run.length;i++)L+=Math.hypot(run[i][0]-run[i-1][0],run[i][1]-run[i-1][1]);return L}
// v5.230.19 · Paleta topográfica estable por paisaje/clima del CIRCUITO.
// No depende de que la sesión concreta esté seca o lluviosa: el SVG sigue siendo un plano maestro.
function apexTerrainContourPalette(spec=null){
 const sp=spec||(trackData&&trackData.spec)||{},id=String(sp.dir||''),j=readStoredCircuitJson(id)||{},env=j.environment||{},theme=String(env.theme||sp.theme||'').toLowerCase(),tone=String(env.terrainTone||'').toUpperCase();
 const lush=['park','forest','mountain','tropical','cloud'].includes(theme),arid=tone==='BROWN'||['dry','dunes','sunset'].includes(theme);
 if(arid&&!lush)return{tone:'BROWN',fill:'#8f704d',stroke:'#cbb18a',label:'#f1dcc0',labelStroke:'#2d2116',opacity:.28};
 return{tone:'GREEN',fill:'#6f8653',stroke:'#c8d4b9',label:'#d9f4d2',labelStroke:'#132117',opacity:.26};
}
function apexRuntimeTerrainBaseSvg(svgCtx=null){
 const grid=apexTerrainContourExportGrid();if(!grid)return'';const pal=apexTerrainContourPalette();
 const x0=grid.minX,z0=grid.minZ,x1=grid.minX+(grid.nx-1)*grid.cell,z1=grid.minZ+(grid.nz-1)*grid.cell;
 const mapP=p=>svgCtx&&Number.isFinite(svgCtx.scale)&&svgCtx.scale>0?[svgCtx.origin[0]+p[0]/svgCtx.scale,svgCtx.origin[1]-p[1]/svgCtx.scale]:[p[0],-p[1]];
 const pts=[[x0,z0],[x1,z0],[x1,z1],[x0,z1]].map(mapP).map(q=>`${svgNum(q[0])},${svgNum(q[1])}`).join(' ');
 const op=Math.max(.10,Math.min(.22,(pal.opacity||.26)*.58));
 const j=readStoredCircuitJson(trackData&&trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir)||{},terrain=j.terrain||{},baseLevel=Number.isFinite(+terrain.baseLevelM)?+terrain.baseLevelM:0;
 return `<polygon id="apex_terrain_base" data-apex-generated="terrain-base" data-apex-type="terrainZone" data-apex-role="terrain-base" data-apex-name="Terreno base" data-apex-terrain-tone="${pal.tone}" data-apex-base-level-id="level_${baseLevel}" data-apex-base-level-m="${baseLevel}" data-apex-level-reference="START_FINISH_ZERO" class="terrain-base" style="fill:${pal.fill};fill-opacity:${op};stroke:${pal.stroke};stroke-width:1;stroke-dasharray:10 8" points="${pts}"/>`;
}
function apexContourBoundaryInfo(p,grid){
 const x0=grid.minX,z0=grid.minZ,x1=grid.minX+(grid.nx-1)*grid.cell,z1=grid.minZ+(grid.nz-1)*grid.cell,W=x1-x0,H=z1-z0;
 const d=[Math.abs(p[1]-z0),Math.abs(p[0]-x1),Math.abs(p[1]-z1),Math.abs(p[0]-x0)],side=d.indexOf(Math.min(...d));let q,s;
 if(side===0){q=[clamp(p[0],x0,x1),z0];s=q[0]-x0}
 else if(side===1){q=[x1,clamp(p[1],z0,z1)];s=W+(q[1]-z0)}
 else if(side===2){q=[clamp(p[0],x0,x1),z1];s=W+H+(x1-q[0])}
 else{q=[x0,clamp(p[1],z0,z1)];s=2*W+H+(z1-q[1])}
 return{q,s,side,x0,x1,z0,z1,W,H,P:2*(W+H)};
}
function apexContourBoundaryCw(fromInfo,toInfo){
 const {x0,x1,z0,z1,W,H,P}=fromInfo,cornerS=[W,W+H,2*W+H,P],cornerP=[[x1,z0],[x1,z1],[x0,z1],[x0,z0]],out=[];
 let a=fromInfo.s,b=toInfo.s;if(b<a)b+=P;
 for(let i=0;i<cornerS.length;i++)for(const shift of [0,P]){const cs=cornerS[i]+shift;if(cs>a+1e-6&&cs<b-1e-6)out.push({s:cs,p:cornerP[i]})}
 out.sort((u,v)=>u.s-v.s);return out.map(o=>o.p).concat([toInfo.q]);
}
function apexContourCloseWithCorners(run,grid){
 if(!run||run.length<2)return{points:run||[],coreCount:(run||[]).length};
 const a=apexContourBoundaryInfo(run[run.length-1],grid),b=apexContourBoundaryInfo(run[0],grid),cwDist=(b.s-a.s+a.P)%a.P,ccwDist=a.P-cwDist;
 let closure;
 if(cwDist<=ccwDist)closure=apexContourBoundaryCw(a,b);
 else closure=apexContourBoundaryCw(b,a).reverse();
 const pts=run.map(p=>[p[0],p[1]]),last=pts[pts.length-1];
 if(Math.hypot(last[0]-a.q[0],last[1]-a.q[1])>.05)pts.push(a.q);
 for(const p of closure){const q=pts[pts.length-1];if(!q||Math.hypot(q[0]-p[0],q[1]-p[1])>.05)pts.push(p)}
 return{points:pts,coreCount:run.length};
}
function apexRuntimeTerrainContoursSvg(svgCtx=null){
 const grid=apexTerrainContourExportGrid();if(!grid)return'';const levels=apexTerrainContourLevels(grid).slice().sort((a,b)=>a-b),nodes=[];let idx=0;
 const labelFor=v=>'level_'+String(Number(v.toFixed(3))).replace('.', '_');
 for(const level of levels){let runs=apexStitchContourSegments(apexTerrainMarchingSegments(grid,level)).filter(r=>r.length>=3&&apexContourRunLength(r)>=42);runs.sort((a,b)=>apexContourRunLength(b)-apexContourRunLength(a));runs=runs.slice(0,6);let labelled=false;
   for(const run of runs){
     const originalClosed=Math.hypot(run[0][0]-run[run.length-1][0],run[0][1]-run[run.length-1][1])<grid.cell*.8;
     const mapP=p=>svgCtx&&Number.isFinite(svgCtx.scale)&&svgCtx.scale>0?[svgCtx.origin[0]+p[0]/svgCtx.scale,svgCtx.origin[1]-p[1]/svgCtx.scale]:[p[0],-p[1]];
     // En SVG siempre se representa como POLYGON, como una placa de curva de nivel.
     // Si la isolínea sale por el borde, la cerramos siguiendo el MARCO y SUS ESQUINAS,
     // no con una diagonal artificial. data-apex-contour-core-count conserva qué vértices
     // pertenecen al contorno altimétrico real; los de cierre son sólo representación SVG.
     const poly=originalClosed?{points:run,coreCount:run.length}:apexContourCloseWithCorners(run,grid),mapped=poly.points.map(mapP),points=mapped.map(q=>`${svgNum(q[0])},${svgNum(q[1])}`).join(' '),id=labelFor(level)+(idx?'__'+(idx+1):''),pal=apexTerrainContourPalette();idx++;
     nodes.push(`<polygon id="${id}" data-apex-generated="terrain-contour" data-apex-type="terrainContour" data-apex-role="terrain-contour" data-apex-contour-level-id="${labelFor(level)}" data-apex-elevation-m="${svgNum(level,2)}" data-apex-closed="${originalClosed?'true':'false'}" data-apex-contour-open="${originalClosed?'false':'true'}" data-apex-contour-core-count="${poly.coreCount}" data-apex-contour-close-mode="${originalClosed?'natural':'bbox-corners'}" data-apex-terrain-tone="${pal.tone}" data-apex-name="Cota ${svgNum(level,1)} m" class="terrain-contour" style="fill:${pal.fill};fill-opacity:${pal.opacity};stroke:${pal.stroke};stroke-width:1.3;stroke-linejoin:round" points="${points}"/>`);
     if(!labelled&&run.length){const coreMapped=run.map(mapP),m=coreMapped[Math.floor(coreMapped.length*.48)],off=svgCtx&&svgCtx.scale?5/svgCtx.scale:5;nodes.push(`<text data-apex-contour-label="${svgNum(level,2)}" data-apex-contour-level-label="${labelFor(level)}" x="${svgNum(m[0]+off)}" y="${svgNum(m[1]-off)}" class="terrain-contour-label" style="font:800 11px system-ui,sans-serif;fill:${pal.label};stroke:${pal.labelStroke};stroke-width:2.4;paint-order:stroke">${level>=0?'+':''}${svgNum(level,1)} m</text>`);labelled=true}
   }
 }
 return nodes.join('\n    ');
}

function apexRuntimeCurbsSvg(){
 if(!trackData)return'';const L=trackData.km*1000,step=5.5,out=[];
 for(const side of ['left','right']){let run=null,idx=0;const flush=()=>{if(!run||run.length<2){run=null;return;}const d=run.map((p,i)=>`${i?'L':'M'} ${svgNum(p[0],3)} ${svgNum(p[1],3)}`).join(' '),id=`apex_curb_${side}_${idx++}`;out.push(`<g id="${id}_group" data-apex-generated="curb-runtime"><path class="apex-curb-white" style="fill:none;stroke:#f4f5f6;stroke-width:1.35;stroke-linecap:butt;stroke-linejoin:round" d="${d}"/><path id="${id}" data-apex-generated="runtime" data-apex-type="marking" data-apex-family="track" data-apex-role="curb-${side}" data-apex-style="curb_red_white" data-apex-width="1.25" class="apex-curb-red" style="fill:none;stroke:#d92b36;stroke-width:1.35;stroke-dasharray:3.8 3.8;stroke-linecap:butt;stroke-linejoin:round" d="${d}"/></g>`);run=null;};
   let prevOn=false;
   for(let abs=0;abs<=L+step;abs+=step){const ss=mod(abs,L),ci=curbInfo(ss),on=!!ci[side],q=poseAt(ss),e=envProfile(ss),lat=(e.half+e.curb*.52)*(side==='left'?1:-1),pt=[q.x+q.nx*lat,-(q.z+q.nz*lat)];if(on){if(!prevOn)run=[];run.push(pt);}else if(prevOn)flush();prevOn=on;}
   if(prevOn)flush();
 }
 return out.join('\n    ');
}
function apexRuntimeDistanceBoardsSvg(){
 if(!trackData)return'';const L=trackData.km*1000,out=[];let i=0;
 for(const b of mergedBrakeBoardDefinitions()){
   // Los carteles ya autorados ya existen físicamente en el SVG base; no los duplicamos.
   if(b&&b.authored===true)continue;
   const s=mod(b.s,L),q=poseAt(s),env=envProfile(s),side=b.side||1,lat=env.half+env.curb+env.runoff+(b.latExtra||1.1),cx=q.x+q.nx*lat*side,cz=q.z+q.nz*lat*side,w=Math.max(2.2,b.width||2.9),d=1.05;
   const P=(along,lateral)=>[cx+q.tx*along+q.nx*lateral,-(cz+q.tz*along+q.nz*lateral)];
   const pts=[P(-d*.5,-w*.5),P(-d*.5,w*.5),P(d*.5,w*.5),P(d*.5,-w*.5)].map(p=>`${svgNum(p[0])},${svgNum(p[1])}`).join(' ');
   const cornerS=Number.isFinite(b.cornerS)?mod(b.cornerS,L):mod(s+(Number(b.mark)||0),L);
   {const bid=`apex_distance_${i++}`;out.push(`<g id="${bid}"><polygon id="${bid}_shape" data-apex-generated="runtime" data-apex-type="distanceBoard" data-apex-role="distance-board" data-apex-name="Cartel ${escapeXml(b.mark||'')} m" data-apex-mark="${escapeXml(b.mark||100)}" data-apex-s="${svgNum(s,3)}" data-apex-corner-s="${svgNum(cornerS,3)}" data-apex-side="${side}" data-apex-width="${svgNum(w,2)}" data-apex-depth="${svgNum(d,2)}" data-apex-height="${svgNum(b.height||4,2)}" data-apex-lat-extra="${svgNum(b.latExtra||1.1,2)}" class="distance-board" points="${pts}"/><text data-apex-board-label="1" class="distance-board-text" x="${svgNum(cx)}" y="${svgNum(-cz)}">${escapeXml(b.mark||100)}</text></g>`);}
 }
 return out.join('\n    ');
}

// v5.230.16 · Modelo universal de curvas.
// El marcador Tn representa el punto de referencia de la curva (aprox. ápice/mitad del arco).
// APEX NO interpreta ese punto como una curva de longitud cero: analiza la curvatura real a
// ambos lados y construye una zona de curva completa. En curvas largas el marcador puede estar
// en mitad del arco; el peralte y el AUT usan la zona, no un pico puntual.
// v5.230.47 · Geometría Tn y nombres independientes.
// El SVG es autoritativo para s (ubicación); el nombre de banking.corners[].name
// en JSON es autoritativo para la leyenda del mapa, HUD y la exportación SVG.
function apexJsonCornerNameMap(dir){
 const cfg=readStoredCircuitJson(dir)||{},bank=cfg.banking||{};
 const source=Array.isArray(cfg.corners)&&cfg.corners.length?cfg.corners:(Array.isArray(bank.corners)?bank.corners:[]);
 const names=new Map();
 for(const c of source){const n=+c.turn||0;if(n>0&&typeof c.name==='string'&&c.name.trim())names.set(n,c.name.trim());}
 return names;
}
function apexNamedCornerMarkers(markers,dir){
 const names=apexJsonCornerNameMap(dir);
 return (markers||[]).map(c=>({...c,name:names.get(+c.turn||0)||String(c.name||`T${c.turn}`)}));
}
function apexStoredCornerMarkers(){
 if(!trackData)return[];const L=trackData.km*1000;
 const src=(Array.isArray(trackData.cornerMarkers)&&trackData.cornerMarkers.length?trackData.cornerMarkers:(trackData.customWorld&&Array.isArray(trackData.customWorld.cornerMarkers)?trackData.customWorld.cornerMarkers:[]));
 if(src.length)return apexNamedCornerMarkers(src,trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir).map((c,i)=>({turn:+c.turn||i+1,s:mod(+c.s||0,L),name:String(c.name||`T${+c.turn||i+1}`),direction:c.direction||null,authored:true})).sort((a,b)=>a.turn-b.turn);
 return[];
}
function apexAutoCornerSeeds(target){
 if(!trackData)return[];const L=trackData.km*1000,step=clamp(L/(Math.max(1,target)*70),4.0,8.0),raw=[];
 for(let ss=0;ss<L;ss+=step){
   const k0=Math.abs(curvatureAt(ss-step)),k1=Math.abs(curvatureAt(ss)),k2=Math.abs(curvatureAt(ss+step));
   if(k1<.00034||k1<k0||k1<k2)continue;
   const safe=cornerSafeSpeed(ss),score=k1*(1+clamp((82-safe)/85,0,.85));raw.push({s:ss,k:curvatureAt(ss),score});
 }
 raw.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);let picked=[];
 for(let sep=clamp(L/(target*4.8),34,88);sep>=16&&picked.length<target;sep*=.78){
   picked=[];for(const c of raw){if(picked.every(p=>Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(c.s,p.s,L))>=sep)){picked.push(c);if(picked.length>=target)break}}
 }
 if(picked.length<target){
   picked=[];for(let i=0;i<target;i++){const a=L*i/target,b=L*(i+1)/target;let best={s:(a+b)*.5,score:-1,k:0};for(let ss=a;ss<b;ss+=step){const k=Math.abs(curvatureAt(ss));if(k>best.score){best={s:ss,score:k,k:curvatureAt(ss)}}}picked.push(best)}
 }
 return picked.slice(0,target).sort((a,b)=>a.s-b.s).map((c,i)=>({turn:i+1,s:mod(c.s,L),name:`T${i+1}`,direction:c.k>=0?'LEFT':'RIGHT',authored:false}));
}
function apexCornerRegionFromMarker(marker,prev,next){
 if(!trackData||!marker)return null;const L=trackData.km*1000,s0=mod(+marker.s||0,L),step=4;
 const prevGap=prev?mod(s0-prev.s,L):L/Math.max(2,trackData.turns||20),nextGap=next?mod(next.s-s0,L):prevGap;
 const limL=clamp(prevGap*.46,28,650),limR=clamp(nextGap*.46,28,650),probe=Math.min(54,limL*.45,limR*.45);
 let signed=0,weight=0,peak=0;
 for(let d=-probe;d<=probe;d+=step){const k=curvatureAt(s0+d),a=Math.abs(k),w=.35+.65*(1-Math.abs(d)/(probe+step));signed+=Math.sign(k)*a*w;weight+=a*w;peak=Math.max(peak,a)}
 let sign=Math.sign(signed)||(Math.sign(curvatureAt(s0))||1);if(weight<1e-6)sign=Math.sign(curvatureAt(s0))||1;
 const threshold=clamp(peak*.18,.00020,.00105),soft=threshold*.55;
 function scan(dir,limit){let lastGood=0,bad=0;for(let d=step;d<=limit;d+=step){const k=curvatureAt(s0+dir*d),a=Math.abs(k),same=sign*k>=0;if(same&&a>=threshold){lastGood=d;bad=0}else if(same&&a>=soft){lastGood=d;bad=Math.max(0,bad-step*.35)}else{bad+=step;if(!same&&a>=threshold*.85)bad+=step*1.5;if(bad>=20)break}}return Math.max(12,lastGood)} let left=scan(-1,limL),right=scan(1,limR);
 // Para marcadores automáticos, el punto editorial debe quedar aproximadamente en mitad del arco.
 // Para marcadores autorados por el usuario NO se mueve el punto: sólo se deducen sus límites.
 let center=s0;
 if(marker.authored===false){const delta=(right-left)*.5;center=mod(s0+delta,L);left=Math.max(12,left+delta);right=Math.max(12,right-delta)}
 const length=Math.max(24,left+right),dir=sign>=0?'LEFT':'RIGHT';
 return{turn:+marker.turn||0,name:String(marker.name||`T${marker.turn||''}`),centerS:center,startS:mod(center-left,L),endS:mod(center+right,L),entryM:left,exitM:right,lengthM:length,direction:dir,curveSign:sign,peakK:peak,thresholdK:threshold,authored:marker.authored!==false};
}
function apexPrepareCornerReferenceModel(){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.model)return[];const target=Math.max(1,parseInt(trackData.turns)||parseInt((raceMetaFor(trackData.spec)||{}).turns)||1),L=trackData.km*1000;
 let marks=apexStoredCornerMarkers();if(!marks.length)marks=apexAutoCornerSeeds(target);
 marks=marks.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.turn-b.turn);
 // Orden físico para encontrar vecinos sin confundir una curva con la siguiente.
 const physical=marks.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.s-b.s),regions=[];
 for(let i=0;i<physical.length;i++){const m=physical[i],prev=physical[(i-1+physical.length)%physical.length],next=physical[(i+1)%physical.length],r=apexCornerRegionFromMarker(m,prev,next);if(r)regions.push(r)}
 regions.sort((a,b)=>a.turn-b.turn);
 const byTurn=new Map(regions.map(r=>[r.turn,r]));
 trackData.cornerMarkers=marks.map(m=>{const r=byTurn.get(+m.turn||0);return{...m,s:r?r.centerS:mod(m.s,L),direction:r?r.direction:m.direction||null}}).sort((a,b)=>a.turn-b.turn);
 trackData.cornerRegions=regions;
 // Lookup precalculado: evita recorrer todas las curvas desde bankAt/render cada frame.
 const n=trackData.model.curve.length,idx=new Int16Array(n),gain=new Float32Array(n);idx.fill(-1);
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const ss=i*trackData.model.step;let best=-1,bg=0;for(let j=0;j<regions.length;j++){const r=regions[j],u=mod(ss-r.startS,L);if(u>r.lengthM)continue;const ramp=Math.min(r.lengthM*.22,Math.max(14,Math.min(55,r.lengthM*.18))),g=ramp>0?(u<ramp?profileSmooth01(u/ramp):(u>r.lengthM-ramp?profileSmooth01((r.lengthM-u)/ramp):1)):1;if(g>bg){bg=g;best=j}}idx[i]=best;gain[i]=bg}
 trackData.model.cornerIndex=idx;trackData.model.cornerGain=gain;return regions;
}
function apexCornerRegionForTurn(turn){return trackData&&Array.isArray(trackData.cornerRegions)?trackData.cornerRegions.find(r=>+r.turn===+turn)||null:null}
// Continuous reference: no quantized lookup and no curvature-sign flips within a named turn.
function apexContinuousCornerReference(s,r){
 const L=trackData.km*1000,u=mod(s-r.startS,L),len=r.lengthM;
 if(!(len>0)||u>len)return{region:null,gain:0};
 const ramp=Math.min(len*.22,Math.max(14,Math.min(55,len*.18)));
 const gain=profileSmooth01(Math.min(1,u/ramp,(len-u)/ramp));
 return{region:r,gain};
}
function apexCornerReferenceAtS(s){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.model||!trackData.model.cornerIndex)return{region:null,gain:0};
 const m=trackData.model,n=m.cornerIndex.length,L=trackData.km*1000,f=mod(s,L)/m.step,i=Math.floor(f)%n;
 const candidates=new Set([m.cornerIndex[i],m.cornerIndex[mod(i+1,n)],m.cornerIndex[mod(i-1,n)]]);
 let best={region:null,gain:0};
 for(const j of candidates){const r=j>=0&&trackData.cornerRegions?trackData.cornerRegions[j]:null;if(r){const v=apexContinuousCornerReference(s,r);if(v.region&&v.gain>=best.gain)best=v;}}
 return best;
}
function apexDetectedCornerMarkersForExport(){
 if(!trackData)return[];if(!Array.isArray(trackData.cornerRegions)||!trackData.cornerRegions.length)apexPrepareCornerReferenceModel();
 return (trackData.cornerMarkers||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.turn||0)-(b.turn||0));
}
function apexRuntimeCornerMarkersSvg(){
 if(!trackData)return'';const marks=apexDetectedCornerMarkersForExport(),out=[];
 for(const c of marks){
   const s=mod(c.s,trackData.km*1000),q=poseAt(s),e=envProfile(s),k=curvatureAt(s),side=k>=0?-1:1,lat=(e.half||6.2)+(e.curb||0)+(e.runoff||0)+9,
         x=q.x+q.nx*lat*side,y=-(q.z+q.nz*lat*side),name=String(c.name||`T${c.turn}`),turn=+c.turn||1,label=`T${turn}`;
   const r=apexCornerRegionForTurn(turn),dir=r?r.direction:(k>=0?'LEFT':'RIGHT'),regionAttrs=r?` data-apex-reference="MID" data-apex-start-s="${svgNum(r.startS,3)}" data-apex-end-s="${svgNum(r.endS,3)}" data-apex-length-m="${svgNum(r.lengthM,2)}"`:'';out.push(`<g id="apex_corner_${turn}" data-apex-corner-group="${turn}"><circle data-apex-generated="corner-marker" data-apex-type="cornerMarker" data-apex-family="timing" data-apex-role="corner-marker" data-apex-turn="${turn}" data-apex-name="${escapeXml(name)}" data-apex-s="${svgNum(s,3)}" data-apex-direction="${dir}"${regionAttrs} cx="${svgNum(x)}" cy="${svgNum(y)}" r="5.4" style="fill:#ffcf45;stroke:#15191e;stroke-width:1.8"/><text data-apex-corner-number="${turn}" class="apex-corner-number" x="${svgNum(x+7)}" y="${svgNum(y-7)}">${label}</text></g>`);
 }
 return `<g id="APEX_CORNERS" data-apex-layer="corners">${out.join('')}</g>`;
}
function apexRuntimePitLaneSvg(){
 if(!trackData)return'';const pn=trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork;if(!pn||!Array.isArray(pn.runs)||!pn.runs.length)return'';
 const generated=pn.generated===true||pn.source==='PROCEDURAL_FALLBACK',nodes=[];let i=0;
 for(const run of pn.runs){
   const pts=Array.isArray(run.points)?run.points:[];if(pts.length<2)continue;
   const d=pts.map((p,j)=>`${j?'L':'M'} ${svgNum(p.x)} ${svgNum(-p.z)}`).join(' '),width=Math.max(3.8,Number(run.width)||5.9);
   nodes.push(`<path id="apex_pitlane_${i++}" data-apex-type="pitLane" data-apex-role="pitlane-centerline" data-apex-width="${svgNum(width,2)}" data-apex-speed-kph="${svgNum(apexPitSpeedLimitKph(),0)}" data-apex-source="${generated?'APEX_PROVISIONAL':'APEX_RUNTIME'}" data-apex-visual="ASPHALT_GUIDE"${generated?' data-apex-generated="pit-fallback"':''} class="pit-line" d="${d}"/>`);
 }
 return nodes.join('\n    ');
}

function apexEnrichExportSvg(svgText,spec){
 let terrainExportCtx=null;
 try{
   const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString(String(svgText||''),'image/svg+xml'),root=doc.documentElement;if(!root||root.nodeName==='parsererror')return svgText;
   try{terrainExportCtx=apexSvgTrackContext(doc,spec)}catch(e){terrainExportCtx=null}
   // v5.230.15: NO borramos APEX_RUNTIME_EXPORT. Una vez exportado e importado, esos
   // elementos ya son parte del SVG autoritativo y deben sobrevivir a exportaciones futuras.
   const pieces=[],backPieces=[],sf=apexCircuitStartFinishS(spec);
   if(!apexExportHasRole(root,'finish-line'))pieces.push(apexRuntimeCrossMarkSvg('finish-line',sf.finish,'Línea de meta'));
   if(!apexExportHasRole(root,'start-line'))pieces.push(apexRuntimeCrossMarkSvg('start-line',sf.start,'Línea de salida'));
   const timing=apexRuntimeTimingControlsSvg(spec,root);if(timing)pieces.push(timing);
   if(!apexExportHasRole(root,'curb-left')&&!apexExportHasRole(root,'curb-right')){const x=apexRuntimeCurbsSvg();if(x)pieces.push(x)}
   // PIT: SVG si existe; fallback provisional sólo si falta.
   if(!apexExportHasRole(root,'pitlane-centerline')){const x=apexRuntimePitLaneSvg();if(x)pieces.push(x)}
   // v5.230.26: una única geometría lógica define TODO el suelo transitable, incluida la red PIT.
   // Las surfaceZone sólo describen el material (asfalto/grava/césped/arena), no el permiso de circulación.
   const hasDrivable=apexExportHasRole(root,'drivable-area'),drivableAuthoritative=String(root.getAttribute('data-apex-drivable-area-authoritative')||'').toLowerCase()==='true';
   if(!hasDrivable&&!drivableAuthoritative){const x=apexRuntimeDrivableAreaSvg();if(x){pieces.push(x);root.setAttribute('data-apex-drivable-area-authoritative','true')}}
   if(hasDrivable&&!drivableAuthoritative)root.setAttribute('data-apex-drivable-area-authoritative','true');
   // Terreno v5.230.19: el SVG ya NO recibe cientos de placas de render. Exportamos
   // curvas de nivel editables. La línea/contorno y data-apex-elevation-m son la fuente
   // de verdad; el relleno de una eventual placa se ignora al reimportar.
   const hasTerrainBase=apexExportHasRole(root,r=>r==='terrain-base'),terrainBaseAuthoritative=String(root.getAttribute('data-apex-terrain-base-authoritative')||'').toLowerCase()==='true';
   if(!hasTerrainBase&&!terrainBaseAuthoritative){const b=apexRuntimeTerrainBaseSvg(terrainExportCtx);if(b){backPieces.push(b);root.setAttribute('data-apex-terrain-base-authoritative','true')}}
   if(hasTerrainBase&&!terrainBaseAuthoritative)root.setAttribute('data-apex-terrain-base-authoritative','true');
   const hasTerrainContours=apexExportHasRole(root,r=>r==='terrain-contour'||r==='terrain-plate')||[...root.querySelectorAll('[data-apex-role="terrain-zone"][data-apex-elevation-m]')].some(el=>!(el.closest&&el.closest('[data-apex-library="1"],#APEX_ELEMENT_LIBRARY'))),terrainContoursAuthoritative=String(root.getAttribute('data-apex-terrain-contours-authoritative')||'').toLowerCase()==='true';
   if(!hasTerrainContours&&!terrainContoursAuthoritative){
     const oldTerrainGroup=root.querySelector('#APEX_TERRAIN_EXPORT');if(oldTerrainGroup&&String(oldTerrainGroup.getAttribute('data-apex-generated')||'')==='materialized-export')oldTerrainGroup.remove();
     for(const el of [...root.querySelectorAll('[data-apex-role="terrain-zone"][data-apex-generated="runtime"]')])if(String(el.id||'').startsWith('apex_terrain_'))el.remove();
     const x=apexRuntimeTerrainContoursSvg(terrainExportCtx);if(x){backPieces.push(x);root.setAttribute('data-apex-terrain-reference','LOCAL_ABSOLUTE');root.setAttribute('data-apex-elevation-zero','START_FINISH');root.setAttribute('data-apex-terrain-complete','true');root.setAttribute('data-apex-terrain-margin-m','115');root.setAttribute('data-apex-terrain-model','CONTOUR_POLYGONS_V3+BASE');root.setAttribute('data-apex-terrain-contours-authoritative','true')}
   }
   if(hasTerrainContours&&!terrainContoursAuthoritative)root.setAttribute('data-apex-terrain-contours-authoritative','true');
   // Carteles: la primera exportación materializa exactamente los que APEX muestra.
   // Tras reimportar, el SVG queda autoritativo: si el usuario borra uno, no reaparece.
   const boardsAuthoritative=String(root.getAttribute('data-apex-brake-boards-authoritative')||'').toLowerCase()==='true';
   if(!boardsAuthoritative){const boards=apexRuntimeDistanceBoardsSvg();if(boards)pieces.push(boards);root.setAttribute('data-apex-brake-boards-authoritative','true')}
   // Curvas T1..Tn: referencias editables para poder renombrarlas y, más adelante,
   // anclar peraltes/altimetría a una curva en vez de a una fracción antigua de vuelta.
   if(!apexExportHasRole(root,'corner-marker')){const c=apexRuntimeCornerMarkersSvg();if(c)pieces.push(c);root.setAttribute('data-apex-corners-authoritative','true')}
   const appendFragments=(xml,id,behind=false)=>{if(!xml.length)return;let g=root.querySelector('#'+id);if(!g){const frag=new DOMParser().parseFromString(`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><g id="${id}" data-apex-generated="materialized-export"></g></svg>`,'image/svg+xml');g=doc.importNode(frag.documentElement.firstElementChild,true);if(behind){const defs=root.querySelector('defs');if(defs&&defs.nextSibling)root.insertBefore(g,defs.nextSibling);else root.insertBefore(g,root.firstChild)}else root.appendChild(g)}const frag=new DOMParser().parseFromString(`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><g>${xml.join('\n')}</g></svg>`,'image/svg+xml');for(const ch of [...frag.documentElement.firstElementChild.children])g.appendChild(doc.importNode(ch,true));};
   appendFragments(backPieces,'APEX_TERRAIN_CONTOURS',true);appendFragments(pieces,'APEX_RUNTIME_EXPORT',false);
   // Nombres en metadatos + número Tn siempre visible en el SVG.
   const exportNames=apexJsonCornerNameMap(spec.dir),svgNs='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
   for(const marker of root.querySelectorAll('[data-apex-role="corner-marker"]')){
     const turn=+marker.getAttribute('data-apex-turn')||0;if(!turn)continue;const name=exportNames.get(turn);if(name)marker.setAttribute('data-apex-name',name);
     const group=marker.parentElement||root;let text=group.querySelector(`[data-apex-corner-number="${turn}"]`)||group.querySelector(`[data-apex-corner-label="${turn}"]`);
     if(!text){text=doc.createElementNS(svgNs,'text');text.setAttribute('data-apex-corner-number',String(turn));text.setAttribute('class','apex-corner-number');const cx=parseFloat(marker.getAttribute('cx')),cy=parseFloat(marker.getAttribute('cy'));if(Number.isFinite(cx))text.setAttribute('x',svgNum(cx+7));if(Number.isFinite(cy))text.setAttribute('y',svgNum(cy-7));group.appendChild(text)}
     text.textContent=`T${turn}`;
   }
   apexWriteCircuitMetadata(doc,root,spec);
   apexSvgApplyUniversalSchema(root);
   root.setAttribute('data-apex-export','COMPLETE_CIRCUIT_V5');root.setAttribute('data-apex-export-version','v5.230.62.8.6.7.6');root.setAttribute('data-apex-library-version','5');root.setAttribute('data-apex-metadata-version','2');
   const out=`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n${new XMLSerializer().serializeToString(root)}`;if(terrainExportCtx&&terrainExportCtx.root&&terrainExportCtx.root.isConnected)terrainExportCtx.root.remove();return out;
 }catch(e){if(terrainExportCtx&&terrainExportCtx.root&&terrainExportCtx.root.isConnected)terrainExportCtx.root.remove();return svgText}
}
function svgNodeWorldCenter(el){
 if(!el)return null;
 const tag=(el.tagName||'').toLowerCase();
 if(tag==='circle'||tag==='ellipse')return{x:parseFloat(el.getAttribute('cx'))||0,z:-(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('cy'))||0)};
 if(tag==='rect')return{x:(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('x'))||0)+(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('width'))||0)/2,z:-((parseFloat(el.getAttribute('y'))||0)+(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('height'))||0)/2)};
 if(tag==='line')return{x:((parseFloat(el.getAttribute('x1'))||0)+(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('x2'))||0))/2,z:-(((parseFloat(el.getAttribute('y1'))||0)+(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('y2'))||0))/2)};
 const pts=sampleSvgNodeWorldPoints(el);if(pts.length){let sx=0,sz=0;for(const p of pts){sx+=p[0];sz+=p[1]}return{x:sx/pts.length,z:sz/pts.length}}
 return null;
}
function svgNodeWorldFootprint(el){
 const tag=(el.tagName||'').toLowerCase();
 if(tag==='polygon'||tag==='polyline')return parseSvgPointsAttr(el.getAttribute('points'));
 if(tag==='rect'){const x=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('x'))||0,y=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('y'))||0,w=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('width'))||0,h=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('height'))||0;return [[x,-y],[x+w,-y],[x+w,-(y+h)],[x,-(y+h)]]}
 return null;
}
function parseImportedLandmarkNode(el,index){
 const sourceRole=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-role')||'').trim(),rawType=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-type')||roleNameToImportType(sourceRole)||'').trim();if(!rawType)return null;
 const center=svgNodeWorldCenter(el);if(!center)return null;const st=nearestTrackState(center.x,center.z,null),sideAttr=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-side'));let side=Number.isFinite(sideAttr)&&sideAttr!==0?Math.sign(sideAttr):Math.sign(st.lat)||1;
 const anchor=proposalWorldAnchor(st.s,st.lat,0,parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-maxds'))||1750);anchor.x=center.x;anchor.z=center.z;anchor.groundQ=poseAt(st.s);
 const type=(rawType==='building')?'block':rawType,node={type,role:rawType,anchor,name:String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-name')||`${rawType} ${index+1}`),side,height:parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-height'))||0,color:el.getAttribute('data-apex-color')||undefined};
 const foot=svgNodeWorldFootprint(el),pathPts=sampleSvgNodeWorldPoints(el),wAttr=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-width'))||0,dAttr=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-depth'))||0,lAttr=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-length'))||0;
 if(type==='grandstand'||type==='pit'){node.length=lAttr||parseFloat(el.getAttribute('width'))||60;node.depth=dAttr||parseFloat(el.getAttribute('height'))||16;node.height=node.height||((type==='pit')?19:14);node.grounded=true}
 else if(type==='block'){node.width=wAttr||parseFloat(el.getAttribute('width'))||20;node.depth=dAttr||parseFloat(el.getAttribute('height'))||18;node.height=node.height||12;if(foot&&foot.length>=3)node.worldFootprint=foot;node.grounded=true}
 else if(type==='tower'){node.width=wAttr||parseFloat(el.getAttribute('width'))||8;node.height=node.height||48;node.grounded=true}
 else if(type==='ferris'){node.radius=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-radius'))||parseFloat(el.getAttribute('r'))||18;node.color=node.color||'#eef3f6';node.grounded=true}
 else if(type==='tree'){node.width=wAttr||((parseFloat(el.getAttribute('r'))||5)*1.2);node.height=node.height||12;node.foliage=node.color||'#3f7f4e';node.grounded=true}
 else if(type==='gantry'||type==='billboard'){node.width=wAttr||lAttr||parseFloat(el.getAttribute('width'))||16;node.span=node.width;node.height=node.height||4.5}
 else if(type==='tunnelPortal'){node.span=wAttr||parseFloat(el.getAttribute('width'))||18;node.height=node.height||6.2;node.deckWidth=dAttr||parseFloat(el.getAttribute('height'))||26}
 else if(type==='road'||type==='bridge'||type==='tunnel'){node.width=Math.max(6,wAttr||10);node.depth=dAttr||node.width;node.height=node.height||(type==='bridge'?1.6:(type==='tunnel'?1.0:.15));node.worldPath=pathPts.length>=2?pathPts:null;node.worldFootprint=(foot&&foot.length>=3)?foot:polyFootprintFromWorldPath(pathPts,node.width);node.color=node.color||(type==='bridge'?'#98a1a8':(type==='tunnel'?'#7f878f':'#7c848c'))}
 else if(type==='parking'){node.width=wAttr||14;node.depth=dAttr||12;node.height=node.height||0.12;node.worldFootprint=(foot&&foot.length>=3)?foot:polyFootprintFromWorldPath(pathPts,node.width);node.color=node.color||'#6d757d'}
 else if(type==='forestZone'){node.width=wAttr||12;node.depth=dAttr||10;node.height=node.height||0.10;node.worldFootprint=(foot&&foot.length>=3)?foot:polyFootprintFromWorldPath(pathPts,node.width);node.color=node.color||'#4b7b50'}
 else if(type==='flagPole'){node.height=node.height||24;node.grounded=true}
 else {node.width=wAttr||parseFloat(el.getAttribute('width'))||12;node.depth=dAttr||parseFloat(el.getAttribute('height'))||10;node.height=node.height||8;if(foot&&foot.length>=3)node.worldFootprint=foot}
 if(!node.maxDs&&anchor.maxRenderDistance)node.maxDs=anchor.maxRenderDistance;
 return node;
}

function apexImportedBarrierSegments(landmarks,L){
 const out=[];
 for(const it of (landmarks||[])){
  if(!it||it.type!=='barrier'||it.collision===false||!Array.isArray(it.worldPath)||it.worldPath.length<2)continue;
  const p=it.worldPath;
  for(let i=0;i<p.length-1;i++){
   const a=p[i],b=p[i+1],mx=(a[0]+b[0])*.5,mz=(a[1]+b[1])*.5,st=nearestTrackState(mx,mz,null),q=poseAt(st.s),vx=mx-q.x,vz=mz-q.z,vl=Math.hypot(vx,vz)||1;
   out.push({family:'BARRIER',s:st.s,x1:a[0],z1:a[1],x2:b[0],z2:b[1],nx:vx/vl,nz:vz/vl,width:it.width||.8,height:it.height||1.05,kind:it.barrierKind||'CONCRETE',name:it.name||''});
  }
 }
 out.sort((a,b)=>a.s-b.s);return out;
}
function apexImportedSurfaceZones(landmarks){
 return (landmarks||[]).filter(it=>it&&(it.type==='surfaceZone'||it.type==='water')&&Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3).map(it=>({kind:it.type==='water'?'WATER':(it.surfaceKind||'ASPHALT'),role:it.sourceRole||it.role||'',points:it.worldFootprint,name:it.name||''}));
}
function apexImportedDrivableArea(ctx,elements){
 const rings=[];for(const el of (elements||[])){const runs=apexSvgElementWorldRunsExact(ctx,el,5.5);for(const run0 of runs){let run=run0.slice();if(run.length<3)continue;if(Math.hypot(run[0][0]-run[run.length-1][0],run[0][1]-run[run.length-1][1])<2.5)run=run.slice(0,-1);if(run.length<3)continue;const area=apexSignedArea2D(run);if(Math.abs(area)<3)continue;let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minZ=Infinity,maxZ=-Infinity;for(const p of run){minX=Math.min(minX,p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p[0]);minZ=Math.min(minZ,p[1]);maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,p[1])}rings.push({points:run,sign:area>=0?1:-1,bbox:[minX,minZ,maxX,maxZ]})}}
 return rings.length?{authoritative:true,fillRule:'nonzero',rings}:null;
}
function apexDrivableAreaContains(x,z){
 const da=trackData&&trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.drivableArea;if(!da||da.authoritative!==true||!Array.isArray(da.rings)||!da.rings.length)return null;let winding=0;
 for(const r of da.rings){if(!r||!Array.isArray(r.points)||r.points.length<3)continue;const b=r.bbox;if(Array.isArray(b)&&b.length===4&&(x<b[0]||x>b[2]||z<b[1]||z>b[3]))continue;if(apexPointInPolyXZ(x,z,r.points))winding+=(r.sign||1)}return winding!==0;
}
function apexImportedBrakeBoards(landmarks,L){
 return (landmarks||[]).filter(it=>it&&it.type==='distanceBoard'&&it.anchor).map(it=>{const physicalLat=Number.isFinite(it.trackLat)?it.trackLat:null,side=physicalLat!=null&&Math.abs(physicalLat)>.35?Math.sign(physicalLat):(it.side||1);return {s:mod(it.anchor.s,L),cornerS:Number.isFinite(it.cornerS)?mod(it.cornerS,L):mod(it.anchor.s+(Number(it.mark)||0),L),side,mark:Number(it.mark)||100,width:it.width||2.9,height:it.height||4,latExtra:it.latExtra||1.1,authoredLat:physicalLat==null?null:Math.abs(physicalLat),authored:true};});
}
function apexPointInPolyXZ(x,z,poly){
 let inside=false;for(let i=0,j=poly.length-1;i<poly.length;j=i++){const xi=poly[i][0],zi=poly[i][1],xj=poly[j][0],zj=poly[j][1],cross=((zi>z)!=(zj>z))&&(x<(xj-xi)*(z-zi)/((zj-zi)||1e-9)+xi);if(cross)inside=!inside}return inside;
}
function apexSurfaceZoneAtWorld(x,z){
 const zones=trackData&&trackData.customWorld&&Array.isArray(trackData.customWorld.surfaceZones)?trackData.customWorld.surfaceZones:[];
 for(let i=zones.length-1;i>=0;i--){const q=zones[i];if(q&&Array.isArray(q.points)&&apexPointInPolyXZ(x,z,q.points))return q}return null;
}
function apexBarrierColor(kind){
 if(kind==='TECPRO')return '#d8dbe0';if(kind==='SAFER')return '#aeb8c2';if(kind==='TYRE')return '#17191b';if(kind==='GUARDRAIL')return '#9ba3aa';return '#c7cbd0';
}
function apexNearestPointOnSegment(px,pz,a){
 const dx=a.x2-a.x1,dz=a.z2-a.z1,l2=dx*dx+dz*dz||1,t=clamp(((px-a.x1)*dx+(pz-a.z1)*dz)/l2,0,1);return{x:a.x1+dx*t,z:a.z1+dz*t};
}
function apexCollisionSegments(){
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld;if(!cw)return[];
 if(Array.isArray(cw._collisionSegments))return cw._collisionSegments;
 const all=[];for(const sg of (cw.wallSegments||[]))all.push(sg);for(const sg of (cw.barrierSegments||[]))all.push(sg);cw._collisionSegments=all;return all;
}
function apexEnsureCollisionGrid(){
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld;if(!cw)return null;if(cw._collisionGrid)return cw._collisionGrid;
 const size=24,grid=new Map(),segs=apexCollisionSegments();
 for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i++){const s=segs[i],pad=Math.max(2,(s.width||.4)*.5+1.8),x0=Math.floor((Math.min(s.x1,s.x2)-pad)/size),x1=Math.floor((Math.max(s.x1,s.x2)+pad)/size),z0=Math.floor((Math.min(s.z1,s.z2)-pad)/size),z1=Math.floor((Math.max(s.z1,s.z2)+pad)/size);for(let gx=x0;gx<=x1;gx++)for(let gz=z0;gz<=z1;gz++){const k=gx+','+gz,a=grid.get(k);if(a)a.push(i);else grid.set(k,[i])}}
 cw._collisionGrid={size,grid};return cw._collisionGrid;
}
// v5.230.14 · El AUT debe conocer los mismos muros físicos que usa la colisión.
// Antes envProfile() sólo exponía los muros procedurales antiguos, por lo que un muro
// autorado en SVG podía existir físicamente y a la vez ser invisible para la IA.
function apexWallContextAtS(s,env=null){
 const e=env||envProfile(s),legacy=!!(e.wall||e.leftWall||e.rightWall),cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld;
 if(!trackData||!cw||!cw.authoritative||!apexCollisionSegments().length)return{wallish:legacy,legacy,exact:false,nearest:Infinity,pos:Infinity,neg:Infinity,corridor:null};
 const L=trackData.km*1000,key=Math.round(mod(s,L)/2);
 if(!cw._wallAwarenessCache)cw._wallAwarenessCache=new Map();
 const cached=cw._wallAwarenessCache.get(key);if(cached)return cached;
 const p=poseFlatAt(s),G=apexEnsureCollisionGrid();
 if(!G)return{wallish:legacy,legacy,exact:false,nearest:Infinity,pos:Infinity,neg:Infinity,corridor:null};
 const search=Math.max(13,e.half+e.curb+e.runoff+4.0),cells=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(search/G.size)),cx=Math.floor(p.x/G.size),cz=Math.floor(p.z/G.size),cand=new Set();
 for(let gx=cx-cells;gx<=cx+cells;gx++)for(let gz=cz-cells;gz<=cz+cells;gz++){const a=G.grid.get(gx+','+gz);if(a)for(const i of a)cand.add(i)}
 let nearest=Infinity,pos=Infinity,neg=Infinity;
 const segs=apexCollisionSegments();
 for(const idx of cand){const sg=segs[idx];if(Number.isFinite(sg.s)&&Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(s,sg.s,L))>58)continue;const q=apexNearestPointOnSegment(p.x,p.z,sg),dx=q.x-p.x,dz=q.z-p.z,raw=Math.hypot(dx,dz),clear=Math.max(0,raw-Math.max(.12,(sg.width||.32)*.5)),lat=dx*p.nx+dz*p.nz;nearest=Math.min(nearest,clear);if(lat>=0)pos=Math.min(pos,clear);else neg=Math.min(neg,clear)}
 const relevant=e.half+e.curb+e.runoff+2.6,exact=nearest<=relevant,wallish=legacy||exact,corridor=Number.isFinite(pos)&&Number.isFinite(neg)?pos+neg:null;
 const out={wallish,legacy,exact,nearest,pos,neg,corridor};cw._wallAwarenessCache.set(key,out);if(cw._wallAwarenessCache.size>4096)cw._wallAwarenessCache.clear();return out;
}
function apexWallishAtS(s,env=null){return apexWallContextAtS(s,env).wallish}
function apexImportedBarrierContactAtPose(x,z,heading,center){
 const segs=apexCollisionSegments();if(!segs.length)return null;const G=apexEnsureCollisionGrid(),cand=new Set(),cx=Math.floor(x/G.size),cz=Math.floor(z/G.size);
 for(let gx=cx-1;gx<=cx+1;gx++)for(let gz=cz-1;gz<=cz+1;gz++){const a=G.grid.get(gx+','+gz);if(a)for(const i of a)cand.add(i)}
 let best=null;const pts=wallFootprintSamples(x,z,heading);
 for(const idx of cand){const sg=segs[idx],clearance=Math.max(.16,(sg.width||.32)*.5+.10),dxs=sg.x2-sg.x1,dzs=sg.z2-sg.z1,sl=Math.hypot(dxs,dzs)||1,fnx=-dzs/sl,fnz=dxs/sl;
   for(const sp of pts){const np=apexNearestPointOnSegment(sp.x,sp.z,sg),dx=sp.x-np.x,dz=sp.z-np.z,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dz),pen=clearance-dist;if(pen<=0)continue;let nx,nz;if(dist>.0005){nx=-dx/dist;nz=-dz/dist}else{const sd=(x-np.x)*fnx+(z-np.z)*fnz,sgn=sd>=0?1:-1;nx=-fnx*sgn;nz=-fnz*sgn}if(!best||pen>best.penetration)best={penetration:pen,nx,nz,x:np.x,z:np.z,zone:sp.zone,lo:sp.lo,la:sp.la,t:center,side:sg.side||0,exact:true,wall:{barrier:{family:sg.family||'WALL',style:sg.wallStyle||'wall_f1',kind:sg.kind||'WALL_F1',height:sg.height||1.05,fence:sg.fence!==false,fenceH:sg.fenceH||1.3},imported:true,name:sg.name||''}}}
 }
 return best;
}

function apexReadCircuitMetadata(doc){
 try{const el=doc&&doc.querySelector?doc.querySelector('#APEX_CIRCUIT_METADATA'):null;if(!el)return null;const x=JSON.parse(String(el.textContent||'').trim());return x&&x.schema==='APEX_CIRCUIT_METADATA_V1'?x:null}catch(e){return null}
}
function apexNormalizeVerticalProfile(meta){
 const v=meta&&meta.verticalProfile;if(!v||v.schema!=='APEX_VERTICAL_PROFILE_V1'||!Array.isArray(v.samples)||v.samples.length<8)return null;
 const fields=Array.isArray(v.fields)?v.fields.slice(0,4):[];
 const wantsLegacy=fields.includes('bankDeg')||fields.includes('roadWidthM');
 const samples=v.samples.filter(a=>Array.isArray(a)&&a.length>=2&&Number.isFinite(+a[0])&&Number.isFinite(+a[1])).map(a=>{
   const out=[+a[0],+a[1]];
   if(wantsLegacy){out.push(Number.isFinite(+a[2])?+a[2]:0);out.push(Number.isFinite(+a[3])?+a[3]:0);}
   return out;
 }).sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]);
 if(samples.length<8)return null;
 // El perfil vertical conserva la referencia s del track_centerline; la meta puede estar en un finishS distinto de 0.
 // Si ya estaba normalizado, la resta es cero. Nunca dependemos de una cota del mar.
 const ref=String(v.reference||'').toUpperCase(),local=v.normalizeToZero===true||ref==='START_FINISH_ZERO'||ref==='START_FINISH'||ref==='TRACK_CENTERLINE_ORIGIN_ZERO';
 const datum=local?samples[0][1]:0;
 if(local)for(const row of samples)row[1]=Number((row[1]-datum).toFixed(5));
 return {schema:'APEX_VERTICAL_PROFILE_V1',spacingM:+v.spacingM||0,fields:wantsLegacy?(fields.length?fields:['lapFraction','elevationM','bankDeg','roadWidthM']):['lapFraction','elevationM'],samples,reference:local?'START_FINISH_ZERO':ref||'LEGACY',normalizeToZero:local,appliedDatumOffsetM:datum};
}
function apexImportedTimingControls(ctx,libraryZone){
 const roles=['sector-line','drs-detection','drs-start','drs-end','speed-trap','start-line','finish-line','straight-mode-activation','override-detection','override-activation'],out=[],els=[];
 for(const role of roles){for(const el of apexSvgFindAll(ctx.root,[role])){if(apexSvgElementInsideLibrary(ctx,el,libraryZone))continue;els.push(el);const m=apexSvgMidPoint(ctx.root,el);if(!m)continue;const w=ctx.toWorld([m.x,m.y]),ns=nearestTrackState(w[0],w[1],null,{radius:180,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:150});out.push({role,s:ns.s,sector:el.getAttribute('data-apex-sector')||null,zone:el.getAttribute('data-apex-zone')||null,name:el.getAttribute('data-apex-name')||role})}}
 return{controls:out,elements:els};
}

function apexImportedCornerMarkers(ctx,libraryZone){
 const out=[],elements=[];for(const el of apexSvgFindAll(ctx.root,['corner-marker'])){if(apexSvgElementInsideLibrary(ctx,el,libraryZone))continue;elements.push(el);const m=apexSvgMidPoint(ctx.root,el);let s=Number.NaN;if(m){const w=ctx.toWorld([m.x,m.y]),ns=nearestTrackState(w[0],w[1],null,{radius:190,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:160});s=ns.s}else{const raw=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-s'));if(Number.isFinite(raw))s=raw*ctx.scale}if(!Number.isFinite(s))continue;const turn=parseInt(el.getAttribute('data-apex-turn'))||out.length+1,parent=el.parentElement,labelEl=parent&&parent.querySelector?parent.querySelector(`[data-apex-corner-label="${turn}"]`):null,name=String((labelEl&&labelEl.textContent)||el.getAttribute('data-apex-name')||`T${turn}`).trim()||`T${turn}`,direction=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-direction')||'').toUpperCase()||null;out.push({turn,s:mod(s,ctx.km*1000),name,direction,authored:true})}out.sort((a,b)=>a.turn-b.turn);return{corners:out,elements};
}

// v5.230.19 · Polígonos de nivel / "placas" topográficas. El CONTORNO sigue siendo el dato.
// Lo que manda es el CONTORNO y su cota, no el relleno del polígono. Boxy puede
// editar path/polyline/polygon libremente; APEX muestrea su borde exacto ya transformado.
function apexContourElevationFromId(value){
 // Los IDs level_2, level_-1.25, level_7_5 son válidos. __2 permite repetidos.
 const m=String(value||'').match(/^level_([+-]?(?:\d+(?:[._]\d+)?|[.]\d+))(?:__\d+)?$/i);
 if(!m)return null;
 const y=Number(m[1].replace('_','.'));return Number.isFinite(y)?y:null;
}
// v5.230.45 · El terreno base SVG ES GEOMETRÍA, no un landmark decorativo.
// Todas las pistas comparten este parser. Conservar el id apex_terrain_base.
// v5.230.58 · NORMALIZACIÓN ÚNICA DE TERRENO SVG.
// Para el motor de terreno, <polygon>/<polyline> no tienen un camino geométrico
// distinto de <path>. Se convierten temporalmente a PATH y TODOS pasan por
// getTotalLength/getPointAtLength con el mismo paso, transformaciones y cierre.
// El SVG original NO se modifica: la conversión sólo existe durante la importación.
function apexTerrainElementWorldRunsUnified(ctx,el,stepM=4.2,closedHint=null){
 if(!ctx||!el)return[];
 const tag=String(el.tagName||'').toLowerCase();
 if(tag!=='polygon'&&tag!=='polyline')return apexSvgElementWorldRunsExact(ctx,el,stepM);
 const raw=parseSvgPointPairsRaw(el.getAttribute('points'));
 if(raw.length<2)return[];
 const closed=closedHint===null
   ?(tag==='polygon'||String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-closed')||'').toLowerCase()==='true')
   :!!closedHint;
 const p=document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','path');
 const d='M '+raw.map((q,i)=>(i?'L ':'')+q[0]+' '+q[1]).join(' ')+(closed?' Z':'');
 p.setAttribute('d',d);
 // Conservamos cualquier transformación propia; las del grupo padre se heredan
 // porque el PATH temporal se inserta como hermano inmediato del elemento original.
 for(const a of ['transform','style','class'])if(el.hasAttribute(a))p.setAttribute(a,el.getAttribute(a));
 p.setAttribute('data-apex-temp-terrain-normalized','1');
 const parent=el.parentNode;if(!parent)return[];
 parent.insertBefore(p,el.nextSibling);
 try{return apexSvgElementWorldRunsExact(ctx,p,stepM)}finally{p.remove()}
}

function apexImportedTerrainBase(ctx,libraryZone){
 const root=ctx.root,el=[...root.querySelectorAll('[data-apex-role="terrain-base"]')]
    .find(e=>!apexSvgElementInsideLibrary(ctx,e,libraryZone));
 if(!el)return null;
 const j=readStoredCircuitJson(ctx.spec&&ctx.spec.dir)||{},tc=j.terrain||{},raw=el.getAttribute('data-apex-base-level-m');
 const byId=apexContourElevationFromId(el.getAttribute('data-apex-base-level-id'));
 const y=raw!==null&&raw.trim()!==''&&Number.isFinite(+raw)?+raw:
   (byId!==null?byId:(Number.isFinite(+tc.baseLevelM)?+tc.baseLevelM:0));
 let rings=[];
 const tag=String(el.tagName||'').toLowerCase();
 if(tag==='rect'){
   const x=+el.getAttribute('x')||0,z=+el.getAttribute('y')||0,w=+el.getAttribute('width')||0,h=+el.getAttribute('height')||0;
   if(w>0&&h>0)rings=[[[x,z],[x+w,z],[x+w,z+h],[x,z+h]].map(p=>ctx.toWorld(apexSvgMapLocalPoint(root,el,p[0],p[1])))];
 }else{
   // terrain-base siempre es un área cerrada. PATH y POLYGON usan la MISMA ruta.
   rings=apexTerrainElementWorldRunsUnified(ctx,el,12,true).filter(p=>p.length>=3);
 }
 if(!rings.length)return null;
 return{id:String(el.id||'apex_terrain_base'),levelId:String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-base-level-id')||'level_0'),
   elevationM:y,rings,terrainTone:el.getAttribute('data-apex-terrain-tone')||'GREEN',authored:true};
}
function apexImportedTerrainContours(ctx,libraryZone){
 const out=[],elements=[],labelElev=new Map();
 for(const t of [...ctx.root.querySelectorAll('text[data-apex-contour-level-label]')]){if(apexSvgElementInsideLibrary(ctx,t,libraryZone))continue;const id=String(t.getAttribute('data-apex-contour-level-label')||''),m=String(t.textContent||'').replace(',','.').match(/[-+]?\d+(?:\.\d+)?/);if(id&&m&&Number.isFinite(+m[0]))labelElev.set(id,+m[0])}
 const candidates=[...ctx.root.querySelectorAll('path,polyline,polygon')].filter(el=>{
   if(apexSvgElementInsideLibrary(ctx,el,libraryZone))return false;
   const role=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-role')||'').toLowerCase();
   const typed=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-type')||'').toLowerCase();
   return role==='terrain-contour'||role==='terrain-plate'||typed==='terraincontour'||((role==='terrain-zone')&&Number.isFinite(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-elevation-m'))))||apexContourElevationFromId(el.id)!==null;
 });
 let idx=0;
 for(const el of candidates){
   const levelId=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-contour-level-id')||''),rawAttr=el.getAttribute('data-apex-elevation-m'),attrElev=rawAttr!==null&&String(rawAttr).trim()!==''?Number(rawAttr):NaN,idElev=apexContourElevationFromId(el.id),levelElev=apexContourElevationFromId(levelId);
   // La altitud escrita explícitamente MANDA; ID como respaldo; rótulo solamente legado.
   const elev=Number.isFinite(attrElev)?attrElev:(idElev!==null?idElev:(levelElev!==null?levelElev:labelElev.get(levelId)));
   if(!Number.isFinite(elev))continue;
   elements.push(el);
   const tag=String(el.tagName||'').toLowerCase(),forceOpen=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-contour-open')||'').toLowerCase()==='true',closed=!forceOpen&&(tag==='polygon'||/\bz\s*$/i.test(String(el.getAttribute('d')||'').trim())||String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-closed')||'').toLowerCase()==='true');
   // v5.230.58: mismo muestreo geométrico para PATH / POLYGON / POLYLINE.
   for(const run of apexTerrainElementWorldRunsUnified(ctx,el,4.2,closed)){
     if(!Array.isArray(run)||run.length<2)continue;
     let pts=run.map(p=>[+p[0],+p[1]]);
     const coreCount=parseInt(el.getAttribute('data-apex-contour-core-count'))||0,closeMode=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-contour-close-mode')||'');
     if(forceOpen&&closeMode==='bbox-corners'&&coreCount>=2&&coreCount<=pts.length)pts=pts.slice(0,coreCount);
     if(closed&&pts.length>2&&Math.hypot(pts[0][0]-pts[pts.length-1][0],pts[0][1]-pts[pts.length-1][1])>1.5)pts.push([pts[0][0],pts[0][1]]);
     out.push({id:String(el.id||`terrain_contour_${idx}`),name:String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-name')||el.id||`Cota ${elev} m`),elevationM:+elev,priority:parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-priority'))||0,points:pts,closed,sourceRole:String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-role')||'terrain-contour'),authored:true});idx++;
   }
 }
 return{contours:out,elements};
}

function apexSampleVerticalProfile(s,column){
 const v=trackData&&trackData.verticalProfile,arr=v&&Array.isArray(v.samples)?v.samples:null;
 if(!arr||arr.length<2)return null;
 const f=mod(s,trackData.km*1000)/(trackData.km*1000);let lo=0,hi=arr.length;
 while(lo<hi){const mid=(lo+hi)>>1;if(arr[mid][0]<=f)lo=mid+1;else hi=mid;}
 const i=mod(lo-1,arr.length),j=lo%arr.length,a=arr[i],b=arr[j],x0=a[0]-(lo===0?1:0),x1=b[0]+(lo===arr.length?1:0),h=x1-x0;
 const y0=+a[column],y1=+b[column];if(!Number.isFinite(y0)||!Number.isFinite(y1)||h<=1e-9)return null;
 const t=clamp((f-x0)/h,0,1);
 const prev=arr[mod(i-1,arr.length)],next=arr[(j+1)%arr.length];
 // A fraction-1 endpoint duplicates fraction 0; skip it when deriving seam slopes.
 const p=i===0&&arr[arr.length-1][0]===1?arr[arr.length-2]:prev;
 const q=j===arr.length-1&&b[0]===1?arr[1]:next;
 const xp=p[0]>=x0?p[0]-1:p[0],xq=q[0]<=x1?q[0]+1:q[0],yp=+p[column],yq=+q[column];
 const d=(y1-y0)/h,dp=Number.isFinite(yp)?(y0-yp)/Math.max(1e-9,x0-xp):d,dn=Number.isFinite(yq)?(yq-y1)/Math.max(1e-9,xq-x1):d;
 const slope=(a,b)=>a*b<=0?0:2*a*b/(a+b),m0=slope(dp,d),m1=slope(d,dn),t2=t*t,t3=t2*t;
 return (2*t3-3*t2+1)*y0+(t3-2*t2+t)*h*m0+(-2*t3+3*t2)*y1+(t3-t2)*h*m1;
}
function apexAuthoredBankAt(s){
 const authored=trackData&&Array.isArray(trackData.authoredBanking)?trackData.authoredBanking:null;
 if(!authored||!authored.length)return null;
 // Authoritative for the entire lap, including flat gaps and absent turns.
 if(!trackData._authoredBankRegions||trackData._authoredBankOwner!==trackData.cornerRegions){
 trackData._authoredBankRegions=authored.map(ent=>({ent,r:(trackData.cornerRegions||[]).find(r=>r.turn===ent.turn)})).filter(v=>v.r);
 trackData._authoredBankOwner=trackData.cornerRegions;
 }
 let total=0,weight=0;
 for(const {ent,r} of trackData._authoredBankRegions){
 const ref=apexContinuousCornerReference(s,r);if(!ref.region)continue;
 const sign=r.curveSign||Math.sign(curvatureAt(r.centerS))||1;let val=Number(ent.bankDeg)||0,gain=ref.gain;
 if(Array.isArray(ent.profile)&&ent.profile.length>=2){
 const pts=ent.profile,u=mod(s-r.startS,trackData.km*1000)/r.lengthM;val=pts[0][1];
 for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++){const a=pts[i-1],b=pts[i];if(u<=b[0]){val=lerp(a[1],b[1],profileSmooth01((u-a[0])/Math.max(1e-6,b[0]-a[0])));break;}val=b[1];}
 const edge=Math.min(.06,20/r.lengthM);gain=profileSmooth01(Math.min(1,u/edge,(1-u)/edge));
 }
 total+=sign*val*gain;weight+=gain;
 }
 // Closely spaced or overlapping regions blend rather than switching abruptly.
 return total/Math.max(1,weight)*Math.PI/180;
}
async function importEditableCircuitSvgText(text,spec,options={}){
 const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString(text,'image/svg+xml'),rootDoc=doc.documentElement;if(!rootDoc||rootDoc.nodeName==='parsererror')throw Error('SVG no válido');
 const meta=raceMetaFor(spec)||{},layoutId=rootDoc.getAttribute('data-apex-layout')||meta.layout||spec.dir,turns=parseInt(rootDoc.getAttribute('data-apex-turns'))||meta.turns||0,laps=parseInt(rootDoc.getAttribute('data-apex-laps'))||meta.laps||0,apexMeta=apexReadCircuitMetadata(doc),verticalProfile=apexNormalizeVerticalProfile(apexMeta),timingData=apexMeta&&apexMeta.timing?clonePlain(apexMeta.timing):null;
 const prevTrackData=trackData,prevPlayer=player;let ctx=null;
 try{
   ctx=apexSvgTrackContext(doc,spec);
   trackData={pts:ctx.points,km:ctx.km,layout:layoutId,direction:ctx.direction,turns,laps,circuitType:'RACE',startAligned:true,detailedSvg:true,spec,verticalProfile,timingData};prepareTrackModel();
   // Todos los elementos se convierten con LA MISMA transformación que track_centerline.
   // Las plantillas que permanecen dentro de la biblioteca NO cuentan como geometría activa.
   const libraryZone=apexSvgLibraryZoneBounds(ctx.root),activeRoleEls=els=>els.filter(el=>!apexSvgElementInsideLibrary(ctx,el,libraryZone)),timingImport=apexImportedTimingControls(ctx,libraryZone),timingControls=timingImport.controls,cornerImport=apexImportedCornerMarkers(ctx,libraryZone),cornerMarkers=cornerImport.corners,terrainImport=apexImportedTerrainContours(ctx,libraryZone),terrainContours=terrainImport.contours,terrainBase=apexImportedTerrainBase(ctx,libraryZone);
   trackData.timingControls=timingControls;trackData.cornerMarkers=cornerMarkers;const terrainContoursAuthoritative=String(rootDoc.getAttribute('data-apex-terrain-contours-authoritative')||'').toLowerCase()==='true'||terrainContours.length>0,terrainReference=String(rootDoc.getAttribute('data-apex-terrain-reference')||'ABSOLUTE').toUpperCase();
   const pitEls=activeRoleEls(apexSvgFindAll(ctx.root,['pitlane_centerline','pitlane-centerline']));
   const wallLeftEls=activeRoleEls(apexSvgFindAll(ctx.root,['wall_left','wall-left']));
   const wallRightEls=activeRoleEls(apexSvgFindAll(ctx.root,['wall_right','wall-right']));
   const edgeLeftEls=activeRoleEls(apexSvgFindAll(ctx.root,['track_left_edge','track-left-edge']));
   const edgeRightEls=activeRoleEls(apexSvgFindAll(ctx.root,['track_right_edge','track-right-edge']));
   const drivableEls=activeRoleEls(apexSvgFindAll(ctx.root,['drivable_area','drivable-area'])),drivableArea=apexImportedDrivableArea(ctx,drivableEls);
   const pitWidth=((pitEls[0]&&parseFloat(pitEls[0].getAttribute('data-apex-width')))||5.9),pitSpeedKph=((pitEls[0]&&parseFloat(pitEls[0].getAttribute('data-apex-speed-kph')))||0);
   const pitLaneSamples=apexSvgGuideSamplesExact(ctx,pitEls,pitWidth),wallLeftSamples=apexSvgGuideSamplesExact(ctx,wallLeftEls,0),wallRightSamples=apexSvgGuideSamplesExact(ctx,wallRightEls,0),edgeLeftSamples=apexSvgGuideSamplesExact(ctx,edgeLeftEls,0),edgeRightSamples=apexSvgGuideSamplesExact(ctx,edgeRightEls,0);
   const pitRuns=apexSvgWorldRunsExact(ctx,pitEls,2.6),pitNetwork=apexBuildPitNetwork(pitRuns,pitWidth),wallLeftRuns=apexSvgWorldRunsExact(ctx,wallLeftEls,2.5),wallRightRuns=apexSvgWorldRunsExact(ctx,wallRightEls,2.5),wallSegments=[...apexBuildExactWallSegments(wallLeftRuns,1),...apexBuildExactWallSegments(wallRightRuns,-1)];
   if(pitSpeedKph>0)pitNetwork.speedKph=clamp(pitSpeedKph,30,120);
   const excluded=new Set([...pitEls,...wallLeftEls,...wallRightEls,...edgeLeftEls,...edgeRightEls,...drivableEls,...cornerImport.elements,...terrainImport.elements,...(terrainBase?[...ctx.root.querySelectorAll('[data-apex-role="terrain-base"]')]:[]),...timingImport.elements.filter(el=>!['start-line','finish-line'].includes(String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-role')||'').toLowerCase())),ctx.trackEl]);
   const startEls=apexSvgFindAll(ctx.root,['start_finish','start-finish','start_marker','start-marker']);for(const x of startEls)excluded.add(x);
   const candidates=[...ctx.root.querySelectorAll('[data-apex-type],[data-apex-role]')].filter(el=>!excluded.has(el)&&!el.hasAttribute('data-apex-library-zone')&&!apexSvgElementInsideLibrary(ctx,el,libraryZone));const landmarks=[];
   for(let i=0;i<candidates.length;i++){const item=apexSvgLandmarkExact(candidates[i],i,ctx);if(item)landmarks.push(item)}
   const brakeBoards=apexImportedBrakeBoards(landmarks,ctx.km*1000),circuitLandmarks=landmarks.filter(it=>it.type!=='distanceBoard'),curbZones=apexImportedCurbZones(circuitLandmarks,ctx.km*1000);
   const structureZones=apexImportedStructureZones(circuitLandmarks,ctx.km*1000),barrierSegments=apexImportedBarrierSegments(circuitLandmarks,ctx.km*1000),surfaceZones=apexImportedSurfaceZones(circuitLandmarks),brakeBoardsAuthoritative=String(rootDoc.getAttribute('data-apex-brake-boards-authoritative')||'').toLowerCase()==='true',geometryPayload={points:ctx.points,km:ctx.km,direction:ctx.direction,layoutId,turns,laps,circuitType:'RACE',source:'EXACT_USER_SVG',authoritative:true,rawSvg:String(text),verticalProfile,timingData,timingControls,cornerMarkers,brakeBoardsAuthoritative};
   const worldPayload={profileRevision:23082,authoritative:true,terrainBase,landmarks:circuitLandmarks,brakeBoards,brakeBoardsAuthoritative,cornerMarkers,pitLaneSamples,pitNetwork,wallLeftSamples,wallRightSamples,wallSegments,edgeLeftSamples,edgeRightSamples,structureZones,barrierSegments,surfaceZones,terrainContours,terrainContoursAuthoritative,terrainReference,drivableArea,curbZones};
   // SVG, huellas, muros y alturas se guardan JUNTOS en una transacción IndexedDB.
   // Nunca duplicamos el SVG (que puede contener una fotografía base64) en localStorage.
   let savedStorage='BASE_MEMORY';
   if(options&&options.persist===false){
     apexBaseTrackSvgCache.set(String(spec.dir||''),geometryPayload);
     apexBaseWorldProfileCache.set(String(spec.dir||''),normalizeCustomWorldProfile(worldPayload));
   }else savedStorage=await apexSaveLargeCircuit(spec.dir,geometryPayload,worldPayload);
   return {geometry:true,storage:savedStorage,geometryPayload,worldPayload,landmarks:circuitLandmarks.length,brakeBoards:brakeBoards.length,cornerMarkers:cornerMarkers.length,pit:pitNetwork.runs.length>0,pitRuns:pitNetwork.runs.length,walls:wallSegments.length,edges:edgeLeftSamples.length+edgeRightSamples.length,wallGroups:wallLeftRuns.length+wallRightRuns.length,barriers:barrierSegments.length,surfaces:surfaceZones.length,terrainContours:terrainContours.length,terrainBase:!!terrainBase,drivableArea:drivableArea?drivableArea.rings.length:0,timing:timingControls.length,vertical:!!verticalProfile};
 }finally{if(ctx&&ctx.root&&ctx.root.isConnected)ctx.root.remove();trackData=prevTrackData;player=prevPlayer}
}
function circuitOptionsHtml(){const f1=calendar.slice(0,F1_SEASON_COUNT).map((e,i)=>`<option value="${i}">R${String(e.r).padStart(2,'0')} · ${stripCountryEmoji(e.country)} — ${e.name}</option>`).join(''),extra=calendar.slice(F1_SEASON_COUNT).map((e,j)=>`<option value="${F1_SEASON_COUNT+j}">${stripCountryEmoji(e.country)} — ${e.name}</option>`).join('');return `<optgroup label="F1 2026">${f1}</optgroup>${extra?`<optgroup label="Fuera del calendario · históricos y otros">${extra}</optgroup>`:''}`}
function populateSvgTrackSelector(preferredDir=''){const sel=$("svgTrackSelect");if(!sel)return;sel.innerHTML=circuitOptionsHtml();const wanted=preferredDir||((calendar[+($("track")&&$("track").value||0)]||{}).dir)||'';const i=calendar.findIndex(c=>c.dir===wanted);sel.value=String(i>=0?i:0)}
function setJsonToolStatus(msg,kind=''){const el=$("jsonToolsStatus");if(!el)return;el.className='svgToolsStatus'+(kind?(' '+kind):'');el.textContent=msg}
function apexCircuitJsonForCurrentTrack(spec){
 if(!trackData)return null;const meta=raceMetaFor(spec)||{},env=circuitEnvironmentProfile(spec.dir),previous=readStoredCircuitJson(spec.dir)||{},priorBank=previous.banking&&Array.isArray(previous.banking.corners)?previous.banking.corners:[],priorByTurn=new Map(priorBank.map(c=>[+c.turn,c])),corners=apexDetectedCornerMarkersForExport().map(c=>{const prior=priorByTurn.get(+c.turn)||{},turn=+c.turn||0;return{...prior,turn,name:String(apexJsonCornerNameMap(spec.dir).get(turn)||c.name||`T${turn}`),direction:String(c.direction||prior.direction||''),s:Number(mod(+c.s||0,trackData.km*1000).toFixed(3)),bankDeg:Number.isFinite(+prior.bankDeg)?+prior.bankDeg:Number((bankAt(+c.s||0)*180/Math.PI).toFixed(4))}}),pitNet=trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork;
 const prev=readStoredCircuitJson(spec.dir)||{},sf=apexCircuitStartFinishS(spec),baseTiming=trackData.timingData||prev.timing||{},td={...baseTiming,controlSchema:'APEX_TIMING_CONTROLS_V5_POINT_CROSSING',startS:Number(sf.start.toFixed(3)),finishS:Number(sf.finish.toFixed(3)),sectors:apexTimingSectorsFromControls(spec),controls:(trackData.timingControls&&trackData.timingControls.length?trackData.timingControls:(Array.isArray(baseTiming.controls)?baseTiming.controls:[])).map(c=>({...c,s:Number((+c.s||0).toFixed(3))}))};
