 return {schema:'APEX_CIRCUIT_JSON_V1',schemaVersion:1,appVersion:APP_VERSION,circuit:{id:spec.dir,name:spec.name,country:spec.country,city:spec.themeCity||spec.name,layout:trackData.layout||meta.layout||spec.dir,calendar:spec.nonCalendar?'EXTRA':'F1_2026',round:spec.nonCalendar?null:spec.r},track:{...(prev.track||{}),lengthMeters:Number((trackData.km*1000).toFixed(3)),surface:'ASPHALT',circuitType:trackData.circuitType||meta.circuitType||'RACE'},race:{direction:trackData.direction||meta.direction||'CLOCKWISE',turns:trackData.turns||meta.turns||0,laps:trackData.laps||meta.laps||0,pit:{...(meta.pit||{}),speedKph:(pitNet&&Number.isFinite(pitNet.speedKph)?pitNet.speedKph:((meta.pit||{}).speedKph||80))}},environment:{...(prev.environment||{}),theme:spec.theme||'park',family:env.family||environmentFamilyFromTheme(spec.theme),terrainTone:apexTerrainContourPalette().tone},visual:{...(prev.visual||{})},activeAero:prev.activeAero?clonePlain(prev.activeAero):(trackData&&trackData.straightMode?clonePlain(trackData.straightMode):null),terrain:{contourReference:'LOCAL_ABSOLUTE',contourScale:1,contourOffsetM:0,...(prev.terrain||{})},walls:{style:'wall_f1',heightM:1.04,catchFence:true,fenceHeightM:2.35,fenceLeanM:.55,postSpacingM:2.8,advertising:{enabled:true,panelLengthM:72},...(prev.walls||{})},pitLane:prev.pitLane?clonePlain(prev.pitLane):null,timing:td?clonePlain(td):null,verticalProfile:apexVerticalProfileForExport(),banking:{mode:'CORNER_MARKERS',corners},gameplay:{...(prev.gameplay||{}),aiGripScale:1,difficultyScale:1},notes:'BASE APEX + override usuario. SVG = geometría física. JSON = ficha, carrera, entorno visual, altitud longitudinal, timing y peraltes.'};
}
function registerCircuitFromJson(j){
 const c=j&&j.circuit||{},env=j&&j.environment||{},id=String(c.id||'').trim();if(!id)throw Error('El JSON necesita circuit.id');let spec=calendar.find(s=>s.dir===id);
 if(!spec){spec={r:null,country:String(c.country||'🌐 Otro'),name:String(c.name||id),themeCity:String(c.city||c.name||id),dir:id,theme:String(env.theme||'park'),nonCalendar:true,libraryGroup:'Importados',externalSource:'user-json'};calendar.push(spec)}
 applyStoredCircuitJsonToSpec(spec,j);if(spec.nonCalendar)persistCustomCircuitSpec(spec);return spec;
}
function validateCircuitJson(j){if(!j||j.schema!=='APEX_CIRCUIT_JSON_V1')throw Error('Esquema no compatible: se esperaba APEX_CIRCUIT_JSON_V1');if(!j.circuit||!String(j.circuit.id||'').trim())throw Error('Falta circuit.id');if(j.verticalProfile&&j.verticalProfile.schema!=='APEX_VERTICAL_PROFILE_V1')throw Error('verticalProfile no es APEX_VERTICAL_PROFILE_V1');return j}

 if(imp)imp.onclick=()=>file&&file.click();
 if(repoLoad)repoLoad.onclick=async()=>{const spec=currentSvgToolSpec();setSvgToolStatus(`Cargando versión guardada de ${spec.name}…`);try{const mr=await fetch("circuits/manifest.json",{cache:"no-cache"});if(!mr.ok)throw Error("No se pudo cargar el manifiesto de circuitos ("+mr.status+")");const manifest=await mr.json(),entry=manifest&&manifest.circuits&&manifest.circuits[spec.dir];if(!entry)throw Error("No hay una versión guardada en apex/circuits para "+spec.name);if(entry.json){const jr=await fetch("circuits/"+entry.json,{cache:"no-cache"});if(!jr.ok)throw Error("No se pudo cargar el JSON guardado ("+jr.status+")");const j=validateCircuitJson(await jr.json());const jspec=registerCircuitFromJson(j);if(!writeStoredCircuitJson(jspec.dir,j))throw Error("No se pudo guardar el override JSON");applyStoredCircuitJsonToSpec(spec,j)}const sr=await fetch("circuits/"+entry.svg,{cache:"no-cache"});if(!sr.ok)throw Error("No se pudo cargar el SVG guardado ("+sr.status+")");const txt=await sr.text(),res=await importEditableCircuitSvgText(txt,spec);updateAllCircuitCards();setSvgToolStatus(`Versión guardada cargada desde apex/circuits en ${spec.name}: SVG${entry.json?' + JSON':''}${res.brakeBoards?', '+res.brakeBoards+' carteles':''}${res.timing?', '+res.timing+' marcadores timing':''}.  if(pair)pair.onclick=async()=>{const spec=currentSvgToolSpec();try{await withPreparedTrackSpec(spec,()=>{const stored=readStoredCustomTrackSvg(spec.dir),baseSvg=buildEditableCircuitSvgForCurrentTrack(spec),svg=apexEnrichExportSvg(baseSvg,spec),j=apexCircuitJsonForCurrentTrack(spec);triggerDownload(`apex26_${spec.dir}_circuito.svg`,svg);setTimeout(()=>triggerDownload(`apex26_${spec.dir}_datos.json`,JSON.stringify(j,null,2),'application/json'),180)});setJsonToolStatus(`SVG + JSON exportados de ${spec.name}.`,'ok')}catch(e){setJsonToolStatus(`No se pudo exportar el par: ${e&&e.message||e}`,'err')}};
}

// ---------- v5.230.62.8.6.7.6.4 · INSPECCIÓN 3D DE CIRCUITOS ----------
const apexInspection={active:false,s:0,lat:0,height:55,fov:80,yaw:0,pitchDeg:-30,speed:45,auto:false,fullScene:true,layers:{timing:true,corners:true,boards:true,pit:true},drag:false,lastX:0,lastY:0,freeX:null,freeZ:null,pointers:new Map(),gesture:null,lastTap:0,uiAcc:0};
function apexInspectionCancelUiTimer(){clearTimeout(apexInspection._uiTimer);apexInspection._uiTimer=null;}
function apexInspectionScheduleUiHide(delay=3200){if(matchMedia('(pointer:fine)').matches)return;apexInspectionCancelUiTimer();if(!apexInspection.active)return;const ctl=$('inspectorControls');if(ctl&&!ctl.classList.contains('insCollapsed'))return;apexInspection._uiTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(!apexInspection.active||apexInspection.pointers.size)return;const c=$('inspectorControls');if(c&&!c.classList.contains('insCollapsed'))return;document.body.classList.add('ins-ui-hidden');},delay);}
function apexInspectionShowUi(delay=3200){if(!apexInspection.active)return;document.body.classList.remove('ins-ui-hidden');apexInspectionScheduleUiHide(delay);}
function apexInspectionHideUi(){if(!apexInspection.active)return;const ctl=$('inspectorControls');if(ctl)ctl.classList.add('insCollapsed');apexInspectionCancelUiTimer();document.body.classList.add('ins-ui-hidden');}

function apexInspectionActive(){return !!apexInspection.active}
function apexInspectionFullScene(){return !!(apexInspection.active&&apexInspection.fullScene)}
function apexInspectionFarDistance(){if(!trackData)return 12000;return Math.max(12000,(Number(trackData.km)||5)*1000*2.8)}
function apexInspectionTrackBounds(){if(!trackData||!Array.isArray(trackData.pts)||!trackData.pts.length)return null;let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minZ=Infinity,maxZ=-Infinity;for(const p of trackData.pts){if(!p)continue;minX=Math.min(minX,+p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,+p[0]);minZ=Math.min(minZ,+p[1]);maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,+p[1])}if(!Number.isFinite(minX))return null;return{minX,maxX,minZ,maxZ,cx:(minX+maxX)/2,cz:(minZ+maxZ)/2,width:maxX-minX,depth:maxZ-minZ};}
function apexInspectionFitWholeCircuit(){
 const b=apexInspectionTrackBounds();if(!b)return;
 const fov=80*Math.PI/180,aspect=W/Math.max(1,H),extent=Math.max(b.width,b.depth*aspect);
 apexInspection.auto=false;apexInspection.freeX=b.cx;apexInspection.freeZ=b.cz;apexInspection.targetY=apexWorldGroundY(b.cx,b.cz);apexInspection.lat=0;
 apexInspection.height=clamp(extent/(2*Math.tan(fov*.5))*1.25,80,5000);apexInspection.fov=80;apexInspection.yaw=0;apexInspection.pitchDeg=-89;
 apexInspectionSyncPlayer();apexInspectionUpdateUi();
}
function apexInspectionTrackPose(){if(!trackData)return null;const L=trackData.km*1000,s=mod(apexInspection.s,L),q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),nx=Number.isFinite(q.ox)?q.ox:(q.nx||1),nz=Number.isFinite(q.oz)?q.oz:(q.nz||0);return{s,q,nx,nz,x:q.x+nx*apexInspection.lat,z:q.z+nz*apexInspection.lat};}
function apexInspectionCameraXZ(){
 const p=apexInspectionTrackPose(),x=Number.isFinite(apexInspection.freeX)?apexInspection.freeX:p?p.x:0,z=Number.isFinite(apexInspection.freeZ)?apexInspection.freeZ:p?p.z:0;
 const tilt=clamp(-apexInspection.pitchDeg,12,89)*Math.PI/180,back=apexInspection.height/Math.tan(tilt);
 return{x:x-Math.sin(apexInspection.yaw)*back,z:z-Math.cos(apexInspection.yaw)*back};
}
function apexInspectionDriverCamera(){
 const p=apexInspectionTrackPose(),pos=apexInspectionCameraXZ(),yaw=apexInspection.yaw;
 const ground=Number.isFinite(apexInspection.targetY)?apexInspection.targetY:p?p.q.y:0;
 return {...(p?p.q:{}),x:pos.x,z:pos.z,y:ground+apexInspection.height,tx:Math.sin(yaw),tz:Math.cos(yaw),nx:Math.cos(yaw),nz:-Math.sin(yaw),pitch:0,pitchRad:apexInspection.pitchDeg*Math.PI/180,inspectionOrbit:true,bank:0,roadRoll:0,surfaceY:ground,centerY:ground};
}
function apexInspectionSyncPlayer(){
 if(!apexInspection.active||!trackData||!player)return;const cpos={x:apexInspection.freeX,z:apexInspection.freeZ},hint=Number.isFinite(player.trackIndex)?player.trackIndex:undefined,n=nearestTrackState(cpos.x,cpos.z,hint),L=trackData.km*1000,s=n&&Number.isFinite(n.s)?mod(n.s,L):mod(apexInspection.s,L),q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s);apexInspection.s=s;player.s=s;player.progressS=s;player.total=s;player.lat=0;player.x=q.x;player.z=q.z;player.heading=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz);player.track=q;player.trackIndex=q.i;player.trackDirDot=1;player.speed=0;player.fovBoost=0;player.cameraLook=0;player.cameraGradePitch=0;
}
function apexInspectionCenterOnS(v,alignHeading=false){if(!trackData)return;const L=trackData.km*1000,s=mod(+v||0,L),q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),nx=Number.isFinite(q.ox)?q.ox:(q.nx||1),nz=Number.isFinite(q.oz)?q.oz:(q.nz||0);apexInspection.s=s;apexInspection.freeX=q.x+nx*apexInspection.lat;apexInspection.freeZ=q.z+nz*apexInspection.lat;apexInspection.targetY=q.y;if(alignHeading)apexInspection.yaw=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz);apexInspectionSyncPlayer();apexInspectionUpdateUi();}
function apexInspectionSetS(v){apexInspectionCenterOnS(v,true)}
function apexInspectionJump(delta){apexInspectionCenterOnS(apexInspection.s+delta,true)}
function apexInspectionPanPixels(dx,dy,baseX=null,baseZ=null){
 const tilt=clamp(-apexInspection.pitchDeg,12,89)*Math.PI/180,distance=apexInspection.height/Math.sin(tilt),f=(W*.5)/Math.tan(sceneHFov()*.5),scale=distance/Math.max(1,f),h=apexInspection.yaw;
 const x=Number.isFinite(baseX)?baseX:apexInspection.freeX,z=Number.isFinite(baseZ)?baseZ:apexInspection.freeZ;
 apexInspection.freeX=x-Math.cos(h)*dx*scale+Math.sin(h)*dy*scale/Math.sin(tilt);
 apexInspection.freeZ=z+Math.sin(h)*dx*scale+Math.cos(h)*dy*scale/Math.sin(tilt);
 apexInspectionSyncPlayer();
}
function apexInspectionResetView(){if(!trackData)return;const sf=apexCircuitStartFinishS(trackData.spec),s=Number.isFinite(apexInspection.s)?apexInspection.s:sf.finish,q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s);apexInspection.lat=0;apexInspection.height=55;apexInspection.fov=80;apexInspection.pitchDeg=-30;apexInspection.yaw=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz);apexInspection.freeX=q.x;apexInspection.freeZ=q.z;apexInspection.targetY=q.y;apexInspection.auto=false;apexInspectionSyncPlayer();apexInspectionUpdateUi();}
function apexInspectionNearestPoint(){
 if(!trackData)return null;const L=trackData.km*1000,pts=[];for(const c of apexTimingPointControls(trackData.spec))pts.push({kind:'TIMING',name:c.name||c.id||c.role,s:c.s});for(const c of trackData.cornerMarkers||[])if(Number.isFinite(+c.s))pts.push({kind:'CURVA',name:`T${c.turn}${c.name&&c.name!==`T${c.turn}`?' · '+c.name:''}`,s:+c.s});let best=null;for(const p of pts){const d=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(p.s,apexInspection.s,L));if(!best||d<best.d)best={...p,d};}return best;
}
function apexInspectionUpdateJumpOptions(){
 const sel=$('inspectorJump');if(!sel||!trackData)return;const options=['<option value="">IR A PUNTO…</option>'];for(const c of apexTimingPointControls(trackData.spec)){const role=String(c.role||'');let label=apexTimingControlLabel(c);options.push(`<option value="${Number(c.s).toFixed(3)}">${escapeXml(label)} · ${Math.round(c.s)} m</option>`)}for(const c of trackData.cornerMarkers||[])if(Number.isFinite(+c.s))options.push(`<option value="${Number(c.s).toFixed(3)}">T${c.turn}${c.name&&c.name!==`T${c.turn}`?' · '+escapeXml(c.name):''}</option>`);sel.innerHTML=options.join('');
}
function apexInspectionUpdateUi(){
 if(!apexInspection.active||!trackData)return;const L=trackData.km*1000,set=(id,val)=>{const e=$(id);if(e)e.textContent=val},range=$('inspectorS');if(range){range.max=String(Math.max(1,Math.round(L)));range.value=String(Math.round(apexInspection.s))}if($('inspectorLat'))$('inspectorLat').value=String(apexInspection.lat);if($('inspectorHeight'))$('inspectorHeight').value=String(apexInspection.height);if($('inspectorFov'))$('inspectorFov').value=String(apexInspection.fov);if($('inspectorSpeed'))$('inspectorSpeed').value=String(apexInspection.speed);set('inspectorRenderInfo',rendererBackendId()==='webgl2'?'WebGL · profundidad 3D · cámara orbital':'Canvas · cámara orbital · WebGL no activo');set('inspectorSValue',`${Math.round(apexInspection.s)} / ${Math.round(L)} m`);set('inspectorLatValue',`${apexInspection.lat.toFixed(1)} m`);set('inspectorHeightValue',`${Math.round(apexInspection.height)} m`);set('inspectorFovValue',`${Math.round(apexInspection.fov)}°`);set('inspectorSpeedValue',`${Math.round(apexInspection.speed)} m/s`);set('inspectorPosReadout',`s ${Math.round(apexInspection.s)} m · ${(apexInspection.s/L*100).toFixed(1)}% · altura ${Math.round(apexInspection.height)} m`);const n=apexInspectionNearestPoint();set('inspectorNearest',n?`Punto próximo: ${n.name} · ${Math.round(n.d)} m`:'Punto próximo: —');const j=readStoredCircuitJson(trackData.spec.dir)||apexBaseCircuitJson(trackData.spec.dir)||{},t=j.timing||{},dq=(j.dataQuality||{}).timingControls||t.startFinishQuality||'sin calidad declarada';set('inspectorTimingQuality',`Timing: ${t.controlSchema||'APEX_TIMING'} · ${dq} · cruce de línea física`);const autoFull=$('inspectorAuto');if(autoFull){autoFull.textContent=apexInspection.auto?'⏸ PAUSAR':'▶ RECORRER';autoFull.classList.toggle('primary',apexInspection.auto)}const autoQuick=$('inspectorQuickAuto');if(autoQuick){autoQuick.textContent=apexInspection.auto?'⏸':'▶';autoQuick.setAttribute('aria-label',apexInspection.auto?'Pausar recorrido':'Recorrer circuito');autoQuick.title=apexInspection.auto?'Pausar recorrido':'Recorrer circuito';autoQuick.classList.toggle('primary',apexInspection.auto)}const qt=$('inspectorQuickTiming');if(qt)qt.classList.toggle('primary',!!apexInspection.layers.timing);
}
function apexInspectionUpdate(dt){
 if(!apexInspection.active||!trackData||!player)return;dt=clamp(finite(dt,0),0,.1);const L=trackData.km*1000,forward=(keys.KeyW?1:0)-(keys.KeyS?1:0),side=(keys.KeyD?1:0)-(keys.KeyA?1:0),vertical=(keys.KeyE?1:0)-(keys.KeyQ?1:0),yaw=(keys.ArrowRight?1:0)-(keys.ArrowLeft?1:0),pitch=(keys.ArrowDown?1:0)-(keys.ArrowUp?1:0);
 if(apexInspection.auto){apexInspection.s=mod(apexInspection.s+apexInspection.speed*dt,L);const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(apexInspection.s);apexInspection.freeX=q.x;apexInspection.freeZ=q.z;apexInspection.targetY=q.y;apexInspection.yaw=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz)}
 if(forward||side){const h=apexInspection.yaw,fx=Math.sin(h),fz=Math.cos(h),rx=Math.cos(h),rz=-Math.sin(h),move=apexInspection.speed*dt*(.55+apexInspection.height/85),c={x:apexInspection.freeX,z:apexInspection.freeZ};apexInspection.freeX=c.x+(fx*forward+rx*side)*move;apexInspection.freeZ=c.z+(fz*forward+rz*side)*move;apexInspection.auto=false}
 if(vertical)apexInspection.height=clamp(apexInspection.height+vertical*180*dt,2,5000);if(yaw)apexInspection.yaw+=yaw*1.35*dt;if(pitch)apexInspection.pitchDeg=clamp(apexInspection.pitchDeg+pitch*42*dt,-89,-12);apexInspectionSyncPlayer();apexInspection.uiAcc+=dt;if(apexInspection.uiAcc>.09){apexInspection.uiAcc=0;apexInspectionUpdateUi()}
}

// Los colores son ayudas de inspección del simulador. Los controles intermedios
// usan una junta discreta en pista; el damero se reserva a la meta de vuelta.
function apexTimingControlLabel(c){
 if(c.role==='finish-line')return 'META · S3 + VUELTA';
 if(c.role==='start-line')return 'SALIDA · PARRILLA';
 if(c.role==='sector-line')return `S${c.sector||''} · PARCIAL`;
 if(c.role==='speed-trap')return 'VELOCIDAD · SPEED TRAP';
 return c.name||c.role||'';
}
function apexSegmentContainsTiming(a,b,s,L){
 const length=wrapDeltaS(b.s,a.s,L),d=forwardDistanceS(a.s,s,L);
 return length>0&&d<length&&d>=0;
}
function apexDrawSectorSurface(c,cam,pitch,webgl=false){
 const s=c.s,half=Math.max(3,envProfile(s).half-.06),fill=webgl?webglFillTrackQuad:fillTrackQuad;
 fill([[s-.09,half],[s-.09,-half],[s+.09,-half],[s+.09,half]],cam,pitch,'#ffffff');
}


function apexInspectionLabelStyle(role){role=String(role||'');if(role==='finish-line')return'#ffffff';if(role==='start-line')return'#26dcff';if(role==='sector-line')return'#6ee5ff';if(role==='speed-trap')return'#ffd45f';if(role==='corner')return'#ff8aa0';if(role==='brake')return'#ffcf5a';if(role==='pit')return'#88ffb8';return'#dbe8f3'}
function apexInspectionProjectAtS(s,lat,cam,pitch,yOff=0){const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),nx=Number.isFinite(q.ox)?q.ox:(q.nx||1),nz=Number.isFinite(q.oz)?q.oz:(q.nz||0),wx=q.x+nx*lat,wz=q.z+nz*lat,wy=q.y+yOff,v=worldToCamera(wx,wy,wz,cam);if(v.z<1||v.z>apexInspectionFarDistance())return null;const p=projectLocal(v.x,v.y,v.z,pitch);return p&&Number.isFinite(p.x)&&Number.isFinite(p.y)?{...p,z:v.z,q,nx,nz,wx,wz,wy}:null;}
function apexDrawInspectionLabel(p,text,color){if(!p||p.x<-120||p.x>W+120||p.y<-80||p.y>H+100)return;ctx.save();ctx.font='900 11px system-ui';const tw=Math.min(260,ctx.measureText(text).width+14),x=clamp(p.x-tw*.5,4,W-tw-4),y=clamp(p.y-31,4,H-28);ctx.fillStyle='rgba(4,8,13,.86)';ctx.fillRect(x,y,tw,21);ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.strokeRect(x+.5,y+.5,tw-1,20);ctx.fillStyle=color;ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.fillText(text,x+tw*.5,y+10.5);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x,p.y-8);ctx.lineTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}
function apexDrawInspectionCrossLine(c,cam,pitch,label){
 // La superficie blanca se dibuja en ambos motores. Sin rótulos superpuestos.
}
function apexDrawInspectionOverlays(cam,pitch){
 if(!apexInspection.active||!trackData)return;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.96;const L=trackData.km*1000;
 if(apexInspection.layers.timing){for(const c of apexTimingPointControls(trackData.spec)){const r=String(c.role||'');let label=apexTimingControlLabel(c);apexDrawInspectionCrossLine(c,cam,pitch,label)}}
 if(apexInspection.layers.corners){for(const c of trackData.cornerMarkers||[]){if(!Number.isFinite(+c.s))continue;const p=apexInspectionProjectAtS(+c.s,0,cam,pitch,1.35);if(!p)continue;const txt=`T${c.turn}${c.name&&c.name!==`T${c.turn}`?' · '+c.name:''}`;apexDrawInspectionLabel(p,txt,apexInspectionLabelStyle('corner'));}}
 if(apexInspection.layers.boards){for(const b of (trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.brakeBoards)||[]){if(!Number.isFinite(+b.s))continue;const side=Number(b.side)||1,lat=(envProfile(+b.s).half||6.2)+3.4*side,p=apexInspectionProjectAtS(+b.s,lat,cam,pitch,1.6);if(p)apexDrawInspectionLabel(p,`${b.mark||''} m`,apexInspectionLabelStyle('brake'));}}
 if(apexInspection.layers.pit){const j=readStoredCircuitJson(trackData.spec.dir)||apexBaseCircuitJson(trackData.spec.dir)||{},pit=j.race&&j.race.pit||{};for(const it of [{name:'PIT ENTRY',s:pit.entry},{name:'PIT EXIT',s:pit.exit}])if(Number.isFinite(+it.s)){const p=apexInspectionProjectAtS(mod(+it.s,L),0,cam,pitch,1.8);if(p)apexDrawInspectionLabel(p,it.name,apexInspectionLabelStyle('pit'));}}
 ctx.restore();
}
async function startCircuitInspection(spec,btn){
 if(!spec)return;const old=btn&&btn.textContent;currentMode='inspection';currentSession='inspection';sessionSettings={diff:1,weather:'CLEAR',noRivals:true};apexWeatherInit('CLEAR',spec);apexRaceLoadingShow(spec,'PREPARANDO INSPECCIÓN 3D…',.03);if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='CARGANDO…'}await new Promise(r=>requestAnimationFrame(r));
 try{const td=await loadTrack(spec);trackData={...td,spec};prepareTrackModel();await apexCompileCircuitStatic((label,ratio)=>apexRaceLoadingUpdate(label,.12+clamp(ratio,0,1)*.66));}catch(e){if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent=old||'ABRIR INSPECCIÓN 3D'}apexRaceLoadingRestore();alert('No he podido preparar la inspección 3D.\n\n'+(e&&e.message||e));return;}
 const L=trackData.km*1000,sf=apexCircuitStartFinishS(spec),start=sf.finish,q=poseAt(start),h=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz),team=teamInfo(selectedTeamKey()),car=carCatalog[team.model]||carCatalog.A26;apexInspection.active=true;apexInspection.preparingClosure=true;apexInspection.fullScene=true;apexInspection.layers={timing:true,corners:true,boards:true,pit:true};apexInspection.s=start;apexInspection.lat=0;apexInspection.height=55;apexInspection.fov=80;apexInspection.yaw=h;apexInspection.pitchDeg=-30;apexInspection.speed=45;apexInspection.auto=false;apexInspection.freeX=q.x;apexInspection.freeZ=q.z;apexInspection.targetY=q.y;apexInspection.previousRendererId=rendererBackendId();apexInspection.pointers.clear();apexInspection.gesture=null;apexInspection.uiAcc=0;for(const id of ['insLayerTiming','insLayerCorners','insLayerBoards','insLayerPit']){const e=$(id);if(e)e.checked=true;}
 player={x:q.x,z:q.z,vx:0,vz:0,heading:h,yawRate:0,s:start,total:start,progressS:start,lat:0,speed:0,laps:1,lap:0,ers:100,hp:100,pos:1,steer:0,longG:0,latG:0,trackIndex:q.i,track:q,trackDirDot:1,fovBoost:0,cameraLook:0,cameraGradePitch:0,carSpec:car,carModel:team.chassis,carColor:playerCarColor(),carAccent:playerCarAccent(),teamKey:selectedTeamKey(),damage:freshDamage(),surface:'TRACK',surfaceKind:'ASPHALT',driveNetwork:'TRACK'};rivals=[];mode='inspection';paused=false;raceT=0;resetImpactFX();document.body.classList.remove('menu-open','ins-ui-hidden');document.body.classList.add('apex-inspection');$('menu').classList.add('hidden');$('hud').classList.add('hidden');$('touch').classList.add('hidden');for(const id of ['weekendPanel','finish','pausePanel','optionsPanel','diagPanel','telemetryPanel']){const p=$(id);if(p)p.classList.add('hidden')}$('circuitInspectorHud').classList.remove('hidden');const ctl=$('inspectorControls');if(ctl)ctl.classList.add('insCollapsed');const hint=$('inspectorGestureHint');if(hint){hint.classList.remove('isFaded');clearTimeout(apexInspection._hintTimer);apexInspection._hintTimer=setTimeout(()=>hint.classList.add('isFaded'),3400)}apexInspectionShowUi(3600);$('inspectorCircuitName').textContent=`${spec.country||''} ${spec.name||spec.dir} · ${Math.round(L)} m · SVG/JSON activos`;try{initRendererBackendSelection()}catch(e){activeRenderer=rendererRegistry.canvas2d;updateRendererBackendUi()}setRendererBackend('webgl2',{notify:false,persist:false});resize();apexInspectionFitWholeCircuit();apexInspectionUpdateJumpOptions();apexInspectionSyncPlayer();apexInspectionUpdateUi();last=performance.now();if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent=old||'ABRIR INSPECCIÓN 3D'}try{await apexInspectorPrepareSurfaceClosure();}catch(e){apexInspection.preparingClosure=false;closeCircuitInspection();apexRaceLoadingRestore();alert('No se ha podido cerrar la malla del inspector. '+(e&&e.message||e));return;}apexInspection.preparingClosure=false;await apexRaceLoadingHide();
}
function closeCircuitInspection(){apexInspectionCancelGesture();apexInspection.active=false;setRendererBackend(apexInspection.previousRendererId||'canvas2d',{notify:false,persist:false});apexInspection.auto=false;apexInspection.pointers.clear();apexInspection.gesture=null;clearTimeout(apexInspection._hintTimer);apexInspectionCancelUiTimer();document.body.classList.remove('apex-inspection','ins-drag','ins-ui-hidden');const dock=$('trackEditorDock');goMenu();if(dock){dock.open=true;setTimeout(()=>dock.scrollIntoView({block:'center'}),30)}}
function bindCircuitInspectorTools(){
 const open=$('openCircuitInspectorBtn'),timing=$('inspectTimingBtn'),close=$('closeCircuitInspectorBtn'),status=$('circuitInspectorStatus');if(open)open.onclick=()=>startCircuitInspection(currentSvgToolSpec(),open);if(timing)timing.onclick=async()=>{await startCircuitInspection(currentSvgToolSpec(),timing);if(apexInspection.active){apexInspection.layers.timing=true;const c=apexTimingPointControls(trackData.spec).find(x=>x.role==='sector-line')||apexTimingPointControls(trackData.spec)[0];if(c)apexInspectionSetS(c.s)}};if(close)close.onclick=closeCircuitInspection;

 for(const [id,d] of [['inspectorBack100',-100],['inspectorBack25',-25],['inspectorForward25',25],['inspectorForward100',100]]){const e=$(id);if(e)e.onclick=()=>apexInspectionJump(d)}
 const toggleAuto=()=>{apexInspection.auto=!apexInspection.auto;apexInspectionUpdateUi();apexInspectionShowUi()};for(const id of ['inspectorAuto','inspectorQuickAuto']){const e=$(id);if(e)e.onclick=toggleAuto}
 const reset=$('inspectorResetView');if(reset)reset.onclick=()=>{apexInspectionResetView();apexInspectionShowUi()};const qcenter=$('inspectorQuickCenter');if(qcenter)qcenter.onclick=()=>{apexInspectionResetView();apexInspectionShowUi()};const fit=$('inspectorFitView'),qfit=$('inspectorQuickFit');if(fit)fit.onclick=()=>{apexInspectionFitWholeCircuit();apexInspectionShowUi()};if(qfit)qfit.onclick=()=>{apexInspectionFitWholeCircuit();apexInspectionShowUi()};
 const jump=$('inspectorJump');if(jump)jump.onchange=()=>{if(jump.value!==''){apexInspectionSetS(+jump.value);jump.value=''}};
 for(const [id,key] of [['insLayerTiming','timing'],['insLayerCorners','corners'],['insLayerBoards','boards'],['insLayerPit','pit']]){const e=$(id);if(e)e.onchange=()=>{apexInspection.layers[key]=!!e.checked;apexInspectionUpdateUi()}};
 const quickTiming=$('inspectorQuickTiming');if(quickTiming)quickTiming.onclick=()=>{apexInspection.layers.timing=!apexInspection.layers.timing;const e=$('insLayerTiming');if(e)e.checked=apexInspection.layers.timing;apexInspectionUpdateUi();apexInspectionShowUi()};
 const controls=$('inspectorControls'),showCtl=$('inspectorToggleControls'),hideCtl=$('inspectorHideControls');if(showCtl)showCtl.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();if(!controls)return;document.body.classList.remove('ins-ui-hidden');controls.classList.toggle('insCollapsed');if(controls.classList.contains('insCollapsed'))apexInspectionScheduleUiHide(2200);else apexInspectionCancelUiTimer()};if(hideCtl)hideCtl.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();if(controls)controls.classList.add('insCollapsed');apexInspectionScheduleUiHide(1800)};
 apexInspectionBindOrbitControls($('game'));
 window.apexStartCircuitInspection=dir=>{const spec=dir?calendarSpecById(dir):currentSvgToolSpec();return startCircuitInspection(spec,open)};window.apexCloseCircuitInspection=closeCircuitInspection;
 if(status)status.title='La inspección usa la geometría compilada de carrera y una cámara libre tipo Google Earth; no es una previsualización alternativa.';
}

// Orbital inspector: target and terrain remain in world coordinates.
function apexInspectionScreenGround(px,py){
 const cam=apexInspectionDriverCamera(),f=(W*.5)/Math.tan(sceneHFov()*.5),ux=(px-W*.5)/f,uy=-(py-H*.5)/f,c=Math.cos(cam.pitchRad),sn=Math.sin(cam.pitchRad),ry=uy*c+sn,rz=-uy*sn+c;
 if(ry>=-.00001)return null;
 const t=((Number.isFinite(apexInspection.targetY)?apexInspection.targetY:cam.surfaceY)-cam.y)/ry;
 if(!Number.isFinite(t)||t<=0)return null;
 return{x:cam.x+t*(cam.nx*ux+cam.tx*rz),z:cam.z+t*(cam.nz*ux+cam.tz*rz)};
}
function apexInspectionZoomAt(factor,x=W*.5,y=H*.5){
 const before=apexInspectionScreenGround(x,y);apexInspection.height=clamp(apexInspection.height*factor,2,5000);
 const after=apexInspectionScreenGround(x,y);if(before&&after){apexInspection.freeX+=before.x-after.x;apexInspection.freeZ+=before.z-after.z;}
 apexInspection.auto=false;apexInspectionSyncPlayer();apexInspectionUpdateUi();
}
function apexInspectionOrbitPixels(dx,dy){
 apexInspection.yaw=mod(apexInspection.yaw-dx*.006+Math.PI,Math.PI*2)-Math.PI;
 apexInspection.pitchDeg=clamp(apexInspection.pitchDeg+dy*.22,-89,-12);apexInspection.auto=false;apexInspectionSyncPlayer();
}
function apexInspectionCancelGesture(){
 const cv=$('game');for(const id of apexInspection.pointers.keys()){try{if(cv.hasPointerCapture(id))cv.releasePointerCapture(id);}catch(_){}}
 apexInspection.pointers.clear();apexInspection.gesture=null;document.body.classList.remove('ins-drag');apexInspection.drag=false;
}
function apexInspectionBindOrbitControls(cv){
 if(!cv||cv.dataset.inspectorEarthBound)return;cv.dataset.inspectorEarthBound='1';
 const metrics=()=>{const a=[...apexInspection.pointers.values()];if(a.length<2)return null;const dx=a[1].x-a[0].x,dy=a[1].y-a[0].y;return{dist:Math.max(8,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),angle:Math.atan2(dy,dx),x:(a[0].x+a[1].x)*.5,y:(a[0].y+a[1].y)*.5};};
 cv.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>{if(apexInspection.active)e.preventDefault();});
 cv.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
 if(!apexInspection.active||e.button>2)return;e.preventDefault();apexInspection.auto=false;apexInspectionCancelUiTimer();
 apexInspection.pointers.set(e.pointerId,{x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,mode:e.button===2||e.button===1||e.shiftKey?'orbit':'pan'});
 apexInspection.gesture=metrics();document.body.classList.add('ins-drag');try{cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){}
 },{passive:false});
 cv.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
 if(!apexInspection.active)return;const p=apexInspection.pointers.get(e.pointerId);if(!p)return;e.preventDefault();const dx=e.clientX-p.x,dy=e.clientY-p.y;p.x=e.clientX;p.y=e.clientY;
 if(apexInspection.pointers.size===1){if(p.mode==='orbit'||e.shiftKey)apexInspectionOrbitPixels(dx,dy);else apexInspectionPanPixels(dx,dy);}
 else{const m=metrics(),g=apexInspection.gesture;if(g&&m){apexInspectionZoomAt(g.dist/m.dist,m.x,m.y);const da=Math.atan2(Math.sin(m.angle-g.angle),Math.cos(m.angle-g.angle));apexInspection.yaw-=da;apexInspectionOrbitPixels((m.x-g.x)*.35,(m.y-g.y)*.65);}apexInspection.gesture=m;}
 apexInspectionUpdateUi();
 },{passive:false});
 const end=e=>{if(!apexInspection.pointers.has(e.pointerId))return;apexInspection.pointers.delete(e.pointerId);apexInspection.gesture=metrics();try{if(cv.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId))cv.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){}if(!apexInspection.pointers.size){document.body.classList.remove('ins-drag');apexInspectionShowUi(4000);}};
 for(const event of ['pointerup','pointercancel','lostpointercapture'])cv.addEventListener(event,end);
 cv.addEventListener('wheel',e=>{if(!apexInspection.active)return;e.preventDefault();const d=e.deltaY*(e.deltaMode===1?16:e.deltaMode===2?H:1);apexInspectionZoomAt(Math.exp(clamp(d,-400,400)*.0015),e.clientX,e.clientY);apexInspectionShowUi(4000);},{passive:false});
 cv.addEventListener('dblclick',e=>{if(!apexInspection.active)return;e.preventDefault();const hit=apexInspectionScreenGround(e.clientX,e.clientY);if(hit){apexInspection.freeX=hit.x;apexInspection.freeZ=hit.z;}apexInspectionZoomAt(.62);apexInspectionShowUi(4000);});
 addEventListener('blur',apexInspectionCancelGesture);
}

// v4.9: clip static inspector surfaces against the same paved ribbon used by rendering.
function apexInspectorPolyArea(poly){let a=0;for(let i=0,j=poly.length-1;i<poly.length;j=i++)a+=poly[j].x*poly[i].z-poly[i].x*poly[j].z;return a*.5;}
function apexInspectorPolyBounds(poly){return{minX:Math.min(...poly.map(p=>p.x)),maxX:Math.max(...poly.map(p=>p.x)),minZ:Math.min(...poly.map(p=>p.z)),maxZ:Math.max(...poly.map(p=>p.z)),minY:Math.min(...poly.map(p=>p.y)),maxY:Math.max(...poly.map(p=>p.y))};}
function apexInspectorFootHeight(foot,p){
 const a=foot.poly[0],b=foot.poly[1],c=foot.poly[2],d=(b.z-c.z)*(a.x-c.x)+(c.x-b.x)*(a.z-c.z);
 if(Math.abs(d)<1e-10)return a.y;
 const u=((b.z-c.z)*(p.x-c.x)+(c.x-b.x)*(p.z-c.z))/d,v=((c.z-a.z)*(p.x-c.x)+(a.x-c.x)*(p.z-c.z))/d;
 return a.y*u+b.y*v+c.y*(1-u-v);
}
function apexInspectorHalfPoly(poly,a,b,side,inside,foot=null){
 const distance=p=>side*((b.x-a.x)*(p.z-a.z)-(b.z-a.z)*(p.x-a.x)),out=[];
 for(let i=0,j=poly.length-1;i<poly.length;j=i++){
 const A=poly[j],B=poly[i],da=distance(A),db=distance(B),okA=inside?da>=0:da<=0,okB=inside?db>=0:db<=0;
 if(okA!==okB){const t=da/(da-db),p={x:A.x+(B.x-A.x)*t,y:A.y+(B.y-A.y)*t,z:A.z+(B.z-A.z)*t};if(foot)p.y=apexInspectorFootHeight(foot,p)-.004;out.push(p);}
 if(okB)out.push(B);
 }
 return out.length>=3&&Math.abs(apexInspectorPolyArea(out))>1e-8?out:[];
}
function apexInspectorSubtractFoot(poly,foot,snap=false){
 const bound=apexInspectorPolyBounds(poly),f=foot.bounds;
 if(bound.maxX<=f.minX||bound.minX>=f.maxX||bound.maxZ<=f.minZ||bound.minZ>=f.maxZ)return[poly];
 let inside=poly;const out=[],side=Math.sign(apexInspectorPolyArea(foot.poly));
 if(!side)return[poly];
 for(let i=0;i<foot.poly.length&&inside.length;i++){
 const a=foot.poly[i],b=foot.poly[(i+1)%foot.poly.length],outer=apexInspectorHalfPoly(inside,a,b,side,false,snap?foot:null);
 if(outer.length)out.push(outer);inside=apexInspectorHalfPoly(inside,a,b,side,true,snap?foot:null);
 }
 return out;
}
function apexInspectorSurfaceIndex(){
 if(trackData._inspectionSurfaceIndex)return trackData._inspectionSurfaceIndex;
 const identity={x:0,y:0,z:0,nx:1,nz:0,tx:0,tz:1},steps=collectRoadBranch(1,identity,0),size=32,buckets=new Map(),roads=new Map(),footprints=[];
 const add=(poly,kind,structure)=>{
 if(Math.abs(apexInspectorPolyArea(poly))<1e-8)return;
 const bounds=apexInspectorPolyBounds(poly),foot={poly,bounds,kind,structure};footprints.push(foot);
 for(let x=Math.floor(bounds.minX/size);x<=Math.floor(bounds.maxX/size);x++)for(let z=Math.floor(bounds.minZ/size);z<=Math.floor(bounds.maxZ/size);z++){const key=x+','+z;if(!buckets.has(key))buckets.set(key,[]);buckets.get(key).push(foot);}
 };
 const quad=(A,B,C,D,kind,structure)=>{add([A,B,C],kind,structure);add([A,C,D],kind,structure);};
 for(let i=0;i<steps.length-1;i++){
 const a=steps[i],b=steps[i+1],A=a.v,B=b.v,structure=!!(a.env.tunnel||b.env.tunnel||apexPhase1StructureAtWorld(a.q.x,a.q.z,a.s)||apexPhase1StructureAtWorld(b.q.x,b.q.z,b.s));
 // Asphalt + kerbs are protected from all neighbouring runoff ribbons.
 quad(a.curb.left?A.lo:A.l,b.curb.left?B.lo:B.l,b.curb.right?B.ro:B.r,a.curb.right?A.ro:A.r,'ROAD',structure);
 quad(a.curb.left?A.lo:A.l,b.curb.left?B.lo:B.l,b.curb.right?B.ro:B.r,a.curb.right?A.ro:A.r,'PAVED',structure);
 const left=[A.lx,a.curb.left?A.lo:A.l,b.curb.left?B.lo:B.l,B.lx],right=[a.curb.right?A.ro:A.r,A.rx,B.rx,b.curb.right?B.ro:B.r];
 for(const poly of [left,right])quad(poly[0],poly[1],poly[2],poly[3],'PAVED',structure);
 roads.set(a.s,{left,right,env:a.env,structure});
 }
 return trackData._inspectionSurfaceIndex={size,buckets,roads,footprints,runoffs:new Map()};
}
function apexInspectorSurfaceCandidates(poly){
 const idx=apexInspectorSurfaceIndex(),b=apexInspectorPolyBounds(poly),found=new Set();
 for(let x=Math.floor(b.minX/idx.size);x<=Math.floor(b.maxX/idx.size);x++)for(let z=Math.floor(b.minZ/idx.size);z<=Math.floor(b.maxZ/idx.size);z++)for(const f of idx.buckets.get(x+','+z)||[])found.add(f);
 return [...found].filter(f=>b.minX<f.bounds.maxX&&b.maxX>f.bounds.minX&&b.minZ<f.bounds.maxZ&&b.maxZ>f.bounds.minZ);
}
function apexInspectorCutSurface(poly,kind){
 const b=apexInspectorPolyBounds(poly),candidates=apexInspectorSurfaceCandidates(poly).filter(f=>kind==='TERRAIN'?f.kind==='PAVED'&&!f.structure:f.kind==='ROAD'&&f.bounds.minY<=b.maxY+1&&f.bounds.maxY>=b.minY-1);
 let out=[poly];
 for(const foot of candidates){const next=[];for(const part of out)next.push(...apexInspectorSubtractFoot(part,foot,kind==='TERRAIN'));out=next;if(!out.length)break;}
 return out;
}
function apexInspectorRunoffPolys(s){
 const idx=apexInspectorSurfaceIndex();if(idx.runoffs.has(s))return idx.runoffs.get(s);
 const step=idx.roads.get(s);if(!step)return[];
 const out=[];
 for(const [quad,right] of [[step.left,false],[step.right,true]])for(const tri of [[quad[0],quad[1],quad[2]],[quad[0],quad[2],quad[3]]])for(const poly of apexInspectorCutSurface(tri,'RUNOFF'))out.push({poly,color:surfaceColor(step.env.runoffSurface,right)});
 idx.runoffs.set(s,out);return out;
}
function apexInspectorDrawRunoff(seg,cam,pitch,webgl){
 for(const item of apexInspectorRunoffPolys(seg.a.s)){const poly=item.poly.map(p=>worldToCamera(p.x,p.y,p.z,cam));if(webgl)webglPushCamPoly(poly,pitch,item.color,.58);else drawCamPoly(poly,pitch,item.color,.58);}
}
function apexInspectorTerrainCellPolys(g,ix,iz){
 if(!g._inspectionClosedCells||g._inspectionClosedOwner!==trackData){g._inspectionClosedCells=new Map();g._inspectionClosedOwner=trackData;}
 const key=iz*g.nx+ix;if(g._inspectionClosedCells.has(key))return g._inspectionClosedCells.get(key);
 const point=(x,z)=>({x:Math.min(g.maxX,g.minX+x*g.cell),z:Math.min(g.maxZ,g.minZ+z*g.cell),y:g.inside[z*g.nx+x]?g.vals[z*g.nx+x]:g.baseLevelM});
 const A=point(ix,iz),B=point(ix+1,iz),C=point(ix+1,iz+1),D=point(ix,iz+1);
 const kind=apexTerrainCellNeedsDetail(g,(A.x+C.x)*.5,(A.z+C.z)*.5,g.cell);
 const structural=kind==='PORTAL'||kind==='TUNNEL';
 const source=structural?apexTerrainFinePatch(g,ix,iz,kind):[[A,B,C],[A,C,D]],out=[];
 for(const poly of source)out.push(...apexInspectorCutSurface(poly,'TERRAIN'));
 g._inspectionClosedCells.set(key,out);return out;
}
async function apexInspectorPrepareSurfaceClosure(){
 const g=apexPlateTerrainGrid(),idx=apexInspectorSurfaceIndex();let chunk=performance.now(),runoffDone=0;
 for(const key of idx.roads.keys()){apexInspectorRunoffPolys(key);runoffDone++;if(performance.now()-chunk>12){apexRaceLoadingUpdate('RECORTANDO ESCAPATORIAS…',.88+.04*runoffDone/idx.roads.size);await apexCompilerYield();chunk=performance.now();}}
 if(!g)return;
 let done=0,total=(g.nx-1)*(g.nz-1);
 for(let iz=0;iz<g.nz-1;iz++)for(let ix=0;ix<g.nx-1;ix++){
 if(g.mask[iz*(g.nx-1)+ix])apexInspectorTerrainCellPolys(g,ix,iz);done++;
 if(performance.now()-chunk>12){apexRaceLoadingUpdate('CERRANDO TERRENO Y ESCAPATORIAS…',.92+.06*done/total);await apexCompilerYield();chunk=performance.now();}
 }
}

}

function buildRaceRoster(playerTeam,seatUid=null){
 let removed=false;
 const roster=[];
 for(const d of driverRoster){
   const takeSeat=seatUid ? d.uid===seatUid : (d.team===playerTeam&&!removed);
   if(takeSeat){removed=true;continue}
   roster.push({...d});
 }
 return roster;
}

function shadeColor(hex,amt=0){
 const m=/^#?([0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(hex||""); if(!m)return hex||"#36dff1";
 let n=parseInt(m[1],16),r=(n>>16)&255,g=(n>>8)&255,b=n&255;
 r=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,r+amt)); g=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,g+amt)); b=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,b+amt));
 return "#"+((1<<24)+(r<<16)+(g<<8)+b).toString(16).slice(1);
}

function defaultSave(){return{schema:4,round:0,points:{PLAYER:0},wins:0,seasonTeam:null,seasonSeatUid:null,practiceRecords:{},seasonWeekend:null,lastSeasonResult:null}}
let save=(()=>{
 try{
   const s=JSON.parse(appStorage.getItem(SAVE_KEY));
   if(s&&(s.schema===2||s.schema===3||s.schema===4)){
     s.points=s.points&&typeof s.points==="object"&&!Array.isArray(s.points)?s.points:{PLAYER:0};
     if(!Number.isFinite(s.points.PLAYER))s.points.PLAYER=0;
     s.schema=4;s.practiceRecords=s.practiceRecords&&typeof s.practiceRecords==="object"?s.practiceRecords:{};
     if(!("seasonWeekend" in s))s.seasonWeekend=null;if(!("lastSeasonResult" in s))s.lastSeasonResult=null;
     return s;
   }
 }catch(e){}
 return defaultSave();
})();
const persist=()=>{try{appStorage.setItem(SAVE_KEY,JSON.stringify(save))}catch(e){}};
let trackData=null,player=null,rivals=[],mode="menu",currentMode="quick",paused=false,countdown=0,raceT=0,lapStart=0,best=Infinity,finishOrder=[],last=performance.now(),keys={},frameShake=0,raceWeather="DRY",pendingRace=null,renderLimitGuides=false,sessionLimit=Infinity,sessionExpired=false;
let sessionFastestLap={id:null,time:Infinity,flashUntil:0};
let sessionLapLog=[],finishFlow=null,seasonAwardContext=null;
let currentSession="race",sessionSettings={diff:1,weather:"CLEAR",noRivals:false},weekendContext=null;
const WEEKEND_SESSIONS=["fp1","fp2","fp3","qualifying","race"];
let impactFX={side:0,forward:0,pitch:0,roll:0,vSide:0,vForward:0,vPitch:0,vRoll:0};
let impactParticles=[];
let gyro={enabled:false,raw:0,center:0,steer:0,last:0,invert:1,source:"—",listening:false,eventCount:0,motionCount:0,alpha:null,beta:null,gamma:null,motionTilt:0,motionLast:0,pedalRaw:0,pedalCenter:0,pedal:0,throttle:0,brake:0,pedalDead:3.0,pedalRange:20,screenRoll:0,screenPitch:0,orientationAngle:0,lastScreenAngle:null,orientationType:"unknown"};

let audio={ctx:null,master:null,compressor:null,engine1:null,engine2:null,engineGain:null,engine2Gain:null,engineFilter:null,windGain:null,surfaceGain:null,surfaceFilter:null,rivalGain:null,rivalOsc:null,rivalPan:null,windSrc:null,surfaceSrc:null,rainGain:null,rainSrc:null,tyreGain:null,tyreSrc:null,muted:false,lastGear:0,lastCountdown:99,impactCooldown:0,popCooldown:0,limiterPhase:0,coreReady:false,optionalReady:false,error:"",startedAt:0,warmed:false,shiftDip:0,blip:0};


function makeNoiseBuffer(ac,seconds=2){
 const len=Math.max(1,Math.floor(ac.sampleRate*seconds)),b=ac.createBuffer(1,len,ac.sampleRate),d=b.getChannelData(0);
 let last=0;
 for(let i=0;i<len;i++){const white=Math.random()*2-1;last=last*.72+white*.28;d[i]=last}
 return b;
}
async function resumeAudio(){
 if(!audio.ctx)return false;
 try{
   if(audio.ctx.state==="suspended")await audio.ctx.resume();
   return audio.ctx.state==="running";
 }catch(e){audio.error=String(e&&e.message||e);return false}
}
function initAudio(){
 if(audio.ctx){
   resumeAudio();
   return audio.coreReady;
 }
 const AC=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;
 if(!AC){audio.error="AudioContext no disponible";return false}
 try{
   const ac=new AC();

   const master=ac.createGain();
   const comp=ac.createDynamicsCompressor();
   master.gain.value=0;
   comp.threshold.value=-24;
   comp.knee.value=18;
   comp.ratio.value=10;
   comp.attack.value=.004;
   comp.release.value=.18;
   master.connect(comp);
   comp.connect(ac.destination);
   // Motor principal: ondas menos agresivas y filtro más cerrado.
   const e1=ac.createOscillator(),e2=ac.createOscillator();
   const eg=ac.createGain(),e2g=ac.createGain(),ef=ac.createBiquadFilter();
   e1.type="triangle";e2.type="sine";
   e1.frequency.value=78;e2.frequency.value=156;
   eg.gain.value=0;e2g.gain.value=0;
   ef.type="lowpass";ef.frequency.value=1100;ef.Q.value=.25;
   e1.connect(eg);e2.connect(e2g);eg.connect(ef);e2g.connect(ef);ef.connect(master);
   e1.start();e2.start();

   Object.assign(audio,{
     ctx:ac,master,compressor:comp,engine1:e1,engine2:e2,
     engineGain:eg,engine2Gain:e2g,engineFilter:ef,
     coreReady:true,error:"",startedAt:ac.currentTime,warmed:false
   });

   // Capas opcionales: todas con ganancias iniciales 0 y volúmenes moderados.
   try{
     const wind=ac.createBufferSource(),wg=ac.createGain(),wf=ac.createBiquadFilter();
     wind.buffer=makeNoiseBuffer(ac,.8);wind.loop=true;wf.type="highpass";wf.frequency.value=1100;wg.gain.value=0;
     wind.connect(wf);wf.connect(wg);wg.connect(master);wind.start();
     audio.windSrc=wind;audio.windGain=wg;
   }catch(e){}

   try{
     const surf=ac.createBufferSource(),sg=ac.createGain(),sf=ac.createBiquadFilter();
     surf.buffer=makeNoiseBuffer(ac,.7);surf.loop=true;sf.type="bandpass";sf.frequency.value=430;sf.Q.value=.65;sg.gain.value=0;
     surf.connect(sf);sf.connect(sg);sg.connect(master);surf.start();
     audio.surfaceSrc=surf;audio.surfaceGain=sg;audio.surfaceFilter=sf;
   }catch(e){}

   try{
     const rain=ac.createBufferSource(),rag=ac.createGain(),raf=ac.createBiquadFilter();
     rain.buffer=makeNoiseBuffer(ac,.9);rain.loop=true;raf.type="highpass";raf.frequency.value=1700;rag.gain.value=0;
     rain.connect(raf);raf.connect(rag);rag.connect(master);rain.start();
     audio.rainSrc=rain;audio.rainGain=rag;
   }catch(e){}

   try{
     const tyre=ac.createBufferSource(),tg=ac.createGain(),tf=ac.createBiquadFilter();
     tyre.buffer=makeNoiseBuffer(ac,.7);tyre.loop=true;tf.type="bandpass";tf.frequency.value=1180;tf.Q.value=1.05;tg.gain.value=0;
     tyre.connect(tf);tf.connect(tg);tg.connect(master);tyre.start();
     audio.tyreSrc=tyre;audio.tyreGain=tg;
   }catch(e){}

   try{
     const ro=ac.createOscillator(),rg=ac.createGain(),rf=ac.createBiquadFilter();
     ro.type="triangle";rg.gain.value=0;rf.type="lowpass";rf.frequency.value=900;
     ro.connect(rf);rf.connect(rg);
     if(typeof ac.createStereoPanner==="function"){
       const rp=ac.createStereoPanner();rg.connect(rp);rp.connect(master);audio.rivalPan=rp;
     }else rg.connect(master);
     ro.start();audio.rivalOsc=ro;audio.rivalGain=rg;
   }catch(e){}

   audio.optionalReady=!!(audio.windGain||audio.surfaceGain||audio.rainGain||audio.tyreGain||audio.rivalGain);
   resumeAudio();
   return true;
 }catch(e){
   audio.error=String(e&&e.message||e);
   audio.coreReady=false;
   return false;
 }
}
function setAudioMuted(v){
 audio.muted=!!v;
 if(audio.master&&audio.ctx){
   const t=audio.ctx.currentTime;
   audio.master.gain.cancelScheduledValues(t);
   audio.master.gain.setValueAtTime(audio.master.gain.value,t);
   audio.master.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(audio.muted?0:.46,t+(audio.muted?.10:.55));
 }
 $("sound").textContent=audio.muted?"🔇":"🔊";
 $("sound").classList.toggle("on",!audio.muted);
}
function toggleSound(){
 initAudio();
 if(audio.ctx&&audio.ctx.state==="suspended"){
   try{audio.ctx.resume()}catch(e){}
 }
 setAudioMuted(!audio.muted);
 if(!audio.muted)soundTest();
 updateDiag();
}
function engineRPM(){
 if(!player)return 1000;
 const kph=finite(player.speed,0)*3.6,g=gear();
 if(g==="N")return 1100;
 if(g==="R")return clamp(1300+kph*52,1300,8500);
 const ratios=[0,105,77,55,43,35,30,26,23];
 return clamp(1000+kph*(ratios[g]||23)*1.28,1000,12100);
}
function nearestRivalDistance(){
 if(!player)return null;
 const a=carAxes();let best=null;
 for(const r of rivals){
   const w=rivalWorld(r),dx=w.x-player.x,dz=w.z-player.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz),lat=dx*a.rx+dz*a.rz;
   if(d<55&&(!best||d<best.d))best={r,d,lat};
 }
 return best;
}
function updateAudio(dt){
 if(!audio.ctx||!audio.coreReady||!player)return;
 try{
   const ac=audio.ctx,t=ac.currentTime,active=(mode==="race"||mode==="countdown")&&!paused&&!audio.muted;
   if(!active){
     audio.engineGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(0,t,.08);
     if(audio.engine2Gain)audio.engine2Gain.gain.setTargetAtTime(0,t,.08);
     if(audio.windGain)audio.windGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(0,t,.10);
     if(audio.surfaceGain)audio.surfaceGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(0,t,.08);
     if(audio.rivalGain)audio.rivalGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(0,t,.08);
     if(audio.rainGain)audio.rainGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(0,t,.08);
     if(audio.tyreGain)audio.tyreGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(0,t,.08);
     return;
   }

   const sinceStart=t-audio.startedAt;
   const warm=clamp(sinceStart/1.5,0,1);
   audio.warmed=warm>=.98;

   const rpm=engineRPM();
   const throttle=player.throttle||0;
   const braking=(player.brakeInput||0)>.08;
   const v=clamp(player.speed/100,0,1.08);
   const nearLimiter=audio.warmed&&mode==="race"&&raceT>2&&rpm>11900&&throttle&&!braking;
   audio.limiterPhase+=dt*(nearLimiter?30:5);
   const limiterCut=nearLimiter&&Math.sin(audio.limiterPhase)>0.58;
   const load=throttle?1:(braking?.08:.18);

   const f=clamp(47+rpm*.0154+player.speed*.075,73,258)*(limiterCut?.90:1);
   audio.engine1.frequency.setTargetAtTime(f,t,.030);
   if(audio.engine2)audio.engine2.frequency.setTargetAtTime(f*(2.02+Math.min(.045,load*.030)),t,.030);
   if(audio.engineFilter)audio.engineFilter.frequency.setTargetAtTime(850+load*570+player.speed*3.0,t,.070);

   audio.shiftDip=Math.max(0,audio.shiftDip-dt);
   audio.blip=Math.max(0,audio.blip-dt);
   const shiftFactor=audio.shiftDip>0?.72:1;
   const blipFactor=audio.blip>0?1.10:1;
   if(audio.shiftDip>0){
     audio.engine1.frequency.setTargetAtTime(f*.91,t,.022);
     if(audio.engine2)audio.engine2.frequency.setTargetAtTime(f*1.82,t,.022);
   }else if(audio.blip>0){
     audio.engine1.frequency.setTargetAtTime(f*1.06,t,.022);
   }
   const core1=(.018+.049*load)*warm*(limiterCut?.42:1)*shiftFactor*blipFactor;
   const core2=(.004+.013*load)*warm*(limiterCut?.35:1)*shiftFactor;
   audio.engineGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(core1,t,.040);
   if(audio.engine2Gain)audio.engine2Gain.gain.setTargetAtTime(core2,t,.040);

   if(audio.windGain)audio.windGain.gain.setTargetAtTime((.003+.045*v*v+(player.drs?.008:0))*warm,t,.10);

   let sg=0;
   if(player.surface==="KERB")sg=.028*(.55+.45*Math.abs(Math.sin(raceT*34)));
   else if(player.surfaceKind==="GRAVEL")sg=.070+.022*clamp(player.speed/70,0,1);
   else if(player.surfaceKind==="SAND")sg=.055+.018*clamp(player.speed/65,0,1);
   else if(player.surfaceKind==="GRASS")sg=.038+.014*clamp(player.speed/75,0,1);
   else if(player.surface==="RUNOFF")sg=.011;
   if(audio.surfaceGain)audio.surfaceGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(sg*v*warm,t,.05);
   if(audio.surfaceFilter){
     const sfreq=player.surfaceKind==="GRAVEL"?310:player.surfaceKind==="SAND"?220:player.surfaceKind==="GRASS"?610:player.surface==="KERB"?760:430;
     audio.surfaceFilter.frequency.setTargetAtTime(sfreq,t,.08);
   }

   const wet=apexWeatherIsWet(),rainI=apexWeatherRainIntensity();
   if(audio.rainGain)audio.rainGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(rainI>(.02)?(.003+.040*rainI+.020*v*v*rainI)*warm:0,t,.12);

   const slipMag=Math.max(Math.abs(player.slipF||0),Math.abs(player.slipR||0));
   const lock=!!(player.lockF||player.lockR);
   const scrub=Math.max(0,(slipMag-.025)*.26);
   const tyreLevel=clamp(scrub+(lock?.030:0),0,.050)*(wet?.82:1)*warm;
   if(audio.tyreGain)audio.tyreGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(tyreLevel,t,.04);

   const near=nearestRivalDistance();
   if(near){
     const rv=clamp(near.r.speed/100,0,1),rf=78+rv*125;
     if(audio.rivalOsc)audio.rivalOsc.frequency.setTargetAtTime(rf,t,.06);
     if(audio.rivalGain)audio.rivalGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(clamp((42-near.d)/42,0,.032)*warm,t,.09);
     if(audio.rivalPan)audio.rivalPan.pan.setTargetAtTime(clamp(near.lat/3.0,-1,1),t,.07);
   }else if(audio.rivalGain)audio.rivalGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(0,t,.10);

   const g=gear(),gNum=typeof g==="number"?g:Number(g),lastNum=typeof audio.lastGear==="number"?audio.lastGear:Number(audio.lastGear);
   if(audio.warmed&&mode==="race"&&raceT>1.5&&Number.isFinite(gNum)&&Number.isFinite(lastNum)&&gNum!==lastNum&&g!=="N"&&audio.lastGear!==0){
     const up=gNum>lastNum;
     soundShift(up);
     if(!up)exhaustPop(.35);
   }
   audio.lastGear=g;

   audio.impactCooldown=Math.max(0,audio.impactCooldown-dt);
   audio.popCooldown=Math.max(0,audio.popCooldown-dt);

   if(audio.warmed&&mode==="race"&&raceT>3&&!throttle&&!braking&&rpm>9800&&audio.popCooldown<=0&&Math.random()<dt*.55){
     exhaustPop(.20+.15*Math.random());
   }
 }catch(e){
   audio.error="updateAudio: "+String(e&&e.message||e);
   if(audio.engineGain&&audio.ctx)audio.engineGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(0,audio.ctx.currentTime,.05);
 }
}
function oneShot(freq,dur=.08,type="sine",gain=.035,endFreq=null){
 if(!audio.ctx||audio.muted||!audio.master)return;
 const ac=audio.ctx,t=ac.currentTime,o=ac.createOscillator(),g=ac.createGain();
 o.type=type;o.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.max(20,freq),t);
 if(endFreq)o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(20,endFreq),t+dur);
 g.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,t);
 g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(.0002,gain),t+.012);
 g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+dur);
 o.connect(g);g.connect(audio.master);o.start(t);o.stop(t+dur+.025);
}
function soundTest(){
 if(!audio.coreReady)initAudio();
 resumeAudio();
 if(!audio.ctx||!audio.master)return;
 setAudioMuted(false);
 oneShot(330,.10,"sine",.024,440);
 setTimeout(()=>oneShot(440,.12,"sine",.022,550),150);
}

function soundShift(up=true){
 audio.shiftDip=up?.095:.060;
 audio.blip=up?0:.11;
 oneShot(up?112:96,.045,"sine",.009,up?82:118);
}
function exhaustPop(strength=.7){
 if(!audio.ctx||audio.muted||audio.popCooldown>0)return;
 audio.popCooldown=.055;
 const ac=audio.ctx,t=ac.currentTime,src=ac.createBufferSource(),g=ac.createGain(),f=ac.createBiquadFilter();
 src.buffer=makeNoiseBuffer(ac,.055);f.type="highpass";f.frequency.value=1300;
 g.gain.setValueAtTime(.055*strength,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+.05);
 src.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(audio.master);src.start(t);src.stop(t+.06);
 oneShot(135,.045,"square",.018*strength,70);
}
function soundScrape(strength=.5){
 if(!audio.ctx||audio.muted||audio.impactCooldown>0)return;
 audio.impactCooldown=.060;
 const s=clamp(strength,.18,1.10),ac=audio.ctx,t=ac.currentTime;
 const src=ac.createBufferSource(),g=ac.createGain(),f=ac.createBiquadFilter();
 src.buffer=makeNoiseBuffer(ac,.095);f.type="bandpass";f.frequency.value=980;f.Q.value=.95;
 g.gain.setValueAtTime(.014*s,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+.09);
 src.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(audio.master);src.start(t);src.stop(t+.10);
 oneShot(420,.028,"triangle",.0065*s,0);
}
function soundImpact(strength=1){
 if(!audio.ctx||audio.muted||audio.impactCooldown>0)return;
 audio.impactCooldown=.10;
 const s=clamp(strength,.35,1.45),ac=audio.ctx,t=ac.currentTime;
 const low=ac.createBufferSource(),lowG=ac.createGain(),lowF=ac.createBiquadFilter();
 low.buffer=makeNoiseBuffer(ac,.13);lowF.type="lowpass";lowF.frequency.value=185;lowG.gain.setValueAtTime(.105*s,t);lowG.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+.115);
 low.connect(lowF);lowF.connect(lowG);lowG.connect(audio.master);low.start(t);low.stop(t+.14);
 const crack=ac.createBufferSource(),crackG=ac.createGain(),crackF=ac.createBiquadFilter();
 crack.buffer=makeNoiseBuffer(ac,.05);crackF.type="bandpass";crackF.frequency.value=1280;crackF.Q.value=.9;crackG.gain.setValueAtTime(.014*s,t+.008);crackG.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+.048);
 crack.connect(crackF);crackF.connect(crackG);crackG.connect(audio.master);crack.start(t+.008);crack.stop(t+.055);
 oneShot(58,.082,"sine",.028*s,28);
 oneShot(168,.035,"triangle",.010*s,45);
 if(navigator.vibrate)navigator.vibrate(Math.round(18+strength*26));
}
function soundBeep(n){
 if(!audio.ctx||audio.muted)return;
 oneShot(n===0?720:390,n===0?.16:.075,"sine",n===0?.032:.018,n===0?860:360);
}
// v5.230.40 · APEX no contiene geometría ni parámetros de un circuito concreto. SVG + JSON son las fuentes autoritativas.
function parseSvgGeometry(svgText,km,direction,startHint=null){
 const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString(svgText,"image/svg+xml"),paths=[...doc.querySelectorAll("path")],p0=paths[0],d=p0&&p0.getAttribute("d");if(!d)throw Error("SVG sin geometría");
 const ns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg",host=document.createElementNS(ns,"svg"),p=document.createElementNS(ns,"path");
 host.setAttribute("width","1");host.setAttribute("height","1");host.style.cssText="position:fixed;left:-9999px;top:-9999px;width:1px;height:1px;opacity:0;pointer-events:none";p.setAttribute("d",d);if(p0&&p0.hasAttribute('transform'))p.setAttribute('transform',p0.getAttribute('transform'));host.appendChild(p);
 let resolvedStart=startHint;
 if(!resolvedStart&&paths.length>1){
   const marker=paths[1],markerD=marker&&marker.getAttribute("d");
   if(markerD){
     try{
       const mp=document.createElementNS(ns,"path");mp.setAttribute("d",markerD);if(marker.hasAttribute('transform'))mp.setAttribute('transform',marker.getAttribute('transform'));host.appendChild(mp);document.body.appendChild(host);
       const bb=mp.getBBox(),mx=bb.x+bb.width*.5,my=bb.y+bb.height*.5,tm=mp.transform&&mp.transform.baseVal&&mp.transform.baseVal.consolidate?mp.transform.baseVal.consolidate():null;
       let rx=mx,ry=my;if(tm&&tm.matrix){const m=tm.matrix;rx=m.a*mx+m.c*my+m.e;ry=m.b*mx+m.d*my+m.f}
       if(bb&&Number.isFinite(rx)&&Number.isFinite(ry)&&bb.width<160&&bb.height<160)resolvedStart={x:rx,y:ry};
     }catch(e){}
   }
 }
 if(!host.isConnected)document.body.appendChild(host);
 const PL=p.getTotalLength(),n=Math.round(clamp(km*1000/5.5,500,1250)),raw=[];
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){let q=p.getPointAtLength(PL*i/n);raw.push([q.x,q.y])}host.remove();
 let area=0;for(let i=0;i<raw.length;i++){let a=raw[i],b=raw[(i+1)%raw.length];area+=a[0]*b[1]-b[0]*a[1]}
 let cw=area>0,anti=direction==="ANTI_CLOCKWISE"||direction==="COUNTERCLOCKWISE";if((!anti&&!cw)||(anti&&cw))raw.reverse();
 if(resolvedStart&&Number.isFinite(resolvedStart.x)&&Number.isFinite(resolvedStart.y)){
   let bi=0,bt=0,bd=Infinity;
   for(let i=0;i<raw.length;i++){const a=raw[i],b=raw[(i+1)%raw.length],dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1],den=dx*dx+dy*dy||1,t=clamp(((resolvedStart.x-a[0])*dx+(resolvedStart.y-a[1])*dy)/den,0,1),x=a[0]+dx*t,y=a[1]+dy*t,dd=(x-resolvedStart.x)**2+(y-resolvedStart.y)**2;if(dd<bd){bd=dd;bi=i;bt=t}}
   const a=raw[bi],b=raw[(bi+1)%raw.length],proj=[a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*bt,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*bt],rot=[proj,...raw.slice(bi+1),...raw.slice(0,bi+1)];raw.splice(0,raw.length,...rot);
 }
 let plen=0;for(let i=0;i<raw.length;i++){let a=raw[i],b=raw[(i+1)%raw.length];plen+=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1])}
 const sc=km*1000/plen,x0=raw[0][0],y0=raw[0][1];return{pts:raw.map(q=>[(q[0]-x0)*sc,-(q[1]-y0)*sc]),startAligned:!!resolvedStart};
}


const CUSTOM_TRACK_SVG_KEY_PREFIX="apex-v52271-custom-track-svg-",CUSTOM_WORLD_SVG_KEY_PREFIX="apex-v52271-custom-world-svg-";
const customWorldProfileCache=new Map(),apexLargeSvgCache=new Map(),apexLargeSvgHydrated=new Set();
// BASE y override se mantienen separados. IndexedDB contiene sólo overrides del usuario.
const apexBaseTrackSvgCache=new Map(),apexBaseWorldProfileCache=new Map();

function readBaseWorldProfile(dir){return apexBaseWorldProfileCache.get(String(dir||''))||null}
