function readEffectiveWorldProfile(dir){return readStoredCustomWorldProfile(dir)||readBaseWorldProfile(dir)}
const APEX_LARGE_DB_NAME="apex-formula-circuit-files",APEX_LARGE_DB_STORE="circuits",APEX_COMPILED_DB_STORE="compiled";
let apexLargeDbPromise=null;
function customTrackSvgKey(dir){return CUSTOM_TRACK_SVG_KEY_PREFIX+String(dir||"")}
function customWorldSvgKey(dir){return CUSTOM_WORLD_SVG_KEY_PREFIX+String(dir||"")}
function safeJsonParse(txt,fallback=null){try{return JSON.parse(txt)}catch(e){return fallback}}
function clonePlain(v){return v==null?v:safeJsonParse(JSON.stringify(v),null)}
function normalizeCustomWorldProfile(obj){if(!obj)return null;return {profileRevision:+obj.profileRevision||0,authoritative:obj.authoritative===true,landmarks:Array.isArray(obj.landmarks)?obj.landmarks:[],brakeBoards:Array.isArray(obj.brakeBoards)?obj.brakeBoards:[],brakeBoardsAuthoritative:obj.brakeBoardsAuthoritative===true,cornerMarkers:Array.isArray(obj.cornerMarkers)?obj.cornerMarkers:[],pitLaneSamples:Array.isArray(obj.pitLaneSamples)?obj.pitLaneSamples:[],pitNetwork:obj.pitNetwork&&Array.isArray(obj.pitNetwork.runs)?obj.pitNetwork:null,wallLeftSamples:Array.isArray(obj.wallLeftSamples)?obj.wallLeftSamples:[],wallRightSamples:Array.isArray(obj.wallRightSamples)?obj.wallRightSamples:[],wallSegments:Array.isArray(obj.wallSegments)?obj.wallSegments:[],edgeLeftSamples:Array.isArray(obj.edgeLeftSamples)?obj.edgeLeftSamples:[],edgeRightSamples:Array.isArray(obj.edgeRightSamples)?obj.edgeRightSamples:[],structureZones:Array.isArray(obj.structureZones)?obj.structureZones:[],barrierSegments:Array.isArray(obj.barrierSegments)?obj.barrierSegments:[],surfaceZones:Array.isArray(obj.surfaceZones)?obj.surfaceZones:[],terrainContours:Array.isArray(obj.terrainContours)?obj.terrainContours:[],terrainBase:obj.terrainBase&&Array.isArray(obj.terrainBase.rings)?obj.terrainBase:null,terrainContoursAuthoritative:obj.terrainContoursAuthoritative===true,terrainReference:String(obj.terrainReference||'ABSOLUTE'),drivableArea:obj.drivableArea&&Array.isArray(obj.drivableArea.rings)?obj.drivableArea:null,curbZones:Array.isArray(obj.curbZones)?obj.curbZones:[]}}

// v5.230.46 · IndexedDB admite archivos SVG grandes y perfiles 3D detallados sin
// consumir la cuota de localStorage. La transacción almacena AMBAS partes de forma atómica.
function apexLargeDbOpen(){
 if(apexLargeDbPromise)return apexLargeDbPromise;
 apexLargeDbPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
   if(typeof indexedDB==='undefined')return reject(Error('IndexedDB no está disponible en este navegador'));
   let req;try{req=indexedDB.open(APEX_LARGE_DB_NAME,2)}catch(e){reject(e);return}
   req.onupgradeneeded=()=>{const db=req.result;if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains(APEX_LARGE_DB_STORE))db.createObjectStore(APEX_LARGE_DB_STORE,{keyPath:'dir'});if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains(APEX_COMPILED_DB_STORE))db.createObjectStore(APEX_COMPILED_DB_STORE,{keyPath:'dir'})};
   req.onsuccess=()=>{const db=req.result;db.onversionchange=()=>{db.close();apexLargeDbPromise=null};resolve(db)};
   req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||Error('Error al abrir IndexedDB'));
   req.onblocked=()=>reject(Error('IndexedDB bloqueado por otra pestaña de APEX'));
 }).catch(e=>{apexLargeDbPromise=null;throw e});
 return apexLargeDbPromise;
}
async function apexLargeDbRead(dir){
 const db=await apexLargeDbOpen();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
   let tx;try{tx=db.transaction(APEX_LARGE_DB_STORE,'readonly')}catch(e){reject(e);return}
   const req=tx.objectStore(APEX_LARGE_DB_STORE).get(String(dir||''));
   req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result||null);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||Error('No se pudo leer el SVG guardado'));
   tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||Error('Lectura IndexedDB interrumpida'));
 });
}
async function apexLargeDbWrite(dir,geometry,world){
 const db=await apexLargeDbOpen();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
   let tx;try{tx=db.transaction(APEX_LARGE_DB_STORE,'readwrite');tx.objectStore(APEX_LARGE_DB_STORE).put({dir:String(dir||''),geometry,world,schema:1})}catch(e){try{tx&&tx.abort()}catch(_){}reject(e);return}
   tx.oncomplete=()=>resolve(true);tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||Error('Error guardando SVG en IndexedDB'));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||Error('No hay espacio o permiso para guardar el SVG'));
 });
}
async function apexLargeDbDelete(dir){
 const db=await apexLargeDbOpen();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
   let tx;try{const stores=db.objectStoreNames.contains(APEX_COMPILED_DB_STORE)?[APEX_LARGE_DB_STORE,APEX_COMPILED_DB_STORE]:[APEX_LARGE_DB_STORE];tx=db.transaction(stores,'readwrite');tx.objectStore(APEX_LARGE_DB_STORE).delete(String(dir||''));if(stores.length>1)tx.objectStore(APEX_COMPILED_DB_STORE).delete(String(dir||''))}catch(e){reject(e);return}
   tx.oncomplete=()=>resolve();tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||Error('No se pudo borrar SVG'));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||Error('Borrado interrumpido'));
 });
}
// v5.230.62.2 · caché persistente de la compilación estática. Se guarda en un
// almacén separado: nunca sustituye el SVG maestro ni el perfil 3D editable.
async function apexCompiledDbRead(dir){
 try{const db=await apexLargeDbOpen();return await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{let tx;try{tx=db.transaction(APEX_COMPILED_DB_STORE,'readonly')}catch(e){reject(e);return}const req=tx.objectStore(APEX_COMPILED_DB_STORE).get(String(dir||''));req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result||null);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||Error('No se pudo leer la caché compilada'));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||Error('Lectura de caché compilada interrumpida'))})}catch(e){console.warn('APEX: caché compilada no disponible',e);return null}
}
async function apexCompiledDbWrite(dir,record){
 try{const db=await apexLargeDbOpen();return await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{let tx;try{tx=db.transaction(APEX_COMPILED_DB_STORE,'readwrite');tx.objectStore(APEX_COMPILED_DB_STORE).put({...record,dir:String(dir||'')})}catch(e){try{tx&&tx.abort()}catch(_){}reject(e);return}tx.oncomplete=()=>resolve(true);tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||Error('No se pudo guardar la caché compilada'));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||Error('Guardado de caché compilada interrumpido'))})}catch(e){console.warn('APEX: no se pudo persistir la caché compilada',e);return false}
}
async function apexCompiledDbDelete(dir){
 try{const db=await apexLargeDbOpen();return await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{let tx;try{tx=db.transaction(APEX_COMPILED_DB_STORE,'readwrite');tx.objectStore(APEX_COMPILED_DB_STORE).delete(String(dir||''))}catch(e){reject(e);return}tx.oncomplete=()=>resolve(true);tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||Error('No se pudo borrar la caché compilada'));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||Error('Borrado de caché compilada interrumpido'))})}catch(e){return false}
}
// Hydration occurs before loadTrack(), not during render. Older versions stored the same
// payloads in localStorage and remain readable until the user reimports that circuit.
async function apexHydrateLargeCircuit(dir){
 const key=String(dir||'');if(apexLargeSvgHydrated.has(key))return;
 let saved=null;try{saved=await apexLargeDbRead(key)}catch(e){
   // A browser may disallow IndexedDB on some file:// viewers. Preserve legacy
   // localStorage imports, but never silently claim that a NEW large import was saved.
   console.warn('APEX: IndexedDB no disponible al recuperar circuito',e);
 }
 if(saved&&saved.geometry&&Array.isArray(saved.geometry.points)&&saved.geometry.points.length>20){
   apexLargeSvgCache.set(key,saved.geometry);
   customWorldProfileCache.set(key,normalizeCustomWorldProfile(saved.world));
 }
 apexLargeSvgHydrated.add(key);
}
async function apexSaveLargeCircuit(dir,geometry,world){
 const key=String(dir||'');let saved=null;
 try{
   // Reproducimos la serialización de la versión antigua; evita DataCloneError
   // si en el futuro algún objeto del importador lleva referencias al DOM.
   const cleanGeometry=clonePlain(geometry),cleanWorld=clonePlain(world);
   if(!cleanGeometry||!cleanWorld)throw Error('No se pudo preparar el SVG y el mundo 3D');
   await apexLargeDbWrite(key,cleanGeometry,cleanWorld);
   saved=await apexLargeDbRead(key);
   if(!saved||!saved.geometry||saved.geometry.rawSvg!==geometry.rawSvg||!saved.world||saved.world.profileRevision!==world.profileRevision)throw Error('La verificación de IndexedDB no devolvió el SVG íntegro');
   // Un SVG/perfil recién importado invalida cualquier malla compilada anterior del circuito.
   await apexCompiledDbDelete(key);
 }catch(e){
   // Último recurso: la sesión permite probar un SVG desde un visor de archivos
   // que bloquee IndexedDB, sin engañar al usuario sobre la persistencia.
   apexLargeSvgCache.set(key,geometry);
   customWorldProfileCache.set(key,normalizeCustomWorldProfile(world));
   apexLargeSvgHydrated.add(key);
   console.warn('APEX: importación temporal; IndexedDB no ha guardado el circuito',e);
   return 'MEMORY_ONLY';
 }
 apexLargeSvgCache.set(key,geometry);
 customWorldProfileCache.set(key,normalizeCustomWorldProfile(world));
 apexLargeSvgHydrated.add(key);
 // Eliminar SOLAMENTE las copias antiguas de ESTE circuito libera localStorage.
 // El JSON, el progreso y los SVG de los demás circuitos permanecen intactos.
 try{appStorage.removeItem(customTrackSvgKey(key))}catch(e){}
 try{appStorage.removeItem(customWorldSvgKey(key))}catch(e){}
 return 'IndexedDB';
}
function readStoredCustomTrackSvg(dir){
 const key=String(dir||'');if(apexLargeSvgCache.has(key))return apexLargeSvgCache.get(key);
 const raw=appStorage.getItem(customTrackSvgKey(key)),obj=raw?safeJsonParse(raw,null):null;
 if(!obj)return null;
 if(Array.isArray(obj.points)&&obj.points.length>20)return obj;
 return typeof obj.trackPathD==='string'&&obj.trackPathD.trim()?obj:null;
}
function readStoredCustomWorldProfile(dir){
 const key=String(dir||'');if(customWorldProfileCache.has(key))return customWorldProfileCache.get(key);
 const raw=appStorage.getItem(customWorldSvgKey(key)),obj=raw?safeJsonParse(raw,null):null,profile=normalizeCustomWorldProfile(obj);customWorldProfileCache.set(key,profile);return profile;
}
async function clearStoredCustomSvgData(dir){
 const key=String(dir||'');
 // Deletion must complete in the persistent store before declaring the reset done.
 await apexLargeDbDelete(key);
 apexLargeSvgCache.delete(key);apexLargeSvgHydrated.delete(key);customWorldProfileCache.delete(key);
 try{appStorage.removeItem(customTrackSvgKey(key))}catch(e){}
 try{appStorage.removeItem(customWorldSvgKey(key))}catch(e){}
}
function sampleSvgPathD(pathD,approxStep=6){
 const ns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg",host=document.createElementNS(ns,"svg"),p=document.createElementNS(ns,"path");
 host.setAttribute("width","1");host.setAttribute("height","1");host.style.cssText="position:fixed;left:-9999px;top:-9999px;width:1px;height:1px;opacity:0;pointer-events:none";
 p.setAttribute("d",pathD||"");host.appendChild(p);document.body.appendChild(host);
 let pts=[];try{const L=Math.max(.01,p.getTotalLength()),n=Math.max(2,Math.min(1500,Math.round(L/Math.max(1.5,approxStep))));for(let i=0;i<=n;i++){const q=p.getPointAtLength(L*i/n);pts.push([q.x,-q.y])}}catch(e){}host.remove();
 return pts;
}
function parseSvgPointPairsRaw(pointsText){
 const nums=(String(pointsText||'').match(/[-+]?(?:\d*\.\d+|\d+\.?)(?:[eE][-+]?\d+)?/g)||[]).map(Number),out=[];
 for(let i=0;i+1<nums.length;i+=2)if(Number.isFinite(nums[i])&&Number.isFinite(nums[i+1]))out.push([nums[i],nums[i+1]]);
 return out;
}
function parseSvgPointsAttr(pointsText){return parseSvgPointPairsRaw(pointsText).map(p=>[p[0],-p[1]])}
function sampleSvgNodeWorldPoints(el){
 if(!el)return[];const tag=(el.tagName||'').toLowerCase();
 if(tag==='path')return sampleSvgPathD(el.getAttribute('d')||'');
 if(tag==='polygon'||tag==='polyline')return parseSvgPointsAttr(el.getAttribute('points'));
 if(tag==='line')return [[parseFloat(el.getAttribute('x1'))||0,-(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('y1'))||0)],[parseFloat(el.getAttribute('x2'))||0,-(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('y2'))||0)]];
 if(tag==='rect'){const x=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('x'))||0,y=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('y'))||0,w=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('width'))||0,h=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('height'))||0;return [[x,-y],[x+w,-y],[x+w,-(y+h)],[x,-(y+h)]]}
 if(tag==='circle'){const cx=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('cx'))||0,cy=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('cy'))||0,r=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('r'))||0,out=[];for(let i=0;i<16;i++){const a=Math.PI*2*i/16;out.push([cx+Math.cos(a)*r,-(cy+Math.sin(a)*r)])}return out}
 return [];
}
function importedGuideFromWorldPoints(points,width=0){
 if(!trackData||!Array.isArray(points)||points.length<2)return[];const out=[];let hint=null;
 for(const pt of points){const ns=nearestTrackState(pt[0],pt[1],hint,{radius:120,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:90});hint=ns.index;out.push({s:ns.s,lat:ns.lat,width:width||0})}
 out.sort((a,b)=>a.s-b.s);const ded=[];
 for(const it of out){if(!ded.length||Math.abs(it.s-ded[ded.length-1].s)>2.5)ded.push(it);else ded[ded.length-1]={s:(ded[ded.length-1].s+it.s)*.5,lat:(ded[ded.length-1].lat+it.lat)*.5,width:Math.max(ded[ded.length-1].width||0,it.width||0)}}
 return ded;
}
function guideNearestSample(samples,s,L,limit=52){
 // v5.226.3: búsqueda binaria sin asignaciones. Los samples están ordenados por s.
 if(!Array.isArray(samples)||!samples.length)return null;
 const target=mod(s,L),n=samples.length;let lo=0,hi=n;
 while(lo<hi){const mid=(lo+hi)>>1;if(samples[mid].s<target)lo=mid+1;else hi=mid}
 let best=null,bestAbs=Infinity;
 const test=i=>{if(i<0||i>=n)return;const sm=samples[i],d=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(target,sm.s,L));if(d<bestAbs){bestAbs=d;best=sm}};
 test(lo);test(lo-1);test(0);test(n-1);
 return best&&bestAbs<=limit?best:null;
}
function guideContinuousSample(samples,s,L,maxGap=18,maxWorldGap=24){
 // Interpola únicamente DENTRO de un tramo SVG continuo. Nunca salta de un subpath
 // al siguiente ni prolonga sus extremos decenas de metros: así no aparecen muros
 // invisibles en accesos, pit entry/exit o huecos dibujados expresamente.
 if(!Array.isArray(samples)||!samples.length)return null;
 const target=mod(s,L),n=samples.length;let lo=0,hi=n;
 while(lo<hi){const mid=(lo+hi)>>1;if(samples[mid].s<target)lo=mid+1;else hi=mid}
 const ni=lo<n?lo:0,pi=(ni-1+n)%n,next=samples[ni],prev=samples[pi];
 const gap=mod(next.s-prev.s,L),fromPrev=mod(target-prev.s,L),toNext=mod(next.s-target,L);
 const sameSeg=(prev.segment==null||next.segment==null||prev.segment===next.segment);
 const worldGap=(Number.isFinite(prev.x)&&Number.isFinite(next.x))?Math.hypot(next.x-prev.x,next.z-prev.z):0;
 if(sameSeg&&gap>1e-5&&gap<=maxGap&&fromPrev<=gap+1e-6&&toNext<=gap+1e-6&&(!worldGap||worldGap<=maxWorldGap)){
   const t=clamp(fromPrev/gap,0,1),mix=(a,b)=>Number.isFinite(a)&&Number.isFinite(b)?lerp(a,b,t):(Number.isFinite(a)?a:b);
   return {s:target,lat:mix(prev.lat,next.lat),width:Math.max(prev.width||0,next.width||0),x:mix(prev.x,next.x),z:mix(prev.z,next.z),segment:prev.segment};
 }
 let best=null,bestD=Infinity;for(const q of [prev,next]){const d=Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(target,q.s,L));if(d<bestD){bestD=d;best=q}}
 // Sólo una tolerancia mínima en el extremo geométrico, suficiente para el muestreo
 // pero demasiado corta para cerrar un acceso de boxes.
 return best&&bestD<=1.85?best:null;
}
// v5.230.62.8.2 · ESQUEMA UNIVERSAL DE OBJETOS SVG
// El SVG es la fuente maestra de geometría + identidad visual del objeto.
// Convención común: data-apex-type / data-apex-family / data-apex-role /
// data-apex-style + parámetros físicos/visuales. Los atributos legacy siguen
// siendo alias válidos para no romper circuitos anteriores.
const APEX_SVG_OBJECT_SCHEMA='APEX_SVG_OBJECT_V1';
function apexSvgBoolAttr(el,name,def=null){
 if(!el||!el.hasAttribute(name))return def;const v=String(el.getAttribute(name)||'').trim().toLowerCase();
 if(['false','0','no','off'].includes(v))return false;if(['true','1','yes','on',''].includes(v))return true;return def;
}
function apexSvgNumAttr(el,name,def=null){if(!el||!el.hasAttribute(name))return def;const v=parseFloat(el.getAttribute(name));return Number.isFinite(v)?v:def}
function apexSvgStringAttr(el,name,def=''){if(!el||!el.hasAttribute(name))return def;const v=String(el.getAttribute(name)||'').trim();return v||def}
function apexSvgInferType(role,type=''){
 const t=String(type||'').trim();if(t)return t;const r=String(role||'').trim().toLowerCase();
 if(r==='wall-left'||r==='wall-right')return 'wall';if(r==='pitlane-centerline')return 'pitLane';
 return roleNameToImportType(r)||'';
}
function apexSvgFamilyFor(type,role=''){
 const t=String(type||'').toLowerCase(),r=String(role||'').toLowerCase();
 if(t==='wall'||r==='wall-left'||r==='wall-right')return 'wall';if(t==='barrier'||r.startsWith('barrier-'))return 'barrier';
 if(['tunnel','tunnelportal','bridge','road','pit','grandstand','block','building','tower','gantry','billboard','flagpole','parking'].includes(t))return 'structure';
 if(['surfacezone','water','marking','drivablearea'].includes(t))return 'surface';if(['terrainzone','terraincontour'].includes(t)||r.startsWith('terrain-'))return 'terrain';
 if(['timingmarker','cornermarker','distanceboard','marker'].includes(t)||r==='start-line'||r==='finish-line'||r.startsWith('drs-')||r==='sector-line'||r==='speed-trap'||r==='straight-mode-activation'||r==='override-detection'||r==='override-activation')return 'marker';
 if(t==='pitlane'||r==='pitlane-centerline')return 'track';if(r==='track-centerline')return 'track';return 'object';
}
function apexSvgLegacyStyleAttr(type){
 const t=String(type||'').toLowerCase();return ({wall:'data-apex-wall-style',tunnel:'data-apex-tunnel-style',tunnelportal:'data-apex-portal-style',block:'data-apex-building-style',building:'data-apex-building-style',grandstand:'data-apex-grandstand-style',pit:'data-apex-pit-style',road:'data-apex-road-style',bridge:'data-apex-bridge-style',barrier:'data-apex-barrier-style'})[t]||'';
}
function apexSvgDefaultStyle(type,role='',el=null){
 const t=String(type||'').toLowerCase(),r=String(role||'').toLowerCase();
 if(t==='wall'||r==='wall-left'||r==='wall-right')return 'wall_f1';
 if(t==='barrier'||r.startsWith('barrier-')){if(r.includes('tecpro'))return 'barrier_tecpro';if(r.includes('safer'))return 'barrier_safer';if(r.includes('tire'))return 'barrier_tyre';if(r.includes('guardrail'))return 'barrier_guardrail';return 'barrier_concrete'}
 if(t==='tunnel')return 'tunnel_concrete';if(t==='tunnelportal')return 'portal_concrete';
 if(t==='road')return r.startsWith('city-road')?'road_city':(r==='service-road'?'road_service':'road_standard');
 if(t==='bridge')return r==='pedestrian-bridge'?'bridge_pedestrian':'bridge_concrete';
 if(t==='grandstand')return 'grandstand_open';if(t==='pit')return 'pit_modern_f1';if(t==='block'||t==='building')return 'building_generic';
 if(t==='tower')return 'tower_generic';if(t==='gantry')return r==='start-gantry'?'gantry_start':(r==='finish-gantry'?'gantry_finish':'gantry_standard');
 if(t==='parking')return 'parking_asphalt';if(t==='surfacezone')return 'surface_'+String((el&&el.getAttribute('data-apex-surface'))||r.replace(/^runoff-/, '')||'asphalt').toLowerCase();
 if(t==='water')return r==='sea'?'water_sea':'water_lake';if(t==='tree')return 'tree_'+String((el&&el.getAttribute('data-apex-variant'))||'deciduous').toLowerCase();
 if(t==='forestzone')return 'forest_mixed';if(t==='terrainzone'||t==='terraincontour')return 'terrain_natural';if(t==='marking')return 'marking_'+(r||'standard').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'_');
 if(t==='distanceboard')return 'board_distance';if(t==='cornermarker')return 'marker_corner';if(t==='timingmarker')return 'marker_'+(r||'timing').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'_');
 if(t==='pitlane')return 'pitlane_asphalt';if(t==='drivablearea')return 'drivable_area';return (r||t||'generic').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'_');
}
function apexSvgReadStyle(el,type='',role=''){
 const t=apexSvgInferType(role,type),direct=apexSvgStringAttr(el,'data-apex-style','');if(direct)return direct.toLowerCase();
 const legacy=apexSvgLegacyStyleAttr(t),lv=legacy?apexSvgStringAttr(el,legacy,''):'';if(lv)return lv.toLowerCase();
 if(String(t).toLowerCase()==='barrier'){
   const bv=apexSvgStringAttr(el,'data-apex-barrier','');if(bv)return 'barrier_'+bv.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'_');
 }
 return apexSvgDefaultStyle(t,role,el);
}
function apexSvgReadAppearance(el,type='',role=''){
 const t=apexSvgInferType(role,type),num=a=>{const v=parseFloat(el&&el.getAttribute?el.getAttribute(a):'');return Number.isFinite(v)?v:null};return {schema:APEX_SVG_OBJECT_SCHEMA,type:t,family:apexSvgStringAttr(el,'data-apex-family',apexSvgFamilyFor(t,role)),style:apexSvgReadStyle(el,t,role),material:apexSvgStringAttr(el,'data-apex-material',''),roofStyle:apexSvgStringAttr(el,'data-apex-roof-style',''),portalStyle:apexSvgStringAttr(el,'data-apex-portal-style',''),interiorStyle:apexSvgStringAttr(el,'data-apex-interior-style',''),lights:apexSvgBoolAttr(el,'data-apex-lights',null),panels:apexSvgBoolAttr(el,'data-apex-panels',null),serviceWalk:apexSvgBoolAttr(el,'data-apex-service-walk',null),lightSpacingM:num('data-apex-light-spacing'),panelSpacingM:num('data-apex-panel-spacing'),serviceWalkWidthM:num('data-apex-service-walk-width'),garageBays:num('data-apex-garage-bays'),glass:apexSvgBoolAttr(el,'data-apex-glass',null),balcony:apexSvgBoolAttr(el,'data-apex-balcony',null),pitwall:apexSvgBoolAttr(el,'data-apex-pitwall',null),garageStyle:apexSvgStringAttr(el,'data-apex-garage-style',''),covered:apexSvgBoolAttr(el,'data-apex-covered',null),seatColor:apexSvgStringAttr(el,'data-apex-seat-color',''),seatRows:num('data-apex-seat-rows')};
}
function apexSvgApplyUniversalSchema(root){
 if(!root)return;root.setAttribute('data-apex-object-schema',APEX_SVG_OBJECT_SCHEMA);root.setAttribute('data-apex-object-schema-version','1');
 const els=[...root.querySelectorAll('[data-apex-type],[data-apex-role]')];
 for(const el of els){
   const role=apexSvgStringAttr(el,'data-apex-role','').toLowerCase(),type=apexSvgInferType(role,apexSvgStringAttr(el,'data-apex-type',''));if(!type)continue;
   if(!el.hasAttribute('data-apex-type'))el.setAttribute('data-apex-type',type);
   if(!el.hasAttribute('data-apex-family'))el.setAttribute('data-apex-family',apexSvgFamilyFor(type,role));
   const style=apexSvgReadStyle(el,type,role);if(style&&!el.hasAttribute('data-apex-style'))el.setAttribute('data-apex-style',style);
   const legacy=apexSvgLegacyStyleAttr(type);if(legacy&&style&&!el.hasAttribute(legacy))el.setAttribute(legacy,style);
   // Barreras: mantenemos kind legacy legible por el motor actual.
   if(String(type).toLowerCase()==='barrier'&&!el.hasAttribute('data-apex-barrier')){
     const s=String(style||'').replace(/^barrier_/,'').toUpperCase();if(s)el.setAttribute('data-apex-barrier',s==='TYRE'?'TYRE':s);
   }
 }
}
function roleNameToImportType(role){
 const r=String(role||'').trim().toLowerCase();
 const map={
  'building':'building','pit-building':'pit','forest-zone':'forestZone','road':'road','service-road':'road','city-road':'road','city-road-line':'road','city-road-polyline':'road','parking':'parking',
  'bridge':'bridge','pedestrian-bridge':'bridge','tunnel':'tunnel','tunnel-portal':'tunnelPortal','tree':'tree','ferris':'ferris','tower':'tower',
  'grandstand':'grandstand','gantry':'gantry','start-gantry':'gantry','finish-gantry':'gantry','billboard':'billboard','flag-pole':'flagPole',
  'crosswalk':'marking','urban-stripes':'marking','curb-left':'marking','curb-right':'marking','start-line':'timingMarker','finish-line':'timingMarker','sector-line':'timingMarker','speed-trap':'timingMarker','straight-mode-activation':'timingMarker','override-detection':'timingMarker','override-activation':'timingMarker','drivable-area':'drivableArea','terrain-zone':'terrainZone','terrain-base':'terrainZone','terrain-contour':'terrainContour','terrain-plate':'terrainContour','distance-board':'distanceBoard',
  'surface-asphalt':'surfaceZone','surface-gravel':'surfaceZone','surface-sand':'surfaceZone','surface-grass':'surfaceZone','runoff-asphalt':'surfaceZone','runoff-gravel':'surfaceZone','runoff-sand':'surfaceZone','runoff-grass':'surfaceZone',
  'lake':'water','sea':'water',
  'barrier-tecpro':'barrier','barrier-safer':'barrier','barrier-tirewall':'barrier','barrier-concrete':'barrier','barrier-guardrail':'barrier'
 };
 return map[r]||'';
}
function polyFootprintFromWorldPath(points,width){
 if(!Array.isArray(points)||points.length<2)return null;const a=points[0],b=points[points.length-1],dx=b[0]-a[0],dz=b[1]-a[1],len=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1,nx=-dz/len,nz=dx/len,hw=Math.max(1.2,(width||10)*.5);
 return [[a[0]+nx*hw,a[1]+nz*hw],[b[0]+nx*hw,b[1]+nz*hw],[b[0]-nx*hw,b[1]-nz*hw],[a[0]-nx*hw,a[1]-nz*hw]];
}

function apexSvgNormRole(v){return String(v||'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'')}
function apexSvgNodeMatches(el,names){
 const r=apexSvgNormRole(el&&el.getAttribute?el.getAttribute('data-apex-role'):''),id=apexSvgNormRole(el&&el.id||'');
 for(const n of names){const k=apexSvgNormRole(n);if(r===k||id===k||id.startsWith(k))return true}return false;
}
function apexSvgFindAll(root,names){return [...root.querySelectorAll('path,line,polyline,polygon,rect,circle,ellipse')].filter(el=>apexSvgNodeMatches(el,names))}
function apexSvgMountDocument(doc){
 const root=document.importNode(doc.documentElement,true),vb=root.viewBox&&root.viewBox.baseVal;
 if(vb&&vb.width>0&&vb.height>0){root.setAttribute('width',String(vb.width));root.setAttribute('height',String(vb.height))}
 else{root.setAttribute('width','2048');root.setAttribute('height','2048')}
 root.style.cssText='position:fixed;left:-100000px;top:-100000px;visibility:hidden;pointer-events:none;overflow:visible';document.body.appendChild(root);return root;
}
function apexSvgRelativeMatrix(root,el){
 try{const a=el.getCTM(),b=root.getCTM();if(a&&b&&typeof b.inverse==='function')return b.inverse().multiply(a)}catch(e){}
 try{return el.getCTM()}catch(e){return null}
}
function apexSvgMapLocalPoint(root,el,x,y){
 const m=apexSvgRelativeMatrix(root,el);if(!m)return [x,y];
 try{const p=new DOMPoint(x,y).matrixTransform(m);return[p.x,p.y]}catch(e){return[m.a*x+m.c*y+m.e,m.b*x+m.d*y+m.f]}
}
function apexSvgSampleMounted(root,el,opt={}){
 if(!el)return[];const tag=(el.tagName||'').toLowerCase(),out=[],map=(x,y)=>apexSvgMapLocalPoint(root,el,x,y);
 if(tag==='path'){
   try{const L=Math.max(.001,el.getTotalLength()),count=opt.count||Math.max(2,Math.min(1800,Math.ceil(L/Math.max(1,opt.step||5))));for(let i=0;i<count;i++){const q=el.getPointAtLength(L*i/count);out.push(map(q.x,q.y))}if(opt.includeEnd){const q=el.getPointAtLength(L);out.push(map(q.x,q.y))}}catch(e){}return out;
 }
 if(tag==='line'){out.push(map(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('x1'))||0,parseFloat(el.getAttribute('y1'))||0),map(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('x2'))||0,parseFloat(el.getAttribute('y2'))||0));return out}
 if(tag==='rect'){const x=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('x'))||0,y=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('y'))||0,w=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('width'))||0,h=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('height'))||0;return[map(x,y),map(x+w,y),map(x+w,y+h),map(x,y+h)]}
 if(tag==='circle'||tag==='ellipse'){const cx=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('cx'))||0,cy=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('cy'))||0,rx=tag==='circle'?(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('r'))||0):(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('rx'))||0),ry=tag==='circle'?rx:(parseFloat(el.getAttribute('ry'))||0);for(let i=0;i<20;i++){const a=Math.PI*2*i/20;out.push(map(cx+Math.cos(a)*rx,cy+Math.sin(a)*ry))}return out}
 if(tag==='polygon'||tag==='polyline'){const raw=parseSvgPointPairsRaw(el.getAttribute('points'));return raw.map(p=>map(p[0],p[1]))}
 return out;
}
function apexSvgMidPoint(root,el){const p=apexSvgSampleMounted(root,el,{count:2,includeEnd:true});if(!p.length)return null;let x=0,y=0;for(const q of p){x+=q[0];y+=q[1]}return{x:x/p.length,y:y/p.length}}
function apexSvgTrackContext(doc,spec){
 const root=apexSvgMountDocument(doc),meta=raceMetaFor(spec)||{},km=parseFloat(doc.documentElement.getAttribute('data-apex-km'))||meta.km||5.0,direction=doc.documentElement.getAttribute('data-apex-direction')||meta.direction||'CLOCKWISE';
 const trackEl=apexSvgFindAll(root,['track_centerline','track-centerline'])[0]||root.querySelector('#track_centerline')||root.querySelector('path');if(!trackEl){root.remove();throw Error('El SVG no contiene track_centerline')}
 const count=Math.round(clamp(km*1000/5.2,600,1400));let raw=apexSvgSampleMounted(root,trackEl,{count});if(raw.length<20){root.remove();throw Error('No se pudo leer la geometría de track_centerline')}
 const first=raw[0],last=raw[raw.length-1];if(Math.hypot(first[0]-last[0],first[1]-last[1])<.001)raw.pop();
 const startEl=apexSvgFindAll(root,['start_finish','start-finish','start_marker','start-marker'])[0]||root.querySelector('#start_finish')||root.querySelector('#start_marker'),start=startEl?apexSvgMidPoint(root,startEl):null;
 if(start){let bi=0,bd=Infinity;for(let i=0;i<raw.length;i++){const dx=raw[i][0]-start.x,dy=raw[i][1]-start.y,d=dx*dx+dy*dy;if(d<bd){bd=d;bi=i}}if(bi)raw=raw.slice(bi).concat(raw.slice(0,bi))}
 let plen=0;for(let i=0;i<raw.length;i++){const a=raw[i],b=raw[(i+1)%raw.length];plen+=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1])}const scale=(km*1000)/(plen||1),origin=raw[0];
 const toWorld=p=>[(p[0]-origin[0])*scale,-(p[1]-origin[1])*scale],points=raw.map(toWorld);
 return{root,km,direction,scale,origin,toWorld,points,trackEl,start};
}
function apexSvgPathSubpathChunksExact(el){
 // Separa el `d` por órdenes M/m REALES. No deducimos huecos por distancia.
 // Para un `m` relativo calculamos el punto final del prefijo y lo convertimos a M
 // absoluto, conservando también los pares implícitos posteriores como `l` relativos.
 const d=String(el&&el.getAttribute?el.getAttribute('d')||'':'').trim();
 if(!d)return[];
 const starts=[];for(let i=0;i<d.length;i++){const c=d[i];if(c==='M'||c==='m')starts.push(i)}
 if(starts.length<=1)return[d];
 const num='[-+]?(?:\\d*\\.\\d+|\\d+\\.?)(?:[eE][-+]?\\d+)?',moveRe=new RegExp('^m\\s*('+num+')\\s*,?\\s*('+num+')');
 const chunks=[];
 for(let k=0;k<starts.length;k++){
   const a=starts[k],b=k+1<starts.length?starts[k+1]:d.length;let raw=d.slice(a,b).trim();if(!raw)continue;
   if(raw[0]==='m'){
     let baseX=0,baseY=0;
     if(a>0&&el.parentNode){
       const probe=el.cloneNode(false);probe.removeAttribute('id');probe.setAttribute('d',d.slice(0,a).trim());el.parentNode.insertBefore(probe,el.nextSibling);
       try{const L=Math.max(0,probe.getTotalLength());if(L>=0){const q=probe.getPointAtLength(L);baseX=q.x;baseY=q.y}}catch(e){}probe.remove();
     }
     const m=raw.match(moveRe);
     if(m){
       const ax=baseX+(+m[1]||0),ay=baseY+(+m[2]||0);let rest=raw.slice(m[0].length);
       // Tras un moveto relativo, pares extra sin letra de comando son lineto relativos.
       if(/^\\s*[-+.0-9]/.test(rest))rest=' l '+rest;
       raw=`M ${ax} ${ay}${rest}`;
     }
   }
   chunks.push(raw);
 }
 return chunks.length?chunks:[d];
}
function apexSvgElementWorldRunsExact(ctx,el,stepM=3){
 if(!el)return[];const tag=(el.tagName||'').toLowerCase(),runs=[];
 if(tag==='path'){
   const chunks=apexSvgPathSubpathChunksExact(el);
   for(const chunk of chunks){
     let part=el,temp=false;
     if(chunks.length>1){part=el.cloneNode(false);part.removeAttribute('id');part.setAttribute('d',chunk);el.parentNode.insertBefore(part,el.nextSibling);temp=true}
     const pts=[];
     try{
       const localL=Math.max(.001,part.getTotalLength()),localStep=Math.max(.12,stepM/Math.max(.0001,ctx.scale)),count=Math.max(2,Math.min(6500,Math.ceil(localL/localStep)));
       for(let i=0;i<=count;i++){const q=part.getPointAtLength(localL*i/count),m=apexSvgMapLocalPoint(ctx.root,part,q.x,q.y);pts.push(ctx.toWorld(m))}
     }catch(e){}
     if(temp)part.remove();
     if(pts.length>=2)runs.push(pts);
   }
   return runs;
 }
 const pts=apexSvgSampleMounted(ctx.root,el,{step:Math.max(.5,stepM/Math.max(.0001,ctx.scale)),includeEnd:true}).map(ctx.toWorld);
 if(pts.length>=2)runs.push(pts);return runs;
}
function apexSvgGuideSamplesExact(ctx,elements,width=0){
 // Los samples auxiliares conservan el subpath explícito. No ordenamos/pegamos un
 // hueco del SVG; sólo ordenamos por s al final para las consultas antiguas por vuelta.
 const out=[];let seg=0;
 for(const el of elements){
   const runs=apexSvgElementWorldRunsExact(ctx,el,3.5);
   for(const pts of runs){let hint=null;for(const pt of pts){const ns=nearestTrackState(pt[0],pt[1],hint,{radius:140,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:110});hint=ns.index;out.push({s:ns.s,lat:ns.lat,width:width||0,x:pt[0],z:pt[1],segment:seg})}seg++}
 }
 out.sort((a,b)=>a.s-b.s);return out;
}
// v5.230.10 — X/Z literal y subpaths M/m explícitos. Un hueco en el SVG siempre
// es un hueco físico, aunque mida menos de 5 m. No hay heurística de "salto".
function apexSvgWorldRunsExact(ctx,elements,stepM=3){
 const runs=[];let runId=0;
 for(const el of elements){
   // data-apex-width es una magnitud física en metros, no una coordenada SVG.
   const width=parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-width'))||0,role=String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-role')||'');
   for(const pts of apexSvgElementWorldRunsExact(ctx,el,stepM))runs.push({
     id:runId++,width,sourceRole:role,points:pts,
     height:parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-height'))||null,
     wallStyle:apexSvgReadStyle(el,'wall',role)||'wall_f1',
     wallColor:el.getAttribute('data-apex-color')||null,
     fence:el.hasAttribute('data-apex-fence')?String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-fence')).toLowerCase()!=='false':null,
     fenceH:parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-fence-height'))||null,
     fenceLeanM:parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-fence-lean-m'))||null,
     postSpacingM:parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-apex-post-spacing-m'))||null,
     advertising:el.hasAttribute('data-apex-advertising')?String(el.getAttribute('data-apex-advertising')).toLowerCase()!=='false':null
   });
 }
 return runs;
}
function apexPitRunEndDistance(a,b){
 if(!a||!b)return Infinity;const dx=a[0]-b[0],dz=a[1]-b[1];return Math.hypot(dx,dz);
}
function apexOrderPitRuns(runs,defaultWidth=5.9){
 // v5.230.13: un pitlane puede estar partido en varios subpaths M...M... simplemente
 // porque cruza el origen de vuelta. No podemos concatenarlos en el orden textual del SVG.
 // Encadenamos únicamente extremos físicamente próximos, sin dibujar ningún segmento nuevo.
 const parts=(runs||[]).filter(r=>r&&Array.isArray(r.points)&&r.points.length>=2).map((r,i)=>({id:Number.isFinite(r.id)?r.id:i,width:r.width||defaultWidth||5.9,sourceRole:r.sourceRole||'pitlane-centerline',points:r.points.map(p=>[+p[0],+p[1],Number.isFinite(+p[2])?+p[2]:undefined])}));
 if(parts.length<2)return parts;
 const avgW=parts.reduce((q,r)=>q+Math.max(3.8,+r.width||defaultWidth||5.9),0)/parts.length,joinTol=Math.max(9,Math.min(22,avgW*3.2));
 const remaining=parts.slice();
 // Empezamos por el tramo más largo: normalmente es el cuerpo principal del pit.
 const lengthOf=r=>{let L=0;for(let i=1;i<r.points.length;i++)L+=apexPitRunEndDistance(r.points[i-1],r.points[i]);return L};
 let seed=0,bestLen=-1;for(let i=0;i<remaining.length;i++){const L=lengthOf(remaining[i]);if(L>bestLen){bestLen=L;seed=i}}
 const chain=[remaining.splice(seed,1)[0]];
 while(remaining.length){
   const front=chain[0].points[0],back=chain[chain.length-1].points[chain[chain.length-1].points.length-1];
   let best=null;
   for(let i=0;i<remaining.length;i++){
     const r=remaining[i],p0=r.points[0],p1=r.points[r.points.length-1];
     const candidates=[
       {d:apexPitRunEndDistance(p1,front),where:'front',reverse:false},
       {d:apexPitRunEndDistance(p0,front),where:'front',reverse:true},
       {d:apexPitRunEndDistance(p0,back),where:'back',reverse:false},
       {d:apexPitRunEndDistance(p1,back),where:'back',reverse:true}
     ];
     for(const c of candidates)if(!best||c.d<best.d)best={...c,index:i};
   }
   if(!best||best.d>joinTol)break;
   const r=remaining.splice(best.index,1)[0];if(best.reverse)r.points.reverse();
   r.joinGap=best.d;
   if(best.where==='front')chain.unshift(r);else chain.push(r);
 }
 // Si hubiera geometrías realmente desconectadas no las hacemos pasar por una falsa
 // continuidad. Se conservan al final para render/edición, pero quedan marcadas como
 // componentes distintas; la ruta BOX usará el componente principal.
 for(const r of remaining){r.detached=true;chain.push(r)}
 return chain;
}
function apexBuildPitNetwork(runs,defaultWidth=5.9){
 const ordered=apexOrderPitRuns(runs,defaultWidth),out={schema:'APEX_PIT_NETWORK_V2',runs:[],totalLength:0,primaryLength:0};let globalS=0,chainId=0,primaryDone=false,prev=null;
 for(const r of ordered){
   if(!r||!Array.isArray(r.points)||r.points.length<2)continue;
   if(r.detached){if(!primaryDone){out.primaryLength=globalS;primaryDone=true}chainId++;}
   let len=0,pts=[];
   for(let i=0;i<r.points.length;i++){
     const p=r.points[i];if(i){const a=r.points[i-1];len+=Math.hypot(p[0]-a[0],p[1]-a[1])}
     pts.push({x:p[0],z:p[1],y:Number.isFinite(p[2])?p[2]:apexAlignedCircuitSurfaceY(p[0],p[1],{preferPit:false}),pitS:globalS+len});
   }
   const width=Math.max(3.8,r.width||defaultWidth||5.9);out.runs.push({id:r.id,width,length:len,startS:globalS,points:pts,chainId,detached:!!r.detached,joinGap:+r.joinGap||0});globalS+=len;prev=r;
 }
 out.totalLength=globalS;if(!primaryDone)out.primaryLength=globalS;out.primaryChainId=0;return out;
}
// v5.230.62.8.5.1.5 · Perfil funcional PIT desde JSON base/override.
// El SVG sigue mandando sobre X/Z. El JSON puede autorar altimetría, anchura y boxes por pitS.
function apexPitLaneJsonProfile(dir=null){
 const id=String(dir||(trackData&&trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir)||''),j=readStoredCircuitJson(id)||{},p=j.pitLane;
 return p&&p.schema==='APEX_PIT_LANE_V1'?p:null;
}
function apexPitProfileSample(samples,s,col=1,fallback=null){
 if(!Array.isArray(samples)||!samples.length)return fallback;
 const ss=+s||0;
 if(ss<=+samples[0][0]){const v=+samples[0][col];return Number.isFinite(v)?v:fallback}
 const last=samples[samples.length-1];if(ss>=+last[0]){const v=+last[col];return Number.isFinite(v)?v:fallback}
 let lo=0,hi=samples.length-1;
 while(lo+1<hi){const m=(lo+hi)>>1;if(+samples[m][0]<=ss)lo=m;else hi=m}
 const a=samples[lo],b=samples[hi],den=(+b[0]-+a[0])||1,t=clamp((ss-(+a[0]))/den,0,1),va=+a[col],vb=+b[col];
 if(Number.isFinite(va)&&Number.isFinite(vb))return lerp(va,vb,t);
 return Number.isFinite(va)?va:(Number.isFinite(vb)?vb:fallback);
}
function apexApplyPitLaneJsonProfile(){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.customWorld)return false;
 const cfg=apexPitLaneJsonProfile(),pn=trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork;
 if(!cfg||!pn||!Array.isArray(pn.runs)||!pn.runs.length)return false;
 const cfgLen=Math.max(1,+cfg.totalLengthM||+pn.primaryLength||+pn.totalLength||1),runtimeLen=Math.max(1,+pn.primaryLength||+pn.totalLength||cfgLen),toCfgS=s=>clamp((+s||0)*cfgLen/runtimeLen,0,cfgLen),toRuntimeS=s=>clamp((+s||0)*runtimeLen/cfgLen,0,runtimeLen);
 const vs=cfg.verticalProfile&&Array.isArray(cfg.verticalProfile.samples)?cfg.verticalProfile.samples:null,ws=cfg.widthProfile&&Array.isArray(cfg.widthProfile.samples)?cfg.widthProfile.samples:null,defaultW=Math.max(3.8,+((cfg.widthProfile||{}).defaultTotalWidthM)||+((raceMetaFor(trackData.spec)||{}).pit||{}).width||5.9);
 for(const r of pn.runs){
   const pts=Array.isArray(r.points)?r.points:[];let sumW=0,countW=0;
   for(const p of pts){
     const ps=toCfgS(p.pitS);
     const elev=apexPitProfileSample(vs,ps,1,null);if(Number.isFinite(elev))p.y=elev;
     const lw=apexPitProfileSample(ws,ps,1,null),rw=apexPitProfileSample(ws,ps,2,null),tw=apexPitProfileSample(ws,ps,3,null),w=Number.isFinite(tw)?tw:(Number.isFinite(lw)&&Number.isFinite(rw)?lw+rw:defaultW);
     p.leftWidth=Number.isFinite(lw)?lw:w*.5;p.rightWidth=Number.isFinite(rw)?rw:w*.5;p.width=Math.max(3.8,w);sumW+=p.width;countW++;
   }
   if(countW)r.width=sumW/countW;
 }
 pn._segments=null;
 if(cfg.speedLimit&&Number.isFinite(+cfg.speedLimit.kph))pn.speedKph=clamp(+cfg.speedLimit.kph,30,120);
 pn.profileSource='JSON:pitLane';pn.profileSchema=cfg.schema;pn.profileTotalLengthM=cfgLen;
 pn.boxZone=cfg.boxZone?apexCloneJson(cfg.boxZone):null;
 pn.boxSlots=Array.isArray(cfg.boxSlots)?cfg.boxSlots.map(slot=>{
   const runtimePitS=toRuntimeS(slot.pitS),q=apexPitPointAtS(runtimePitS);
   return {...apexCloneJson(slot),runtimePitS,centerX:q?q.x:null,centerZ:q?q.z:null,centerY:q?q.y:(Number.isFinite(+slot.elevationM)?+slot.elevationM:null)};
 }):[];
 trackData.pitLaneProfile=cfg;return true;
}
function apexMigratePitNetworkV2(pn){
 if(!pn||!Array.isArray(pn.runs)||!pn.runs.length)return pn;
 if(pn.schema==='APEX_PIT_NETWORK_V2'&&Number.isFinite(+pn.primaryLength))return pn;
 const raw=pn.runs.map((r,i)=>({id:Number.isFinite(+r.id)?+r.id:i,width:+r.width||5.9,sourceRole:'pitlane-centerline',points:(r.points||[]).map(q=>[+q.x,+q.z,Number.isFinite(+q.y)?+q.y:undefined])})).filter(r=>r.points.length>=2);
 if(!raw.length)return pn;
 const migrated=apexBuildPitNetwork(raw,raw[0].width||5.9);
 for(const k of ['speedKph','generated','source','meta'])if(pn[k]!==undefined)migrated[k]=pn[k];
 migrated.migratedFrom=pn.schema||'APEX_PIT_NETWORK_V1';
 return migrated;
}
function apexSmoothStep01(t){t=clamp(t,0,1);return t*t*(3-2*t)}
function apexBuildProvisionalPitNetwork(){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.spec)return null;const pit=pitLaneSpec();if(!pit)return null;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,entry=Number(pit.entry),exit0=Number(pit.exit),side=Number(pit.side)>=0?1:-1,width=Math.max(4.2,Number(pit.width)||5.4);
 if(!Number.isFinite(entry)||!Number.isFinite(exit0)||!Number.isFinite(L)||L<=100)return null;
 let exit=exit0;while(exit<=entry)exit+=L;const span=exit-entry;if(span<120||span>L*.48)return null;
 const merge=Math.min(115,Math.max(58,span*.19)),step=4,pts=[];
 const add=u=>{
   const s=mod(u,L),q=poseAt(s),env=envProfile(s),fromEntry=apexSmoothStep01((u-entry)/merge),toExit=apexSmoothStep01((exit-u)/merge),blend=Math.min(fromEntry,toExit);
   // En los extremos el eje nace dentro del carril exterior de la pista para que TRACK↔PIT
   // sea físicamente atravesable. En la zona central se separa fuera de la pista.
   const join=Math.max(2.2,(Number(env.half)||6.2)*.58),outer=Math.max(Number(pit.offset)||12,join+(Number(env.curb)||.6)+(Number(env.runoff)||1.2)+width*.72),lat=side*lerp(join,outer,blend);
   pts.push([q.x+q.nx*lat,q.z+q.nz*lat]);
 };
 for(let u=entry;u<exit;u+=step)add(u);add(exit);
 const pn=apexBuildPitNetwork([{id:0,width,sourceRole:'pitlane-centerline',points:pts}],width);
 pn.generated=true;pn.source='PROCEDURAL_FALLBACK';pn.speedKph=apexPitSpeedLimitKph();pn.meta={entry,exit:exit0,side,offset:Number(pit.offset)||12,width,speedKph:pn.speedKph};
 return pn;
}
function apexEnsurePitFallback(){
 if(!trackData)return;const cw=trackData.customWorld,pn=cw&&cw.pitNetwork;
 // SVG / importado siempre gana. También respetamos muestras antiguas mientras se migran.
 if(pn&&Array.isArray(pn.runs)&&pn.runs.length)return;
 if(cw&&Array.isArray(cw.pitLaneSamples)&&cw.pitLaneSamples.length>3)return;
 const generated=apexBuildProvisionalPitNetwork();if(!generated)return;
 const base=cw?{...cw}:{profileRevision:0,authoritative:false,landmarks:[],brakeBoards:[],pitLaneSamples:[],pitNetwork:null,wallLeftSamples:[],wallRightSamples:[],wallSegments:[],edgeLeftSamples:[],edgeRightSamples:[],structureZones:[],barrierSegments:[],surfaceZones:[]};
 base.pitNetwork=generated;base.runtimePitFallback=true;trackData.customWorld=base;
}

function apexBuildExactWallSegments(runs,side){
 const out=[];
 for(const r of (runs||[])){
   const p=r.points||[];
   for(let i=0;i<p.length-1;i++){
     const a=p[i],b=p[i+1],dx=b[0]-a[0],dz=b[1]-a[1],len=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(len<.05)continue;
     const mx=(a[0]+b[0])*.5,mz=(a[1]+b[1])*.5,st=nearestTrackState(mx,mz,null,{radius:120,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:100});
     out.push({family:'WALL',s:st.s,side,runId:r.id,x1:a[0],z1:a[1],y1:apexPhase1WallFootY(a[0],a[1]),x2:b[0],z2:b[1],y2:apexPhase1WallFootY(b[0],b[1]),width:.32,height:r.height||0,kind:'W
